Measured in the harness on H's panel with the drawn lattice and measured strips: **19 of 19 cards on their own label**, four calls, where v381 gave every card the whole panel. Suite `verify-panel` (the wash case: the drawn lattice first, then three strips whose boxes are the panel's own — the two log lines, four calls, 19 cards, every one on its own label; the mock answers a queue since); the aiframe, picture, badpic, autocard, english, kind and units suites pass. **The pitch is not the tell, the movement is (v383, H's washing machine at v382 — the same twenty cards with the whole panel as every picture, and the log carrying no "one strip per row" line at all):** v382 had added a second condition to `templateBoxes` — a lattice repeats the same gap — and it kept H's own drawn grid from ever reaching the strips: of his twenty labels 下筒 sits inside the dial, where it breaks its row's rhythm, so that row failed the per-row pitch test and only 13 of the 20 labels counted as evenly spaced, under the four fifths the rule wanted. The premise was wrong: a panel's buttons *within one block* are as evenly spaced as a lattice is, so the pitch cannot tell a drawing from a measurement in either direction. `templateBoxes` is the width rule of v381 again — four fifths of the boxes of one width while the labels' character counts differ —, and **the question v382 wanted to ask of the strips' answer is asked of the boxes themselves: did they move?** `stripBoxes` records each box's centre before it overwrites it and hands back the median shift and the median box width; a shift under `STRIP_MOVE` a quarter of a box means the model drew the same lattice a second time and nothing was measured. So a near-equal-width truth is no longer thrown away for its shape — the strips confirm it and it is used — and a redrawn grid is caught by what it did rather than by how it looks. Measured in the harness on H's panel with the drawn lattice and measured strips: 19 of 19 cards on their own label, four calls, the log's "141 % of a box away from the grid"; the panel suite's other cases and the aiframe, picture, badpic, autocard, english, kind and units suites pass. **The strips are gone, and the model cannot localise on this panel (v385, H's washing machine at v384 — the first run whose Diagnostics carried Qwen's raw answer):** the strip re-ask fired exactly as designed — the drawing test caught the lattice, three strips were asked, the movement test passed at 58 % of a box — and the cards were wrong all the same. **What the raw boxes say, measured against the panel's own label positions:** in the first strip 羽绒, 大件 and 羊毛 land within 1.3 points of the truth and overlap it by 80–95 %, while 混合, 快速, 单脱水 and 煮洗 sit **12 to 16 points to the right** of theirs with **no overlap at all**; the second strip is 11 to 28 points off on every label, the third 10 to 14 on the left block and 5 on the right. The whole-picture answer of v383 has the same shape — the right-hand block within 4 points, the left-hand block 19 to 20 points off. So the model reads this panel perfectly and **places its bright right-hand block correctly and guesses its dim left-hand block, every call, at every scale** (v382 had measured that dimness from the pixels; it turns out to be what the model trips on too). A second question gets a second drawing. Worse, the strips' own y drifted up and squashed the answer's three well-separated rows (37–43, 44–50, 51–57 % in v383) into two that overlap, so `labelPlan` found nothing to line up ("the labels' rows do not line up with the picture's") and every label fell back to placing itself — where v383 had at least placed 10 of 20 by row. Three Qwen calls, 132 seconds of waiting, and a worse answer than the one call before them. `stripBoxes` and the `STRIP_*` constants are gone; `templateBoxes` keeps the v381 width rule, and a drawn grid means every card of that photo keeps its text and gets the frame's own picture, as in v380. What that leaves unsolved: H rejected that picture ("Die Bild crops sind noch falsch"), and nothing in the model's coordinates can be trusted on a panel like this — the next mechanism to try is the pixels with the reader (find the picture's own runs, read each one, match it to the text the model named), which needs H's original photo file in the harness first (built as v386, below). Suite `verify-panel` (the wash case back to one call: the drawing's two log lines, no "one strip per row", 19 cards, every card with a picture, the Latin line still dropped); the aiframe, picture, autocard and english suites pass. **Field-measured, not a harness guess — H's own answer from the AI log of v384.** **The reader finds the labels, the model only names them (v386, H sent the washing-machine photo itself with "Go" on the three-line offer "the reader instead of the model's boxes"):** with the real photo in the harness at last, the two halves of the question were measured before anything was built. **(1) Can the reader read a label of this panel?** The nineteen labels were cut from the photo at its own 1600 px — 混合 is 74 × 40 pixels there, its characters 30 px tall — scaled to a 45, 64 and 90 px character height and read by the on-device reader in colour, black-and-white and chromaticity copies: 10 of 19 come back exactly right in the best single mode, 14 within one character, and matched against the model's own nineteen texts **16 of 19 pick their own label and none picks another's** (the chromaticity copies are noise on this panel and are not used). **(2) Can the app find the labels' runs without the model's coordinates?** Not from the anchors — the drifted grid puts a label's anchor a cell and more away, past `LB_SIDE`, so a scan around it never reaches its characters. But a **lattice of probe boxes over the panel**, each scanned by `labelScan`'s local cut, nominates the picture's own rows including the dim ones; the rows are then cut across the whole width into runs, in eight tiles with each tile's own Otsu, and every label of the panel has a run of its own. So: `labelRunsOf(gy,labels,uni)` builds the candidate runs (the lattice at `RL_STEP` 0.6 of the median label box over the model's box with a box of room around it; rows merged at 0.7 overlap and kept when `RL_SUP` 3 probes agree; runs cut at gaps of `RL_GAP` 0.55 row heights, width `RL_WMIN` 0.8 to `RL_WMAX` 12 row heights; deduplicated at `RL_DUP` 0.6 IoU, at most `RL_CAP` 420), `readLabels` cuts each run from the photo with 0.3 row heights of room, scales it to `RL_PX` 45 and reads it through `runPasses` — the pool, so the phone's cores share the work —, and each run is paired with the label whose text it reads: the character counts within one and the longest common subsequence over `RL_HIT` 0.6 of the longer (`labelHit`), the surest pair first, each run and each label used once. **A label nothing matched keeps the frame's own picture** — the v380 answer —, so a card can be short of its own crop but **never carries a neighbour's**. It runs only where `templateBoxes` says the boxes are a drawing; a measured answer (the rice cooker, the Meituan screen, every answer that has worked) is placed by v359–v376 exactly as before, and costs nothing new. Measured through the whole app on H's own photo with a drawn lattice like the one his phone got: **19 cards, 15 of them on their own label, 4 with the panel, none on a neighbour's**, one AI call, 17 s from the photo landing to the cards (304 runs read on one worker in the harness; the phone's pool of four shares them). The four that keep the panel — 血渍洗, 内裤, 柔顺剂, +烘干 — are the dimmest and the underlined ones, which the reader cannot name even from a perfect crop. What it costs: a few hundred small reader passes on a panel whose boxes are a drawing, a second or three on the phone, and nothing on any other photo. Suite `verify-panel` (the wash case: the drawing's line naming the reader, the rows-and-runs line, 19 cards each with a picture, at least ten with a crop of their own, none holding under half of its own label's width, one call, no "one strip per row", the Latin line still dropped); the aiframe, picture, autocard, english, kind, units, badpic and savenow suites pass. **Field-checked 2026-09-09** (H, the washing machine taken again at v386: "Much better. A few are now correct, but many are still not yet correct." — the phone named **8 of the 20 labels**, none of them wrong, and its log carried the numbers to say why: "15 rows, 74 runs" where the harness had found 304). **What the field run taught, and v387:** the harness was rebuilt around H's own answer from the v386 AI log — 20 boxes of exactly 50 × 40 on the 800 × 715 picture, pitch 70, the union box a band 35–60 % down — and it **reproduced the phone: 7 named of 20, 16 rows, 90 runs, the same labels found and the same missed**. With that in hand the fault was one line. **The run search was never the problem: all 19 labels had a run of their own** (measured, IoU 0.37–0.73 against the panel's true label positions). The reader's crop was. `readLabels` scaled the cut so that the **padded** crop was `RL_PX` tall, and a 41 px label inside a 65 px padded box came out at 45 px — *smaller than it started*, 20 px a character, unreadable. The scale is the **run's own height** since (`RL_PX` 64, never under 1×), and two more things came with the measurement: the run is the ink's own extent, so the column cut clips the first and last character a little — the tight margin reads a label whose icon stands close above it (快速 reads 快速 at 0.3 and 一局快速 at 0.5), the loose one reads a label the cut trimmed (云程序 reads 元程序 at 0.3 and 云程序 at 0.5) —, so every run is read at **both** margins (`RL_ROOM` 0.3 and 0.5) and in **both** the colour and the black-and-white copy, an unevenly lit panel needing each for one of its halves; the best of the four readings scores against each label. Measured on H's own answer over his own photo: **13 of the 20 named, none wrong**, where the phone had 8 — 20 s from the photo landing, four readings of each of 90 runs through the pool. The seven that keep the panel: 智洗烘长按童锁 (the model itself merged the label with its long-press sub-line), 下筒 (inside the dial, no run of its own), 袜子, 洗衣液, 柔顺剂, +烘干 and 时间 — the dimmest and the underlined, which the reader misreads even from a perfect crop (measured at v386: 16 of 19 was the ceiling with ideal boxes). **What the next step is, when H asks:** the model's reading order and row topology are reliable (v376's finding), so a label with no confident reading could take the run that keeps its row's left-to-right order between two labels that were named — 袜子 is the first of its row, 洗衣液 柔顺剂 +烘干 stand left of the named 时间 漂洗 温度. Not built. Suite `verify-panel` unchanged in its shape (the wash case's log lines and counts hold); the aiframe, picture, autocard, english, kind and units suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's washing machine taken again is the check: more than half the buttons with their own picture, and still none with a neighbour's. **Field-checked 2026-09-09 at v387** (H, 14 of 20 named on the phone and none wrong, with screenshots of the six that kept the panel — 智洗烘长按穿衣, 袜子, 洗衣液, 柔顺剂, +烘干, 下筒: "Still a few missing or unprecise crops"). **Half a reading and the row's order name two more, and the crop gets the room the cut lost (v388):** two things, both measured on H's own answer over his own photo. **(1) The crops were tight.** The run is the ink's own extent, so the column cut clips the first and last stroke and the band is thinner than the characters — 云程序's run lost 19 px on the left and 17 on the right of a 101 px label, and the 0.3 text heights of `FRAME_ROOM` added afterwards gave back only 7. `growRun` gives every chosen run `RL_GROW` a fifth of its width sideways and nearly half its height up and down, but **never more than half the way to its neighbouring run** — so two cards of one row can meet but never overlap — and `FRAME_ROOM` is not added on top of it any more. Measured against the panel's true label positions: every card now holds its whole label with a little room (margins 0.2–0.9 % of the photo), where 云程序, 筒自洁 and 单脱水 were clipped by up to 0.8 %. **(2) A label only half read takes the run its row's order gives it.** The model's reading order and row topology are reliable (v376's finding) while its coordinates are not: 袜子 reads as 计子 / 读子 / 福子 and 下筒 as 下简 — half the characters, under the `RL_HIT` bar of 0.6 — and each has exactly one run it can be. After the greedy pairing, a pair scoring at least `RL_WEAK` 0.5 is taken when the run stands in the label's own row (the band of that row's named runs), when its width per character is `RL_WIDE` 0.6 to 1.8 of the row's own (a run far wider holds more than this label — H's panel has a 138 px run that would otherwise have taken 下筒), and when it keeps the row's left-to-right order against every label already placed. Diagnostics: "the picture's own characters stand in 16 rows, 90 runs; the reader named 13 of the 20 labels, and 2 more by half a reading and their row's order". Measured through the whole app on H's photo with his own answer: **15 of 20 cards on their own label, none on a neighbour's**, one AI call, 19 s. The five that keep the panel are 智洗烘长按穿衣 (the model merged the label with its long-press sub-line, so there is no one run for it), 洗衣液, 柔顺剂, +烘干 and 时间 — the dimmest labels, which read as 法流, 错副, 姑 and nothing at all from a perfect crop at every scale, margin and copy tried (measured). What it costs: nothing new — no extra reader pass, no extra call. Suite `verify-panel` (the wash case's `own` bar raised from 10 to 15 of 19 with `wrong` still 0); the aiframe, autocard, picture, badpic, english, kind, units and savenow suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's washing machine taken again is the check: 袜子 and 下筒 with their own buttons, and every crop holding its whole label. **Field-checked 2026-09-09 at v388** (H, 16 of 21 placed on the phone and none wrong — 下筒 found by the half reading, the model splitting 智洗烘 and 长按穿衣 into two labels this time; the five that kept the panel are 智洗烘, 袜子, 洗衣液, 柔顺剂 and +烘干, and one card's pinyin read "x yī yè"). **A label with no reading at all takes the run its row's order gives it, and every label's pinyin is checked (v389):** two things. **(1) The labels' own pinyin never went through the v187 check.** `aiReadPicture` ran `saneP` on the answer's own "p" and took each label's "p" as it came, so Qwen dropping the ǐ of xǐ put "x yī yè" on H's 洗衣液 card; every label's pinyin is checked against its own characters now, and a broken syllable is replaced by the app's own, label by label. **(2) The dimmest labels are placed by their row's order alone.** 袜子, 洗衣液, 柔顺剂 and +烘干 read as nothing from a perfect crop at every scale, margin and copy (measured at v388), but each stands in a row whose other labels the reader named. After the v388 half-reading pass, a run of unnamed labels between two placed ones takes the free runs between them — **only when the order is unambiguous**: exactly as many free runs as labels to place, each of the row's own height (`RL_HGT` 0.6 to 1.6 of the placed runs' median), each of the row's own width per character (`RL_WIDE` as in v388), and each standing in a column another placed label established (`RL_COL` 0.6 of the placed runs' median width — the panel's buttons line up in columns, while a stray run inside the dial or at the picture's edge does not, and that is what separates the four real labels from the seven other free runs in those rows). Anything short of that keeps the whole picture: a card showing the neighbour's button is worse than a card showing the panel. Diagnostics: "the reader named 13 of the 20 labels, and 2 more by half a reading and their row's order, and 4 more by their row's order alone", and each such label's own line says "no reading, its row's order gives it 22–28 % across, 50–55 % down". Measured through the whole app on H's photo with his own answer: **19 of 20 labels placed, 18 of them verified against the panel's true label positions and none on a neighbour's** (margins 0.0–0.9 % of the photo), one AI call. The one that keeps the panel is the label the model itself merged with its long-press sub-line — there is no single run for it. Suite `verify-panel` (the wash case's `own` bar raised to 16 of 19 with `wrong` still 0); the aiframe, autocard, picture, badpic, english, kind, units and savenow suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's washing machine taken again is the check: 袜子, 洗衣液, 柔顺剂 and +烘干 with their own buttons, and every card's pinyin whole. **Each label's frame comes from the pixels, not from the model's box (v359, H's rice cooker taken again at v358 — the four top cards showed the small black button instead of its label: "Cropped wrong areas"):** the photo was rebuilt exactly (1600×1429, the proposal 0–71 % across, the picture the AI saw 637×800, Qwen's real answer from the diagnostics) and the harness reproduced the phone's frames to the percent — and drawing the answer's own boxes on that picture said why: **Qwen's per-label boxes sit on the buttons**, one label height below the characters ([158,236,255,285] for 低卡饭, whose text stands at y 189–216), and `snapBox` then did its job on the ink it was pointed at. The union box drifted the same way and the v326 band check caught that one ("a line above the AI's box, read as 快毒粗粮饭"); the label boxes had nothing to catch theirs. So **a label's box is an anchor, not a measurement**: `labelRect(bmp,box,uni)` looks around it — `LB_UP` 1.6 box heights above and below, `LB_SIDE` 1.5 sideways — for the row of ink the label stands in. Ink rows give the bands (`LB_ROW` 2 % of the region's width), a band of the anchor's own order of height (`LB_MINH` 0.2 to `LB_MAXH` 1.25 of it) is a candidate, its columns are cut into runs at gaps of `LB_MERGE` half a band height — inside a label the characters nearly touch, between two labels there is a character's width of air — and each run is scored: **twice its overlap with the anchor, less how far its centre sits from the anchor's, less how much its width differs from the anchor's** — the model's box may sit on the button but its *width* is the label's, and the button is a third of that — with the nearest row counting only as a tiebreaker (`LB_NEAR` 0.6, since the boxes drift by a whole label height). A run that fills `LB_FILL` 0.8 of its box is solid — a button, a bar, a plate — and is not characters (measured: 低卡饭 fills 0.45 of its box, the button under it 0.89). A second row joins when it sits within `LB_GAP` of the first and stands over it (保温 above 取消). **The cut** comes from the label's own surroundings when they hold ink (`LB_INK0` 0.5 % to `LB_INK1` 35 % of the region, the minority class) — a panel is lit unevenly and one cut over the whole answer's box lost the characters at its dim end (粗粮饭 came out as 粗粮) —, else from the whole answer's box, which certainly holds ink: over surroundings that are bare panel Otsu splits the panel's own gradient in half and the characters drown in it (预约, whose anchor stands on bare panel). Nothing of the kind near the anchor: the AI's box stays as it is, and Diagnostics says so per label ("低卡饭: the AI's box 25–40 % across, 30–36 % down, its characters at 27–41 %, 24–27 %"). `snapBox` is not used in the split path at all — its room reaches into the neighbours and its poster passes take the button. Measured on the rebuilt photo: all seven labels whole in their own card picture, where v358 gave four buttons, a half label and a miss. **A panel with two dozen buttons (v360, H's washing machine at v359 — 24 labels and not one card: "Die Waschmaschine hat allerdings nicht funktioniert"; Diagnostics `[picture] could not read the model's answer` and a reply that stops inside its `boxes` array):** the picture call asked for `max_tokens` **1000** (v173's number, for one sign's short JSON), and Qwen's answer for 26 elements — every one with its own pinyin, meaning and rectangle — ran past it and was cut off mid-JSON, so `JSON.parse` threw, the reading fell back to the reader's garbage 引事组, DeepSeek called it garbage and the phone kept one flagged card. Four things now. **(1) The budget** is `PIC_TOKENS` 4000 (the text check's own), and the prompt asks the model to leave `"boxes"` out when it gives `"labels"`, whose entries carry their own rectangles — that halves the answer of a panel. **(2) The picture gets its own timeout** (`PIC_TIMEOUT_MS` 60 s beside `AI_TIMEOUT_MS` 25 s, `timedFetch`/`aiFetch`/`relayFetch` take it as an argument): the cut-off answer already took 22 s, so a whole one would have been aborted by the v331 rule and thrown away twice. **(3) An answer that stops in the middle keeps what came** (`mendJSON`): the text is cut back to the last comma or bracket, every open bracket is closed and the fields that arrived whole are used — the card then has the text even when the boxes or the labels never did, and Diagnostics says "the AI's answer stopped in the middle — kept what came (zh, p, m, note, box)". **(4) A label whose frame cannot be cut no longer refuses the split**: the placeable ones keep their cards and the log names the rest ("no frame for 长按童锁, 长按单烘 … — the other labels keep their cards"), `SPLIT_PX` is 8 copy pixels (16 until v359) — a panel's fine print stands 6 px tall in the 800 px picture. And **the labels are measured on the frame at its own pixels** (`labelGrey(bmp,uni)` prepares the grey, the threshold and the ink's polarity once for the whole panel, up to `LB_CAP` 1600, and `labelRect(gy,box)` works in fractions on it): until v359 every label rebuilt the whole picture as a canvas — 24 of them — at the 800 px the AI saw, where a washing machine's label is 16 pixels tall. Measured on H's photo rebuilt with its 24 labels (the texts and meanings from Qwen's own answer, the boxes drifted onto the icons as the model drifts): **20 cards, each with its own text, pinyin and meaning**, where v359 made none; the four lines of fine print are left out for want of a frame. **A label's card picture is its own cut at the photo's pixels (v362, H: "Now do the label crops at full resolution"):** until v361 `splitCards` took the 16:9 window of v329 (`windowCut`), which is right for a sign that fills its photo and wrong for a button — a label of about 80 × 36 photo pixels widened to 16:9 carries its neighbours into the picture, and on H's washing machine nearly every card showed two or three buttons. Now the picture is `cropBlob(id, the label's own frame)` — the frame the label was placed on, cut from the photo at its own pixels, no widening; the front's 16:9 box shows it with the blurred fill of v232 beside it, as a tall crop has always been shown. Nothing else changes: the card's `frame` is the same, Crop again starts where it did, a photo that makes one card keeps the 16:9 window. Measured on H's washing machine rebuilt with its 24 labels: **fifteen of the twenty cards show their own label and nothing else** (混合 快速 单脱水 煮洗 羽绒 大件 羊毛 智洗烘 袜子 云程序 内裤 时间 漂洗 温度 +烘干), where every card carried its neighbours before; the rice cooker's seven cards are tight on their labels too. **What is still wrong there:** 洗衣液 and 柔顺剂 take the row above them (云程序 stands right over 洗衣液, and the two-line rule of v359 cannot tell that from 保温 above 取消), 筒自洁 and 血渍洗 take a fragment of the row above, and 下筒 inside the dial finds no characters and keeps the AI's box. Tried and dropped on the way, both unmerged: cutting the chosen band back to the label's own rows inside the run's columns (the anchor sits on the icon, so the nearest block is the icon's, and 快速, 单脱水, 煮洗 and 智洗烘 lost their characters), and refusing the two-line join when both rows do not fit the anchor's own height (the take is the icon's row on this panel and the label joins as its "second line" — with the guard four good cards became the icon alone). What that says: **on this panel the run the app takes is the icon's, and the label rides along** — the width match cannot separate an outline icon from two characters of the same width. The next step, when H asks, is a test for what is text and what is a drawing. Measured in the harness on H's own photos with the model's answers mocked: the rice cooker 11 cards with every frame on its own label, the washing machine 23, the dishwasher's lower panel 2, the oven's two columns 6, **three texts one under the other 3** — a panel's column, a wall of separate signs, the case v357's geometry refused; an answer without labels, one whose boxes are not all on the same scale, one whose box covers the whole picture and `apart:false` each make exactly one card. Suite `verify-panel` (all of those, **the phone's own case — the rice cooker photo with Qwen's real answer, the reader stood down as it was on the phone: seven cards, each frame covering its own label** —, the row's head and buttons, Edit opening that label's card from the camera, Delete with Undo, the German head line); the aiframe (32 cases), autocard, picture, badpic, savenow, english, guide, cards, detail, recrop, recropai, edit, ask, pick and twopass suites pass; checked by screenshot in light, dark and German. What it costs: no extra AI call, about a second more before the row fills, and a panel's worth of cards in the deck at once (Learn still gives eight new a session). Not yet field-checked — H's rice cooker taken again is the check: a card per button, each showing its own label. (`verify-autoframe`'s `read` case fails on v356 too — a pre-existing flake of the reader on H's poster, not this change.) The lead line reads "Photos stay on this phone. Take one — the card is made for you." and the guide's first section describes the new flow (Crop and Save now in its second paragraph), both in six languages. **The harness:** `S.autoCard` (from `window.AUTO_CARD`, which the seed sets to false) keeps the crop-mode flow for the frame suites — the hidden proposal, the shown frame, the preview; the autocard suite switches it on after boot. Measured in the harness on the synthetic poster: the finished card at 4.3 s with the frame 8–91 % across, 66–84 % down (the quick look's), one AI call, unflagged. Suite `verify-autocard` (the shimmer, the bar and Cancel with no layer and no Save now; the result with the front, the back and the buttons, the card verified with the placed frame and its cut to the pixel, the photo tap both ways; Edit opening the form with the frame at the card's frame and no reading, Back to the result; Delete with Undo and the photo kept; Cancel dropping the placeholder with nothing left running; a blank image making no card, with the note; 邪不压正 with a bad AI verdict flagged with the note; a reload while reading resuming to the result; the German lead and buttons); checked by screenshot in light, dark and German; the autoframe, aiframe, savenow, picture, recrop, english and guide suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the next photo on the Xiaomi is the check: no frame, the light over the photo, the finished card with Edit and Delete under it.
2. **Frame (Crop, Crop again, and the harness's crop-mode flow):** the photo lands directly in crop mode. **A photo that opens by itself gets its frame drawn by the app** (v203, H's poster photo that needed no framing: "do an automatic image analysis and suggest a crop, which the user can change if needed — or apply no crop at all", described first and built on "Go"): while the reading box says "Finding the text …" with the bar, `textRegion` takes the ink of a 360 px chromaticity copy, the rows that look like text (the inkHeight rule with four stroke edges), the first and last of them, the columns holding ink inside that band, padded by one text height — and proposes **the whole photo** when nothing stands out or the text fills it (H's "no crop at all"); **the proposed frame has a fixed shape whenever the photo allows it** (v207, H: "propose a certain aspect ratio that fits for most images — I want uniformity across image previews", described first with 16:9 recommended, built on "Go"): `shapeBox` widens the padded text box, never narrows it, to `FRAME_RATIO` 16:9 — the shape of the Cards list's 124×70 thumbnails —, centred on the text and shifted to stay inside the photo; a text the photo cannot hold at that shape (a tall sign, a text that fills the photo) keeps its own padded box or the whole photo, so those previews stay irregular; the card image then carries more surroundings, the reader's second look tightens as before, frames drawn by hand are untouched; `proposeFrame` sets `CROP.rect` from the layer's size (`CROP.proposed` = "whole" / "16:9" / "text", a line in Diagnostics' reading steps that says which), draws the frame and hands over to `showCropPreview`, so the usual wait and the reading follow without a finger; a finger, Cancel or another photo during the ~100 ms drops the proposal. The proposed frame is adjustable like a drawn one (corners, moving, the handle). It comes only with a photo that opens by itself — taken, the first from the album, shared (`CROP.auto` set in `importPhotos`); **the Crop button never proposes**, so Cancel, then Crop, gives the empty layer to draw on as before. **How the frame is found today (v287–v312, consolidated on H's "Zieh CLAUDE.md nach" at v312):** the ink-row proposal is computed as before but **never drawn in the inbox** (`CROP.hidden`; `proposeFrame` logs "frame proposed by the app (not shown): …") — drawings and Latin letters extend it, and H wants the frame shown once, on the text (v287–v288, his poster 手作冰淇淋 under a wide proposed frame: "Why don't you automatically crop the chinese text?", then on a frame that moved after the reading: "Only show the frame after identifying the right area, don't make an existing frame jump. Notify the user that the frame is being analyzed while searching for the text. And of course, the analysis must be super fast"). The photo opens with the bar "Finding the text …", no frame, no handles, no Save now, no preview, no 1.2 s wait — `cropSign` starts at once from `proposeFrame`. Then, in this order: **(1) The quick look** (v290, H: "Why does it take so long? you don't need to translate first, you just need to identify text first"): while the frame is hidden, one pass on a copy of at most `FIRST_MAX` 1 000 px (status "looking for the text …"; 0.3 s on H's poster in the sandbox against 1.1 s for the whole 1 600 px frame); its confident boxes (`frameBoxes`: `SURE_BOX` 70, a lone character beside a line of three or more left out — the score's fragment rule, the ice-cream cone read as 槛 / 及 / 环 at 88 % by every colour pass, while 喜欢 and 爸爸 with two characters stay) give the lines, `textRowExtent` their ends from the image, and `frameOnText` places the frame there with `FRAME_ROOM` 0.3 text heights around the text and **no 16:9 widening** (v293, H on the ALEXA 35 card whose 16:9 frame held a logo and a field of fabric with 再创标杆 small in the middle: "Make the automatic crop frame tighter. Chinese Text should be well readable in the thumbnail list" — the Cards list's box is 16:9 itself with the blurred fill behind, and a one-line text widened to 16:9 came out at half the thumbnail's width; the ink-row proposal keeps its 16:9 of v207 as the fallback). **Garbage places nothing** (`textLike(lines)`, v296 — H's 邪不压正 poster: the faces read as 品语失色全了 at 86 % were framed tightly and sent to Qwen, "Oh no! jetzt macht er gesichtserkennung!?!??": half the characters in dictionary words of two or more, or the confident characters read at 95 % on average — **since v319 at least three characters read with confidence, at 95 % on average, and no dictionary path; since v320 fine print under 0.4 of the frame's ink height places nothing; since v321 the reading's winning whole-frame pass places the frame when nothing else did**, see below; Diagnostics "quick look: … — not text, no frame from it"; the price: a lone character the reader misreads at the quick look, the red test's 有 read as 角 at 85 %, keeps the ink-row proposal); a band that is the frame itself shows the proposal at once ("frame shown as proposed — the text fills it"). The quick look is not one of the reading's passes (tried as the first pass it lost 爸爸 on H's poster), so the reading — the native first pass, the close look, the fallbacks — runs unchanged after it. `textLike` is a function declaration since v305, so the harness can stand it down as it does `effScore` (the overrides are restored, never deleted — a declaration's global binding cannot be deleted). **(2) The close look's placement** as the fallback for a quick look without a usable box: `secondLook` calls `r.onTight(textArea)` after its colour passes, and `place` in `cropSign` runs again after the whole close look — `textBandOf(tightPasses,cardRect)`: per pass the extent of its confident boxes, the quartiles over the passes (a stray one-pass read is out, a line most passes saw is in, a lone pass counts as it is), half a text height of room at the ends since the boxes' ends drift (v69), never outside the second look's band, turned back into the frame as framed (`unrotatedBox`, shared with `cutUnrotated`); only the simplified reader's tight passes as read, and only those that pass `textLike`. **(3) The 2 s fallback** (`FRAME_WAIT`, v289, H: "Show the ink-row frame after 2 seconds if the reader is slower"): a timer in `proposeFrame` drops `CROP.hidden` and draws the proposal as the frame (Diagnostics "frame shown as proposed — the reader took longer than 2 s"; the card image then takes the second look's band, `cutUnrotated`, when nothing is placed). **The shown proposal may still move once** (v310, H's 风流一代 poster: the reading proper on the whole photo stood at "recognizing … 46 %" at the 2 s mark, the close look then read the title at 97 % on four passes, and the frame stayed the whole photo with the first pass's garbage band as the card's picture — "Warum wurde hier nichts cropped?"; two ways offered, H: "Do A"): `place` and `frameOnText`'s guard allow the reader's placement while `CROP.proposed` stands and nothing was placed yet (`!CROP.followed`) — the one jump H rejected in v288, only in the slow case, only from the app's proposal, never after a finger touched it. Option B, the frame staying while only the card's picture is cut on the text, was not built. **(4) The AI's box** when the reading is weak (`WEAK_READ` by `effScore`) or the text check calls it garbage (`picOnBad`, v302): `picSystem()` asks for `"box":[left,top,right,bottom]` — one rectangle around all the lines read, **in pixels of the picture, whose size the user message names** ("… this picture (800×544 pixels)"; v294 — v293 asked for fractions and Qwen's answer came a tenth off; whether pixels land better is not settled, the three real answers on 邪不压正 were each a tenth or two too far right) and, since v312, `"boxes"`, one rectangle per line of zh in the same order; `picBox` returns fractions (pixels of the sent picture and a 0–1000 grid are scaled, fractions accepted; a box under 2 % or malformed is none; `r.pic.box` in Diagnostics). While the frame is still the app's (`CROP.proposed` — the hidden proposal, the shown one, or a frame the quick look placed; `READ_APP[id]` for a card saved with Save now; never one the hand touched), the box is mapped onto the picture the AI saw (`picSeen`) and `frameOnText(…,"AI")` places the frame there, once ("frame placed on the text by the AI: …"). **The picture the AI gets** (`picBase`, `sg.picBlob` for the garbage-verdict path): **since v319 always the area the reading started from with its straightening** (`r.blob`, `dk`; Diagnostics "the AI gets the whole proposal, not the placed frame's cut" when a frame was placed), see the v319 paragraph below; **from v301 to v318** the placed frame's cut when the frame stood on text the reader could read (`CROP.followed` or `PLACED[id]`, upright already — v301, H's 绿皮书 poster: the quick look had placed the frame on the title block, the reading proper on the whole proposal was weak, the whole poster went to Qwen and the card came back with the Oscar nominations from the top edge: "warum wurden hier characters ausserhalb des crops mitgelesen?"; Diagnostics "the AI gets the placed frame's cut"), else the area the reading started from with its straightening (`r.blob`, `dk`) — never a placed cut from garbage (v296; v288–v295 had sent the placed cut). On the placed cut the box **centres the frame inside the placed one** (v303, H's 绿皮书 card: "Works now, but should be more centered. I like the fact that you cropped around the large main characters. Was that coincidence or not?" — no coincidence, the quick look read 绿友书一 at 97 %; the 一 was a reflection streak through the hook of 书, so `textRowExtent` walked along it to the photo's right edge; `refine` in `frameOnText`, `seenBase` = the placed frame: the box's rectangle in the upright cut is turned with the frame around the frame's centre and laid inside it, the frame moves once more, only inward; "frame centred on the text by the AI: …"). **The box is snapped to the characters** first (`snapBox`, below). **The main text only** (v312, H's 青春无烟 / 未来无限 poster: the small line 第39个世界无烟日 above the title and the date line below it, half of it outside the frame, on the card — Qwen had read them from the whole proposal before its box placed the frame: "Hier hat er wieder die Sachen ausserhalb des Crops und das Kleingedruckte mitgelesen. Bitte beides vermeiden"): the prompt asks for the main text and leaves fine print — lines whose characters are under a third the height of the largest (dates, credits, small notes, slogans in small type) — and any line the picture's edge cuts off to the model; `mainLines` in `aiReadPicture` is the safety net from the line boxes: a line under `FINE_PRINT` (a third) of the tallest line's height is dropped with its pinyin and meaning parts when they come one per line (" / "), the box becomes the union of the lines kept, so the frame no longer stretches to the small print; Diagnostics "fine print left out of the AI's answer: …", `r.pic.boxes`/`dropped`. What it costs: a poster's credits smaller than a third of its title leave the card (the v300 per-line meanings stay for lines of comparable size). **A box on the edge of the app's proposal reaches beyond it** (v313, H's 北京现代 badge on a car, 2026-09-07: the ink rows had cut the chrome characters in half at the proposal's top — 32–78 % down —, the quick look and the reading read garbage, Qwen read 北京现代 from the visible halves and boxed them at y 0–55 of the 496 px picture, the snap found nothing at that size, and the frame at 32–39 % down showed half characters: "Why is the crop so wrong here?"): when the picture the AI saw is the app's proposal with its straightening (`picSeen.dk`) and the box touches the picture's top, bottom, left or right edge within 2 %, the text may go on beyond it — `frameOnText` takes `grow`, room in the copy's pixels beyond that edge (1.5 box heights above or below, two box heights sideways), extends the frame past the proposal's edge into the photo and clips it to the photo (upright frames only); Diagnostics "the AI's box touches the picture's top edge — the frame reaches beyond it". Nothing for the hand's frame or the whole photo, where there is nothing beyond. Measured on the red 有 with a mocked box at the proposal's top edge: the frame from 26 % down where the proposal began at 36 %. What it cannot do: know how much of the text lies beyond — a line cut lower than half its height gets less than it needs, and the ink-row proposal that cut it is untouched (the badge's chrome reflects the sky, so its upper halves left no ink rows). Suite `verify-aiframe` (the red 有 with the box on the top edge: the frame above the proposal's top, the log line); the autoframe and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the badge taken again is the check: the whole characters in the card's picture. **A line the app's frame cut off is read again (v314, H's 大闸蟹 / 我选蟹状元 poster, 2026-09-07: the close look had placed the frame at 37–77 % down, through the first line; the AI got that cut, read 我选蟹状元 alone with the note "The top line is cut off by the image edge and omitted" — the v312 rule at work —, and the card had the second line only: "Warum nur die zweite zeile und nicht auch die erste???"):** `picSystem()` asks for `"cut"` — the edges of the picture that cut off a line of Chinese text the model left out because of that ("top", "bottom", "left", "right", several separated by commas, "" when none); `aiReadPicture` hands it back as `cut`. In `cropSign`, when the first picture answer is good and names a cut edge and the frame is still the app's (`CROP.proposed`, or `READ_APP[id]` for a Save-now card) and not turned, the standing frame (`CROP.rect`, or `PLACED[id]`; since v319 the frame the AI saw, `base` — the proposal —, whatever the reader placed meanwhile) grows past every named edge — by 1.2 × the tallest line's height until v317, to the photo's edge since v318, see below —; the new frame becomes the placed one (`CROP.followed`, `PLACED[id]`), its cut the picture (`placedCut`, `picBase` with no straightening) and **the AI reads once more** from it — Diagnostics "the AI says the picture's top edge cuts off a line — the frame reaches beyond it (8–91 % across, 58–85 % down) and the AI reads again"; the second answer then places, snaps and centres as any answer does (`r.pic.cut`). Once per reading; a second answer naming a cut edge changes nothing. What it costs: one more Qwen call in that case. Nothing for the hand's frame, a turned frame or a frame that is the whole photo. Measured on the synthetic poster with a mocked Qwen that first names the top edge: the placed frame 65–85 % down grows to 58–85 %, the second call gets a taller picture (252 → 342 px) and answers both lines, the frame is centred at 68–82 % with 喜欢 and 手作冰淇淋 on the card. Suite `verify-aiframe` (the cut line: two calls with the second picture taller, both lines, the grown frame's log line and the centred one); the picture, badpic and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the 大闸蟹 poster taken again is the check: both lines on the card. **A frame that holds no Chinese text at all is opened to the whole photo, and no card is made from garbage (v348, H's scooter badge 九号 Fz110, 2026-09-08 — the card read 量词口还 under a picture of the yellow Fz110 plate: "Und noch ein knaller: voll falsch!"):** the ink rows proposed 47–93 % across, 45–64 % down — the yellow plate beside the white characters, the strongest ink in the photo —, the quick look read 看户11吕 at 80 % and placed nothing, every pass came back garbage, and Qwen, given that cut, answered `bad` with "The image shows a label with Latin letters and numbers ('Fz 110') but no Chinese characters" — right about the picture it was shown; DeepSeek called the reading garbage too, and the card was made from it all the same. Two things now. **(1) The frame reaches the whole photo and the AI reads again:** when the picture answer is `bad` and the frame is the app's own (the proposal, or `READ_APP[id]` for a card saved with Save now) and not turned, the frame grows past every edge — the v314/v318 path with all four sides named — and the AI reads the whole photo once more, its box then placing, snapping and centring as any answer's does; Diagnostics "the AI found no Chinese text in the picture — the frame reaches the whole photo (0–100 % across, 0–100 % down) and the AI reads again". Once per reading, never for the hand's frame or a turned one, nothing when the frame is the photo already. **(2) A card the app makes by itself is not made when the AI found no Chinese text in the picture** (`sg.noText`, set wherever a picture answer comes back bad — the weak path and `picOnBad`): `finishPending` hands such a reading to `failPending`, the placeholder goes and the photo keeps its Crop with "Nothing could be read. Tap Crop to frame the text by hand." — the v325 rule for a photo with nothing readable. A text check that calls the reading garbage stays weaker evidence, since it never saw the picture, and still makes a flagged card as in v325 (H's 邪不压正 poster on a phone without a picture provider). What it costs: one more Qwen call on a photo whose framed area holds no Chinese; a photo that really has none now leaves no card where it left a flagged one. Measured in the harness on the badge rebuilt from H's screenshot (the proposal on the plate, the answer bad, then good on the whole photo): two calls, 563×334 then 800×784, the box on 九号 snapped 12–50 % across, 43–57 % down, the frame 8–54 % across, 39–61 % down; with both answers bad the row says so and no card is made. Suite `verify-aiframe` (`nine`), suite `verify-autocard` (`noText`, and the weak case — a garbage reading without a picture provider — still makes its flagged card); the picture, badpic, savenow and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the badge taken again is the check: 九号 on the card, or no card at all. What it cannot do: make the ink rows see the white characters beside the plate — the frame is opened only after the AI has said there is nothing in it.

**The snap** (`snapBox(bmp,box,n,lens)`, v297, H's 邪不压正 poster: Qwen's box `[230,190,740,510]` of 800×647 a tenth too far right — it cut 邪 between 牙 and 阝 — and a tenth too low into the faces under HIDDEN MAN: "Schrift ist immer noch nicht mittig im Rahmen und links leicht abgeschnitten. Insbesondere unten zu viel Luft."; a vision model's box is a rough place, the pixels know the characters): on a grey copy of at most 800 px, thresholded by Otsu over the box, the connected blobs of both colours are labelled within the box widened by one text height (`SNAP_ROOM`; the text height = the box's height over the answer's lines), each colour as it is and eroded by up to 3 px (`Hb/60`; the eroded pass frees 牙 from a reflection bridge to the poster's white border, the plain pass keeps the small 姜文电影 whose strokes the erosion takes away). A character is a blob at least `SNAP_MIN` 0.15 text heights tall and smaller than the box itself (`SNAP_MAX` 0.95 of its width and height — v298, H's 流浪地球 poster: "Hier hat das framing nicht richtig funktioniert" — the brush title's strokes join into one blob of 344×370 px, a cap at 1.3 text heights dropped it and the frame was made from the small pieces, cutting 流 and 地; a border ring around the box is bigger than the box and still drops). A **clean** blob — whole, not cut by the widened box's edge — counts by its whole extent when at least half of it lies inside the AI's box; **a fused blob counts by its part inside** (v299, 流浪地球 taken again, upright: "Still the same" — the title's blob had fused with something running to the picture's edge, fell out, and the dark gaps inside the title made the box) when that part is a character's size both ways and holds a text height's square of ink (`Hb²` — the wedge of 邪不压正's slanted border inside the box does not); a blob whose inside part covers 0.9 of the box both ways is the background or a ring and drops; a clean blob with no pixel inside stays for the sideways pass; the colour whose characters cover more area is the text (**no median or dominant colour tells the background here — the white title fills more of Qwen's box than the red does**, measured). Then four passes: **sideways** — a clean blob of the text's colour in a taken blob's line band within half a text height joins (sideways only: under a line the cheeks of the faces would qualify); **the column pass** (v303, 绿皮书: the reflection streak, 15–20 px thick on the copy, fused with 书 and ran to the picture's edge, so 书 was no clean blob and held too little ink for the fused rule — the snap ended after 皮) — a fused blob of the text's colour beside a taken blob is read column by column within the line's band (`fused`, `masks`, `SNAP_COL` 0.15): a column with at least 0.15 of the band's height in ink and nothing of the blob further than a quarter of the band above or below it is a stroke's (the white mat beside 邪不压正 runs the whole height — without that rule it joined), the streak's thin columns are not, and the run of stroke columns nearest the line joins by its own extent; **the width budget** (`trim`, v305, H's 业主直租 sign with a QR code beside the text: "Das ginge schon noch zentrierter" — the code's bottom finder square is a dark blob of a character's size in the second line's band, and the snap took it, 22–85 % across where the characters end at 58 %) — the taken blobs are grouped into line bands by vertical overlap, a band whose blobs span more than `SNAP_WIDE` 1.6 × its line's character count (`lens`, the answer's lines without spaces and punctuation; matched to the lines by order when the counts agree, else the longest line for every band — vertical credits split into many bands on a poster) × its tallest blob is cut at gaps of `SNAP_GAP` 0.8 text heights into runs, and the run with the most blob area keeps its neighbours only while the width stays within the budget (a word space is under one text height; the gap to the code was 119 px against a text height of 100); **the shadow pass** (v306, 邪不压正 photographed again with a lamp at its right: "Jetzt funktioniert das schon wieder nicht" — the poster's left half in shadow, and under the one Otsu cut over the box the dark 邪 on that darker ground was no blob at all, nothing to erode or join: the true cause of every 邪 cut since v300, not the mat, as v300's reading from a reconstruction without the shadow had guessed) — from each end of every line, anchored on the tallest taken blob within 5 % of the union's end, a strip of 1.5 text heights beyond the union (the blob's own rows, a tenth of a height of room) is cut by its own Otsu, and a blob of the text's colour of a character's kind — not touching the strip's outer edge, at least 0.3 text heights tall, 0.3–1.3 wide, filled under 0.85, at least 1.6 ink runs per row on average (the wall above the poster is one solid run per row and 0.93 filled) — within half a text height of the line's end and overlapping its band by half joins; up to three per side, then the width budget runs again. The snapped box is the union; nothing, or under a fifth of the AI's box, leaves the AI's box as it is (Diagnostics "the AI's box … snapped to the ink: …", `r.pic.snap`). Measured in the harness: 邪不压正 29–93 % × 29–79 % → 18–84 % × 30–73 % (邪 whole, 姜文电影 and HIDDEN MAN in, the faces out), the lamp photo 25–71 % × 22–57 % (邪 whole), 绿皮书 1–99 % × 17–72 % (书 whole, the streak beyond it out), 业主直租 21–58 % × 26–53 % (the code out), both 流浪地球 cases keep the credits, the title and the film line. **Tried and dropped, all unmerged:** light–dark crossings per row and column (the faces' rows had as many as the title's, the slanted border and the glass reflections kept every column lively); a walk along the line's ink beyond the blobs (took 邪不压正's slanted border and 流浪地球's robot arm for strokes, 6–8 % wider); a second, twice as strong erosion (cut the faces into character-sized pieces the sideways chain walked into); lighting flattening with a local mean over an integral image (rescued 邪 at one window size, invented blobs at 流浪地球's edges at every other); looser threshold passes; a wider search box (`SNAP_ROOM` 2) with a near-margin rule; fused candidates outside the box — each broke an earlier poster. **The limit** (H on v300's cut 邪: "Leave it as it is."): a character the box misses and the pixels fuse with its surroundings stays outside; the frame's corner is the hand's fix. **A character written in bars alone (v376, H's 三立方咖啡 shop sign, 2026-09-09 — the card's picture began at 立: "Here's another wrong crop"):** 三 is three horizontal strokes with air between them, so under the box's cut it is three blobs, each about a twentieth of the text height — every one of them a speck by `SNAP_MIN`, and the snap began at the next character (Qwen's box 13–92 % across, the snap 31–91 %). Now bars of the same colour standing over one another — each `SNAP_BAR` 1.6 times wider than tall, within `SNAP_STACK` half a text height of each other, sharing at least 0.6 of their width, together between `SNAP_MIN` and 1.25 text heights tall and no wider than 1.5 of one — are put together into one character before the colours are weighed (三, 二, 三十 and the like). **One bar alone is not**: a rule under a line, the edge of a plate and an underline stay out. Measured on the sign rebuilt in the harness: the snap 14–75 % across where it was 26–75 %, so 三 is whole. Suite `verify-aiframe` (`bars`: the rebuilt sign with Qwen's real box — the snap's left edge at or under 20 % and the frame's at or under 18 %); the panel, autocard, picture, badpic and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the sign taken again is the check: 三立方咖啡 whole in the card's picture. **A plate is not the text (v315, H's parking sign 请您停车入位, 2026-09-07 — the card's picture ran from 11 to 100 % across with the plate ending at 81 %: "Warum ist das nicht mittig?"):** rebuilt in the harness from the card's crop laid into a photo of the ground's colour, with Qwen's real box `[100,430,560,550]` of 600×800: the white plate's part inside the box was 84 % of its width and 78 % of its height — under the background rule's 0.9 both ways — and its area (22 500 px) beat the six dark characters (10 000 px), so the light colour was the text, the plate its one "character", and the shadow pass then joined the light specks of the textured ground beside it (2 runs per row, 0.5 filled — a character's kind by every measure), 16–99 % across; the v313 edge rule fired on top of that. Now, before the colours are weighed, **a blob wider than two text heights that holds a row of two or more character-shaped clean blobs of the other colour** — at least 0.4 text heights tall, no wider than 1.3 times their height, within its width and at least half within its rows, side by side without overlap in one band — is the ground the characters stand on and drops. The row matters: the plain and the eroded pass both see the one character-shaped dark gap inside 流浪地球's brush title, and a first cut of the rule that only counted such blobs took the two sightings for two and dropped the title (the aiframe suite's upright 流浪地球 case fell to 35–96 % across); one gap is no row, and the title keeps its box. Measured: the snap ends on 位, 16–75 % across and 49–63 % down, the frame 10–81 % with the room. And the v313 rule asks whether the proposal's edge is the photo's own before it grows (`seenBase` against the photo, half a percent): on the whole photo there is nothing beyond, and the line no longer claims it. Suite `verify-aiframe` (the rebuilt parking sign with `textRegion`, `textLike` and `effScore` stood down: the snap's line within 13–18 % / 72–78 % across and 46–53 % / 60–66 % down, the frame 7–14 % to 77–85 % across, no edge line); the autoframe, picture and badpic suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the sign taken again is the check: the plate centred in the card's picture with the ground to the right gone. **A character as tall as the AI's box is still a character (v317, H's coconut-water carton 椰子水, 2026-09-07 — the card's crop cut the tops of 椰 and 水: "Warum hat er hier wieder was abgeschnitten??"):** Qwen's box for 椰子水 was 33–59 % down (its coordinates on a 0–1000 grid, which `picBox` scales), the characters 37–62 %, so they stood at 0.95 of the box's height and a little over it — and `SNAP_MAX` 0.95 of the box (v298, for the ring around a box) called every tall stroke of 椰, 子 and 水 "bigger than a character"; a blob that big counts only by its part inside the box with a text height's square of ink, which one character never holds, so the tall strokes fell out, and the short pieces that were left — 木's left stroke, 子's hook, 水's right part — made the box 42–58 % down, 24–81 % across (the log's numbers, read back from the pieces of the photo rebuilt from H's screenshot; a first reading of the log had blamed the crease through 子 and the light windows inside 耳, and a colour rule built on that was dropped unmerged once the pieces matched). Now a blob taller than 0.95 of the box is clean when it is no taller than 1.25 text heights and no wider than 1.5 times its height — a character reaching past a tight box —; a ring around the box is wider than that and still drops, and with two or more lines the text height is under half the box, so nothing changes there. Measured on the rebuilt carton with the box drawn two percent shorter than the characters: the snap 24–80 % across, 38–61 % down (the whole characters), where the same box gave the short pieces before; the parking sign and both 流浪地球 cases unchanged. Suite `verify-aiframe` (the carton with the tight box: the snap's line, the frame's top at or above 35 % and its bottom at or below 63 %); the autoframe, picture, badpic and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the carton taken again is the check: 椰子水 whole in the card's picture. **A box that passes the picture's edge by a little is read both ways, and the snap decides (v340, H's ARRI poster 突破光影边界, 2026-09-08 — the card's picture ARRI with the title cut at the bottom: "ARRI gehört nicht rein und chinesischer Text ist abgeschnitten"):** Qwen answered `[120,330,860,450]` for an 800×600 picture — pixels, the title running to the right edge, so x1 passed the width by 60 —, and `picBox` took anything above the picture's size for the 0–1000 grid: 12–86 % across, 33–45 % down is the blank blue above the title, the snap found nothing there, the frame was placed on it and the 16:9 window around it held ARRI and cut the title. The numbers alone cannot tell: the far 邪不压正 of v328 answered the same way and meant the grid (H's other poster of the day, 专业电影人首选, boxed within the picture and came out right — "Hier hat es ja funktioniert"). Now `picBoxPix` gives a second reading when the box passes the picture's width or height by at most `PIX_OVER` 1.2 (and stays under 1000): the numbers as pixels, clamped to the picture (`pic.boxAlt`); in `cropSign`'s box branch both readings are snapped (`snapBox` hands back `count`, the character-shaped blobs it took, both passes together), and **the pixel reading wins when its snap holds at least three such blobs and the grid's fewer** — Diagnostics "the AI's box 12–86 % across, 33–45 % down passes the picture's edge on the 0–1000 grid and holds no characters there — read as pixels it holds 28 blobs of a character's size: 15–100 % across, 55–75 % down", or "… read on the 0–1000 grid it holds 28 blobs of a character's size, read as pixels 2; the grid stays"; the snapped line names the box as read. A box beyond 1.2 × the picture is the grid as before, a box within the picture is pixels as before. What it costs: one more snap on such a box. Tried and dropped on the way: the share of rows that look like text in each box (the glow and the light beam over the blank blue counted as text rows, 66 against 60 %). Rebuilt in the harness from the whole-photo view of H's screenshot (`arri-photo.png`, the whole-photo proposal, Qwen's real answer): the pixel snap 14–92 % across, 48–67 % down — the title —, the frame 10–97 % across, 42–73 % down, ARRI out; the same poster with a grid box on the title that passes the width keeps the grid. Suite `verify-aiframe` (`arri`, `arriGrid`; the far and near 邪不压正 cases unchanged); the autocard, picture, badpic and english suites pass. Now the frame reaches **the photo's edge** on every side the AI names as cut: how far the line runs is unknown, the photo's edge is the only sure end, and the AI's box then places the frame on the line; the v312 fine-print rule keeps the small print beside it off the card. And **the picture the AI gets is always the area the reading started from with its straightening** (`picBase={orig:r.blob,dk}`, `sg.picBlob=dk.blob`) — the proposal, or the hand's frame —, never the placed frame's cut: a reading that ends weak after a sure placement is fragile (绿皮书: the quick look read the title at 97 % on the 1 000 px copy, the reading proper on the whole proposal did not), so the AI decides from the whole proposal — the main text only, without fine print or a line the edge cuts (v312) — and its box places the frame anywhere in it (`frameOnText` through `base`); the v303 centring inside the placed cut only remains after a v314 regrow, where the grown frame is what the AI saw. The v314/v318 regrow grows the frame the AI saw — the proposal — past the named edge, whatever the reader placed meanwhile. The AI's coordinates cannot tell which line is which, and the pixels alone cannot either — a first cut that let `snapBox` take such a band on its own took the faces under 邪不压正 and a band above 业主直租 for lines (the after, saved, savenowBox and qr cases fell) —, so **the reader decides**: when the AI read two lines or more, `snapBox` looks at the strip of 1.2 text heights below the union and above it, cut by its own Otsu, for a band of the text's colour that starts within half a text height of the union, is at least a third of the tallest taken blob and at most 1.2 text heights tall, holds strokes (two ink runs per row at least, under 0.85 filled), spans at least 0.4 of the union's width and does not run into the strip's far edge — one per side — and hands them back as `beyond` (in the bitmap's pixels, not taken); `cropSign`'s box branch cuts each band from the picture the AI saw with 0.3 band heights of room, scaled to about 64 px, as a JPEG, reads it with the first simplified worker (`readPass`, no progress lines), and **the band joins the box when the reader's line is one the AI read** — at least three of its characters and half of them (characters and digits) in one of the answer's lines; the faces read as garbage and a Latin line as nothing of the kind, so they stay out. What it costs: one more reader pass per band, after the AI's answer, on the main thread's turn — a few hundred milliseconds; a line of the answer the reader cannot read (a font it does not know) stays out, and the frame is as before. Now it runs for every answer, and **a band that touches the union is a line the box cut through**: the candidate carries `side` and `near`, and when the band starts at the union's edge (within a tenth of its height) the cut the reader gets reaches 0.6 band heights into the union, so the characters come whole (the arrow between them reads as 人 and the match rule of v324 — three characters, half of them — takes the line all the same). What it costs: one reader pass per band beside a one-line answer, after the AI's call. With no fine print in the answer nothing changes. **(3) The shadow pass took the poster's mat and the wall beside it** on both sides of the title — cut light against the red by the strip's own Otsu, one blob 130 px wide, 0.6–0.7 filled, 2–3 runs per row, a character by every measure of v306 — because the strip was only a tenth of a text height taller than the line and clipped the band to a character's height; the strip has 0.35 text heights of room above and below since, and a blob taller than 1.35 text heights is out (the mat band shows 210 px against 124). Not yet field-checked — the poster taken again from across the room is the check: the title with a little room, nothing of the wall. **And the picture in the finished card's row is centred again** (v328, H's screenshot on the wide screen: the crop at the box's left edge with no blurred fill at the right): the v325 rule `.shot .result img{width:auto}` had outranked the photo box's own `.picbox .signimg{width:100%}` and `.picbg{width:120%}`, so the crop sat at its natural width at the left; only the linked row's thumbnails keep `width:auto` now. **The characters hung on the poster's mat (v330, H's 邪不压正 taken close, 2026-09-08 — the card's picture with 正's right stroke cut: "the 正 is slightly cut off"):** rebuilt from the whole photo in H's screenshot (`hm-near-photo.png`, the phone's proposal 10–90 % across, 21–75 % down, Qwen's real answer on the 0–1000 grid with 姜文电影 as fine print): the snap had taken 不压 alone (20–69 % of the picture), the frame ended in the middle of 正 and the 16:9 window happened to show most of it. Under the box's Otsu cut the grey wall is light, so the wall, the white mat, the faces and 邪 and 正 — each touching the mat — were one light blob whose part inside the box was the whole line; **the plate rule of v315 dropped it**, because the thin red gaps inside the blocky 压 (22 × 91 px, 66 × 120 px) passed as a row of two character-shaped dark blobs — a character is at least 0.45 as wide as tall since. And once it stayed, the take of a fused blob's part inside (v299) ran from the mat's strip to the box's edge, cutting 正 anyway; so **a wide fused take (over two text heights) that stands in a line with clean characters is read column by column like a fused blob** (`wide`, `seed` on the take; the column pass runs over `fused` and the wide takes; the take and its eroded twin go once a stroke run stands in for it; a fused title alone, 流浪地球, keeps its take), **every stroke run within half a text height of the line joins** and the line grows with each (until v329 only the run nearest the line's nearest blob — the mat's blob carries 邪 at the left end and 正 at the right), and **the column test looks only at the ink joined to the band**: the blob's ink elsewhere in the column no longer counts (the wall above the poster and the faces below belong to the same blob, and no column of the characters was free of them), and the joined ink may run on by `reach` 0.15 of the band beyond it (0.25 until v329; the slanted mat's columns run 20 px past the band at the right end and would have joined at 0.25; the mat's own columns still run the whole height). The far poster of v328 had the same fault unseen: its "title alone" snap of 36–68 % was 不压 alone, and it holds all four characters now (25–86 % across of its picture, the mat out; the far case's expectation was corrected). Measured on the picture the app sends (800 × 550): the snap 101–694 px across — 邪 from its first stroke, 正 to its last, the mat out on both sides —, the frame 14–85 % across and 29–54 % down of the photo where it was 17–73 %. Suite `verify-aiframe` (`near`: `textRegion` returning the phone's proposal and `shapeBox` stood down, the snap 13–87 % across, the frame 11–17 % to 82–88 %; the far, lamp, wander, green, plate, carton, QR, street and cut-line cases unchanged); the autocard, english, picture and badpic suites pass. **Field-confirmed 2026-09-08** (H, the poster taken again at v330: "Works Great").

**The placed frame:** `frameOnText(id,orig,base,rect,angle,by)` maps the rectangle from the straightened copy through the frame the reading started from (`base`, captured at `cropSign`'s start — v297; until v296 `CROP.rect`, which a quick-look placement may have replaced meanwhile). **It turns with the straightening** (v297: `a` = the deskew angle when the copy was straightened, ≥ 1.5°; the frame is the rectangle itself, its cut through `cropBlob` the upright text as for a frame turned by hand (v185), the card's `frame` carries the angle, Diagnostics "…, turned by -8°"; until v296 the frame was the axis-aligned bounding box of the turned rectangle, wider and taller than the text with the text running diagonally through it) — **unless the turned frame would leave the photo** (v311, H's 无名 poster: a level poster on a busy wall — the branches of its own artwork, a picture frame above it — straightened by −14° on a flat, spurious profile peak (the variance at −14° only 1.09 × that at 0°, a plateau from −15° to −13°; the 12° test sign peaks at 1.8 ×, but the first 邪不压正 photo rotated by 11° at 1.06 ×, so the profile alone cannot tell a busy photo's false angle from a busy poster's true one, and the estimate is left alone); Qwen read the poster from the tilted copy with its black wedges, its box `[30,320,650,800]` of 673×800 covered the copy, the frame at −14° ran from −8 to 119 % across and 32 to 107 % down, and the card's picture was the poster tilted with black wedges: "Und das hier ging ja ordentlich daneben"): the frame's four corners are turned onto the photo, and when one lies outside by more than 2 % of the photo's size the frame is placed upright instead — the box's bounding box through `unrotatedBox`, clipped to the photo, angle 0 — with the line "… — upright, the frame turned by −14.0° would leave the photo" (measured on the photo cut from H's screenshot: 0–100 % across, 20–100 % down, the poster's lower part upright); a wrong angle whose turned frame still fits keeps its tilt. **A large straightening the reading does not confirm is not trusted for the picture at all (v346, H's noodle sign 虞西苏 面馆, 2026-09-08 — the card's picture the whole photo with the banner small in the middle: "Hier ist wieder zu viel Luft drumrum"; the diagnostics of the sign taken again):** the banner hangs straight in a doorway, and the profile straightened the frame by **−20°** on a spurious peak (the door frame, the window grid); every pass then came back garbage, so the AI got the tilted copy, read 虞西苏 / 面馆 from it and boxed the text at 11–79 % across, 31–94 % down — but with no pass confirming the angle, `sure` was false, the turned frame would have left the photo and the v311 rule placed the box **upright**: the bounding box of a rectangle turned by 20° is half again as large, so it covered the proposal, the log carried no "frame placed" line at all (the frame equalled the proposal — "nothing to move") and the card's picture was the app's whole proposal. Now `trustAngle` = the reading confirms the angle (`sureAngle`, the v333 test, hoisted so the picture path and the placement share it) **or** the angle is under `SKEW_TRUST` 12°: without it the AI gets `r.blob`, the frame as it is, `picBase.dk` is null and the box maps back one to one (Diagnostics "the straightening of −20.0° is not confirmed by the reading — the AI gets the frame as it is"); `picBase` carries its `base` since, so the picture's frame is the proposal whether it was straightened or not (until v345 `seenBase` read `picSeen.dk`), and the v313 edge rule follows that field. A confirmed angle keeps the turned frame (绿皮书 at −18.5°, the reader read 绿肥书 at 95 %), and an unconfirmed small one too (邪不压正 at −8° and −3.5°, where the bounding box costs little). What it changes on H's 无名 poster (v311, unconfirmed −14°): the AI's box now places an upright frame of its own, 0–53 % across, 57–92 % down, instead of the clipped bounding box of the turned one. Suite `verify-aiframe` (the blade case expects the new line and the tight upright frame; the tilt, near, far, plate, icons, street, cutline and arri cases unchanged); the autocard, picture, badpic, savenow and english suites pass. **Field-confirmed 2026-09-08** (H, the sign taken again at v346: "Noodle sign is fixed"). **A turned frame the reading confirms is trimmed to the photo instead (v333, H's 绿皮书 poster photographed at 18°, 2026-09-08 — the card's picture the whole lower photo, half of it the desk: "Hier ist viel zu viel Luft unten!"):** the straightening was right (the poster stands at −18°, the reader read 绿肥书 at 95 % on the straightened copy), the AI's box ran to the copy's right edge — into the filled corner — and the turned frame with its room reached past the photo, so the v311 rule made it upright: the bounding box of a rectangle turned by 18° is a fifth wider and taller than the text, clipped to the photo it was the whole proposal (0–100 % across, 31–100 % down), and the card showed the desk. Now `frameOnText` takes `sure` — the reading confirms the straightening: the reader's own placements always (`placeRect`: the quick look, the close look and the winning pass place only from lines that pass `textLike` on the straightened copy), the AI's box when any pass on that copy passes `textLike` — and a turned frame that would leave the photo keeps its turn and is **trimmed to the photo** (`fitTurned`: in the frame's own turned coordinates the side whose corner sticks out furthest moves in, until every corner lies in the photo; the log says "… turned by −18.5° — trimmed to the photo"); without `sure` the v311 upright fallback stays (无名's spurious −14°: the reader read nothing on that copy). **And a turned window gives up the widening that would leave the photo** (`windowRect` through `fitTurned` with the frame as the extents the sides may not pass; v329–v332 let `cropBlob` fill what lay outside — a wedge of fill beside a poster at 18°), so the card's picture is narrower than 16:9 there and the box shows the blurred fill beside it; a frame that sticks out itself stays as it is. Rebuilt in the harness from the whole-photo view of H's screenshot (`gb-tilt-pic.png`, the phone's proposal, `estimateSkew` forced to −18.5° once, Qwen's real answer in the picture's pixels, `textLike` stood up once the AI is asked): the frame 11–87 % across, 29–92 % down at −18.5° with every corner in the photo, the window 0.92:1 — the title and the date line upright with the poster's lower blue band under them, no desk. Suite `verify-aiframe` (`tilt`; the blade case still upright); the autocard, english, picture and savenow suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the tilted poster taken again is the check: the text upright, the desk gone. **The trim gives up the room, never the text, and what still sticks out is filled (v334, H's 流浪地球 poster at 14°, 2026-09-08 — the card's picture a narrow cut with 郭 and 球 cut: "Crop ist wieder bissl Käse"):** the AI's box was nearly the whole straightened copy (13–98 % across, 29–97 % down — the title with the names and credits fills the poster), so the trim of v333 took a quarter of the width to bring the corners in, and cut the text with it. Now `fitTurned` takes `keep` — the sides may move only as far as the room around the snapped box (`sure.box` from the AI branch; a reader's placement carries no box, a tenth of the frame a side), a side that reaches it stops there, and the frame keeps its turn with the corners past the photo's edge, which `cropBlob` fills with the crop's dominant colour (v185); the log says "… turned by 14° — trimmed to the text, the rest reaches past the photo's edge", or "trimmed to the photo" when the room was enough; `windowRect`'s fit stops at the frame the same way. On 流浪地球 the frame is the photo's width and a little more at 14°, every character whole, a dark wedge in two corners. **Three faults of the snap showed on 绿皮书 at 18° on the way:** the poster's pale lower half, fused with the copy's filled corner, was taken by its part inside (v299's rule; 590 × 347 px, the whole box's width) and carried the snap to the copy's edge — so **a wide take that stands beside clean characters of its line and holds no stroke column of that line goes** (the v330 column pass already read it; a fused title without clean neighbours keeps its take), and **every take carries its seed** (the field survived the plain pass's background rule only in the eroded pass, and an eroded take had no seed to read from — the eroded pass's pixel lies in the plain mask too); then the date line 3月1日 全国上映 under the title is dark on the pale half, the other colour, so **the band search below and above the union tries the other colour where the text's colour gave no band** (the reader still confirms the band as a line of the answer — here the light streak pieces had already carried the union over the date line, and the band found below was the fine print 改编自真实故事, read as garbage and left out), and a band **a quarter of the tallest taken blob** counts (a third until v333: the date line stands at 0.33 of the title, and the AI kept it as a line of the text). Tried and dropped unmerged: a row of small characters from 0.2 text heights for the plate rule (the gaps inside the blocky 邪不压正 and the brush strokes of 流浪地球 made such rows too — the near poster fell back to 不压), a per-band pixel test of the trimmed strips against the copy's fill colour (the poster's own blue beside the title stands out from the grey fill as much as a character does). Measured in the harness: 绿皮书's snap 28–81 % across, 17–61 % down (the title, the date line inside through the streak pieces), the frame 10–92 % across, 30–80 % down at −18.5° with no trim needed, the window 1.24:1 with the title and the date line upright; 流浪地球's snap 9–98 % across, the frame 0–102 % across at 14° with every character; the near and far 邪不压正, both 流浪地球 cases and the parking sign unchanged. Suite `verify-aiframe` (`tilt` with the new numbers, `we` from the whole-photo view of H's screenshot — the frame's cut at the frame's size, the "reaches past" line); the autocard, english, picture, badpic and savenow suites pass. Not yet field-checked — both posters taken again are the check: 绿皮书 upright without the desk, 流浪地球 with 郭 and 球 whole and small dark wedges at two corners. Once placed: `CROP.rect`, `CROP.proposed="text"`, `CROP.followed`, `CROP.hidden` goes, the layer gets `.framed`, the bar reads "Reading the text …" with Save now, Diagnostics "frame placed on the text: 8–91 % across, 65–85 % down" (`frameOnText`; a text that fills the proposal, every edge within 1 %, shows the proposal itself). **The card image is the placed frame's cut** (`cropBlob` with the new rect, `placedCut`; the second look's `cutUnrotated` band only when no frame was placed), the picture the AI gets is that cut, and the card's `frame` is the placed frame — the frame, the enlarged view, the picture and the stored frame are one thing. **It never moves after that**: a later re-read by hand starts a fresh log and never places (`CROP.proposed` goes at the hand's first gesture, `wireCrop`'s pointerdown). **A card saved with Save now gets the placed frame too** (v304, H's three posters on v303 — 流浪地球, 邪不压正, 绿皮书 — every card with the whole frame's crop and the Review flag: "Enger crop funktioniert manchmal, aber nicht immer" — Save now takes the frame away (`CROP=null`), so `frameOnText`'s `PENDING` guard had refused every placement and `finishPending` kept the crop of the frame the card was saved with): `PLACED[id]` = the frame placed while the card waited, only when the reading started from the app's own frame (`READ_APP[id]`, set at `cropSign`'s start from `CROP.hidden`/`proposed`, carried by `saveNow` as `reading.app` and by `resumePending`; the hand's frame stays the card's), a frame already placed at the tap becomes `PLACED` at once (`saveNow`, `CROP.followed`), the AI gets that frame's cut and centres inside it, `finishPending` takes `PLACED[id]` as the card's `frame` and the placed cut as its `img`, and `abandonReading`, `finishPending` and `failPending` drop it; the `frameOnText` guard is one test (`pend`); the Edit form's hand-off (`RECROP` → `PENDING`) carries no `app`, so nothing moves there. The box branch's catch logs `logErr("snap")` and "the AI's box could not be used: …" instead of swallowing the error.

**While the app looks:** a stroke frames by hand (`hiddenTake` in `wireCrop`: from 8 px of movement the search and its reading stop — `abandonReading` —, the frame is drawn from the press point and read as any hand frame; a tap changes nothing, the search goes on; the click handler and the enlarged view ignore a hidden frame; the layer carries no `.framed` while hidden, so the stroke draws instead of scrolling). The Edit form's Crop again keeps the shown proposal with its preview and wait (`RECROP`), a Save-now card has no frame left (`PENDING`), a turned frame is the hand's. **The frame's dashes travel along its edge while the reading and the AI work on it** (v295; H: "show swirls of color in the image while analyzing the frame area (or propose something better!!!)", then on the blobs: "I'm not sure if I'm happy with the swirls.
