I just want the user to know that something's happening with the crop frame"): while `READING[id]` holds a step that is no failure, the frame carries `.working` and a `.work` overlay with an SVG rectangle on its edge (`inset:-2px`, the rect 1 px inside, stroke #fff 2 px, `stroke-dasharray 7 5`, `march` .8 s linear; the static dashed border transparent meanwhile, the dark outline and the handles stay, nothing on the picture, no touches taken; still under `prefers-reduced-motion`); in the inbox and Crop again alike, gone with the editor; nothing while the frame is still hidden. **Rejected on the way, do not bring back unasked:** the sweeping tint band over the photo of v288–v290 (H had asked for it as "some apps show a wafting color over the image while it's analyzed by AI", and once the quick look placed the frame within a second: "If it's so fast, then we don't need the wafting color, right?"; `.shotwrap .scan`, `--tint-scan` and `@keyframes scan` left styles.css) and the two blurred blobs of the tint and a warm gold drifting inside the frame of v294 (the swirls). **Also found on the way (v290):** the reading status writes the bar straight into the photo's box (`readingStatus`), and the traditional reader's chain that runs on in the background when the copies made the reading strong (v236) once kept sending "recognizing … N %" after the editor was up — a finished reading bumps its run (`done(r)` at every exit of `cropSign`) and the chain checks `r.done` between passes and stops; and **the reader is warmed when the Camera tab opens** (`warmReader` in `renderInbox`, once per session, only when all `OCR_FILES` are on the phone), so the first photo does not pay the reader's load. The guide's first paragraph describes it ("The app looks for the text and draws a frame around it while it reads … Let go, and the reading starts again.", six languages).

**Measured in the four-core sandbox,** from the photo landing to the frame: the synthetic poster (drawings above 手作冰淇淋) 0.3 s, the red 有 0.1 s, H's five-line poster 0.9 s (1.4 s as the session's first photo, with the reader's load); the editor follows at 2.6–4.8 s; the phone's eight cores are not measured, and the 2 s fallback stays for a slow phone. The frames: the synthetic poster 8–91 % across, 65–85 % down (手作冰淇淋 across the whole thumbnail, screenshot `autoframe-list`), the red 有 the ink-row proposal, H's five-line poster a trim of 2 %; the proposal on H's street photo of the two-line sign takes the fence below it (the fence bars look like text rows) and the reader's placement tightens; nothing to fix on the ten regression images, which are tight crops and get the whole photo. **Field-confirmed:** the frame on the 流浪地球 title (v299/v300, H: "Wondering earth works now") and the quick look's frame on the 绿皮书 title block (v301, H: "you did really well by identifying the main characters in the image. And cropping around them"). **Not yet field-checked:** the marching dashes (v295), the centred frame (v303), Save now with the placed frame (v304), the 业主直租 sign without its code (v305), the shadow pass on the lamp photo (v306), the slow reading's move (v310), the upright frame on 无名 (v311), the main text only on 青春无烟 (v312), the frame reaching beyond the proposal on the 北京现代 badge (v313), the cut line read again on 大闸蟹 (v314), the plate dropped by the snap on the parking sign (v315), the tall characters kept on the coconut carton (v317), the wave logo no longer text and the cut line's side on the Nongfu bottle (v318), the one bar for the reader's placement and the whole proposal to the AI (v319), fine print left out of the placement (v320), the winning pass placing the frame (v321), the reader's boxes trimmed by the snap (v322), a line the AI's box left out read by the reader and joined (v324), and how long the search takes on the Xiaomi. **Suites:** `verify-autoframe` (H's poster: the search state — hidden proposal "whole", no `.framed`, "Finding the text …", no Save now, the log's "(not shown)" —, then the frame shown as proposed with `.framed`, Save now and "Reading the text …", the five lines read, Cancel and Crop without a proposal; the synthetic poster: the hidden proposal over drawings and text, the frame placed on the text with `followed`, the rect unchanged through the editor, the picture the frame's cut to the pixel, no re-read after 1.8 s, Save storing that frame and picture; the red 有's proposal, a corner dragged by hand staying where the hand put it and reading 有; a tap during the search changes nothing, a stroke takes over with the hand's frame; `FRAME_WAIT` at 0.3 s: the proposal shown after 0.5 s, then placed with `followed` (v310); the working frame's dashes, the transparent border, the overlay taking no touches, gone with the editor), `verify-aiframe` (H's 邪不压正 poster with Qwen's real pixel answer: the box path from the proposal, the snap within 15–20 % / 82–87 % across and 27–32 % / 70–75 % down, the frame at −8° reaching under 12 % and past 90 % across, the three log lines, Save with `frame.a` −8; H's 流浪地球 poster from his screenshot and the upright photo with Qwen's real answers; 绿皮书 with the real box [30,20,680,250] of 800×284; the placed-cut cases with `effScore` at 0 — the frame placed by the quick look, the picture as wide as the placed cut, the centred line with the frame inside the placed one; 业主直租 with the code and `textLike` stood down; the lamp photo of 邪不压正 (the snap's left edge under 30 %, the frame's left under 26 %); Save now on the placed frame and on the shown proposal; the 无名 photo upright inside the photo from 20 % down; a three-line answer with line boxes whose small lines are dropped; `picBox` on fractions, pixels, the grid and junk; a frame drawn by hand during the search never moved), and the twopass, consensus, picture, badpic, frameadjust, recrop, savenow, diag, english and guide suites alongside; the search phase and the placed frame checked by screenshot in light and dark.  Otherwise, or after Cancel: a finger stroke draws a rectangle (bright dashed frame with a dark outline, nothing dimmed), the corner handles resize it, dragging inside moves it, a preview of the cut sits below. **Reading starts by itself 1.2 s after the finger lifts** (`READ_TIMER`; a corner drag restarts the wait; "Read now" and a small "Image only" link remain). **The frame can be turned** (v185, H: "enable rotating the crop rectangle", described first and built on "Go"): a round handle with a stem hangs 34 px above the frame's top edge (`.h.rot`, `ROT_HANDLE`); dragging it turns the frame around its centre (`CROP.rect.a` in degrees, the raw pointer, not the clamped one, so the finger may leave the photo; snapped to 0 within 1.5°), the frame is drawn with `transform:rotate(a)` and the corner and inside tests run in the frame's own turned coordinates (`rotPt`, `toLocal`; a corner drag in the old centre's turned system is shifted so the rect turned around its new centre lands on the same spot), moving keeps the angle, a new frame starts at 0°. `cropBlob` draws the photo turned back around the frame's centre, so the cut is the frame's content **upright** — the reader's straightening then finds nothing to do (`sg.angle` 0), the card image is the upright cut, and what the frame covers outside the photo takes the dominant colour of the real pixels around it (`dominantRGB`, shared with `deskewBlob` since v185; probing the canvas outside the photo once made that colour black and the reader read garbage); the enlarged view of a turned frame shows the upright cut itself (`_prevURL`). Measured on the 12° test sign: a frame turned to −13° cuts an upright image (deskew 0) that reads both lines at once. It stays a rectangle — not the four free corners of v127/v128. **The browser's own touch behaviours stay off the app** (v248–v249; H's screenshot while adjusting a frame in Crop again: Chrome's "Copy image / Download image / Share image" sheet over the frame — "Don't make those things pop up while adjusting the crop", then "Find all such useless behaviours and remove them"): one `contextmenu` listener on the document cancels the long-press menu on every picture, canvas and control (`img, canvas, button, .croplayer, .shotwrap, .drawsheet, .picbox, .thumbbox, .reticle, .ck, .chip, .tab, .grade, .seg, .linked`), and styles.css gives pictures and canvases `-webkit-touch-callout:none`, `-webkit-user-drag:none` and `user-select:none`, the controls (buttons, tabs, chips, grade tiles, strips, the picker, the switch, the drawing sheet, the crop layer, the text box, the photo boxes, the enlarged view, the linked row) `user-select:none`, and `body` `touch-action:manipulation` — no double-tap zoom, the crop layer and the pad keep their stricter values (the callout is Safari's, for the iPhone). **The badges and the forms' labels are non-selectable too since v272** (`.badge`, `label`, `.topline`, `.mrow .t` in the same rule — H's screenshot: a long press on the Edit badge had opened the Xiaomi's dictionary card for "edit" under the form); plain text — More's sentences, the fields, the meaning — keeps its menu, so a meaning can still be copied (measured: `user-select` none on the badge, the label and the hint, auto on the meaning field and More's sentence). **The keyboard's autocorrect, capitalisation and spell-check are off** on the fields that hold Chinese, pinyin or a search — the Add and Edit forms' characters and pinyin, the context word and its pinyin, the line inputs under the strips, the preview's pinyin and the Cards search (`autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false"`; Android capitalised the first letter of a pinyin textarea and underlined every syllable) — the meaning fields keep them, English prose wants them. Measured in Chromium: the contextmenu event is cancelled on the front picture and text box, a tab, a grade tile, a list thumbnail, a chip, the Edit form's crop, a strip character, the pad, the reference view and the sheet, and not on the meaning field; the attributes sit on the pinyin, line and search fields; the phone's long press not yet field-checked. **A changed frame drops the old reading at once** (v286, H's SF Express card, 2026-09-07: "Crop and recrop didn't work as expected … It doesn't crop to my selection" — the card carried the whole panel and both lines under a frame pulled onto 顺丰速运 alone): until v285 a corner drag or a move only restarted the 1.2 s wait, and the old reading stayed in `SIGN[id]`; any re-render in that window — on the phone the first reading's AI answer landing after the drag — drew the old editor with its Save card again, and Save stored the old picture and text with the new frame. Now the frame's release calls `abandonReading(id)` (the timer, the run number, the reading and its status go; a running reading abandons at its next step, the old AI answer finds no reading) before the preview and the re-read. Suite `verify-frameadjust` (the two-line sign read whole, the top corner pulled onto the second line: A — the re-read gives one line and Save stores it with the new frame and a low crop; B — a re-render during the wait shows no Save card; C — the drag during the first reading's AI check, its late answer changes nothing, the re-read's own answer fills the card); the lock, zoom, rotate, recrop, savenow, autoframe, touchcrop and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked. **Once a frame exists it is the only frame** (H, v129–v132, seen on the phone): a stroke outside it draws nothing and **scrolls the page** like anywhere else (the layer carries `.framed` with `touch-action:pan-y` from the finger's release and whenever `CROP.rect` is set in the render; the frame and its handles keep `touch-action:none` and take the finger), adjusting the frame is allowed at any time, during a reading too, and re-reads; a new frame needs **Cancel** first, which removes the frame and stops a running reading. **A tap outside the frame enlarges the framed area** (v169, H: "toggle between full image display and only cropped area display by tipping with a finger outside the cropped area", described first and built on "Go"): a short tap without movement (under 8 px and 600 ms; a swipe still scrolls) sets `CROP.zoom` — **the tap is taken from the click event** (v206, H: "tipping outside a cropped frame and then reducing the visible image to the cropped part does not seem to work with iPhone": until v205 it was read from `pointerup` with the distance and time check, and on a layer that lets the page scroll (`touch-action:pan-y`) iOS Safari turns the touch into a scroll gesture and sends `pointercancel`, so no pointerup ever came; a click is the platform's own "a tap, not a scroll" on every browser — the press position and time are kept for the same check, so a mouse drag outside the frame, which also ends in a click, does not toggle, and a frame gesture marks the layer (`layer._gesture`) so its closing click is ignored; measured in Chromium with mouse and with emulated touch, the iPhone itself not yet — no WebKit in the sandbox), the photo box then shows the cut alone with a 4 % margin, enlarged to the box's width (`.shotzoom`, a background image positioned by percentages, `zoomStyle`), the frame's handles hidden; a tap on the enlarged view goes back, the frame is where it was; adjusting the frame stays in the whole-photo view; Cancel clears it with the frame (`CROP=null`). **Each reading has a run number** (`READ_RUN[id]`): a reading that a corner drag or Cancel superseded abandons at its next step — no status text, no result, no AI call (v117: two readings of one frame once asked DeepSeek twice within a second). **The frame stays on the photo** through reading and the card preview (`cropRectStyle` re-draws it from `CROP.rect` in percentages after any re-render) and goes only on Save or Cancel. No dialogs in the flow: no frame → nothing happens; the same text from the same photo is an inline note ("This text is already saved from this photo.", `d.c===c&&d.shot===id`), from another photo it becomes a second card (v118).
3. **Read** (`cropSign`): the framed area is **straightened first** (`deskewBlob`: projection-profile skew estimate on a ≤ 360 px copy, ±20° in 1° steps then half degrees, text pixels = far from the median brightness so both polarities count; below 1.5° nothing happens; **the rotated copy is for the reader only — the card image is the crop as framed, never rotated** (v145, H: "don't show the cropped images rotated in the preview, I don't want to see that gray frame around them"); the corners the rotation opens in the reading copy are filled with the crop's dominant colour — the most frequent 4-bit colour bin, then the mean of its pixels (v138, H's 美团 logo: one edge pixel was the dark logo frame, the dark corners became the "ink" of the chromaticity copy, the black characters fell on its background side, the ink height came out as 0 and the reader never saw 美团 at a fitting size; a channel-wise median is no real colour and a border colour is the frame line again — both changed the ink height of 业主直租 and lost its winning pass); H: a tilted 首都铁骑 read as garbage) → on-device OCR line by line (Tesseract.js 7, `chi_sim`, PSM 6, symbols under confidence 35 dropped; `readPass`; **digits are kept** when fairly sure (confidence ≥ 70) and near the characters' height (0.4–1.6 × the line's median box height — the boxes' heights swing: a ribbon ring once read as a 1 at 2.1 ×, the real 30 of 30分钟 measured 0.55–0.7 ×; H: the number was missing from the translation); letters stay out; a number reads as itself in `lineMeaning` and stays one token in the pinyin, `pySpaced`) — the status text lives in `READING[id]` and is re-queried on every update, so a re-render (foreground return, AI answer for another photo) cannot swallow it or the error; the user sees **one line with a moving bar** (`readingHTML`: "Reading the text …") for every step, only failures show as text (H: the steps are of no use to the user); a step that lasts longer than `READ_STUCK` (20 s) shows its own text under the bar ("still at: loading the reader …", `READ_AT`), because a stuck step is a failure; `cropSign` is guarded from its first line (the cut-out, the reader load) and every failure shows as "Reading failed: …" and goes to the error log — → second look (`secondLook`) and competing readings (below) → `SIGN[id]` = `{lines, orig, conf, boxes, img, angle, tightened, region, alts}` (`img` = the straightened, maybe tightened crop the text was read from, `boxes[k][i]` = each symbol's box in it; `region.passes` = the competition and `region.tightBlob` the tight crop, for diagnosis; **`alts`** (v96) = up to six other readings, distinct texts best first, always including the traditional reader's best and the best reading of another length — the AI gets them as `alt` ("the true text is often a mix of them, or a well-known name they circle around"; 养兴多 / 义乐多 / 和准浴多 → 养乐多), the picker offers their characters at the same position (`altCharsAt`, lines with the same character count only), and they are saved on the card as `alts` for a later AI check) → the editor, laid out **like the Edit form** (H, v90): a **Characters** field (H, v114 — not "Text"; the Edit form and the Add form say the same instead of "Word" / "Sign text") with a **character strip** per line (and a **line input under the strip** for typing the correct text (v120, H: "like in Edit mode" — it went in v109 as "unnötig" and came back for the 拉 door plate that no reading got right; on `change` the strip is redrawn from the typed line and the AI check runs again, `wireSlines(root,onInput,onCommit)`; the Edit form has the same input, and its strip follows typing the same way since v120)), a Pinyin field and a Meaning field that follow the reading and the AI check unless edited by hand (`sg.pinTouched`/`meanTouched`, set by the fields' input handlers in `wireSlines` — a hand-written meaning saves as verified, no offline model), a **⚑ Flag for review** checkbox with a note field (`sg.flag`/`flagNote`, `data-sflag`/`data-snote` — H, v94: flag a new card at once instead of opening it again; the Add form has the same pair, `f-flag`/`f-note`, kept in the draft), the source under the meaning ("from the phrasebook" / "composed word by word, unverified"; after a good AI answer nothing — **never "checked by the AI"**, H v105: "not relevant for user", and since v154 not the model's remark either, H: "don't show the OCR slip message to the user"; the remark stays in the post-save suggestion box and in Diagnostics); "Tap a character to change it, or type the line below." sits directly under the first strip, before the line field and the simplified reference, in the preview and the Edit form alike (`slineHTML`, H v111/v112, the second half since v120) and the head line shows the low-confidence note, or in red "This reading looks wrong — frame the text tightly and read again, or fix the characters."); **while the AI check runs, the moving bar with "Checking pinyin and meaning …" sits under the Meaning field** in place of the source line (v200, H: the meaning takes a while to come out clean and nothing said the app was still at it; `busyHTML`/`AI_BUSY_TEXT` in `signPreview` while `sg.aiBusy` — the meaning field holds the word-by-word gloss meanwhile, and the bar tells that it will change; the head line carries no AI status; the Add form and the Edit form show the same bar under their Meaning field while their AI request runs); no per-line pinyin and no gloss table any more; with a red hint when only 1–2 characters were found with low confidence (frame tightly, drag a corner) → **automatic AI check** (`signAskAI`, when the AI is live) → the preview shows pinyin and meaning (no AI status text — H, v105; no remark — v154) → **Save card**. **Traditional characters on the photo** (v101, H: "the hanzi doesn't match the image" — the Yakult logo is 養樂多, the card 养乐多): the photo is taken to show traditional characters by a **vote** (`tradPhotoOf`, v113): every pass that read a line of the final text (the same line, or one character off) casts its effective score for its reader, the merged composite abstains, and traditional wins only with more than 1.5 × the simplified reader's votes (`r.trad`, `sg.trad`); **a silent simplified reader is no vote** (v142, H's 美团 photo: the only pass agreeing with the final text was the traditional reader's lone 国, the simplified reader had read 加 and 权, so 0 votes against 54 marked the photo traditional and H's typed 美团 came out as 美團 — "Richtig ist aber simplified"): with no simplified vote at all, traditional needs at least two traditional passes on a text of at least three characters. **A line typed over the reading drops the flag** (v142): when the typed line keeps none of the reading's characters, `sg.trad` is cleared and the preview goes back to plain characters (`wireSlines`' `onCommit`); the reading is no evidence for a text that has nothing left of it. On a simplified sign both readers read the same lines, so it stays off — H's vertical escalator sign 儿童及行动不便者乘梯须有成年人陪同 was marked traditional in v101–v112 because one line from the traditional reader inside the merged winner was enough; a text with no traditional form at all (推) is never marked — both readers give the same glyph, so there is nothing to vote on (v116); on the Yakult logo the simplified reader never produces 乐 (樂), so the traditional side wins. **The mark can be changed by hand** (v146–v150, H's 9楼 was marked traditional and shown as 9樓: "Falsch erkannt als traditional. Bitte änderbar machen im Crop modus und unter Edit"): a two-way switch **Simplified | Traditional** under the line input in the Read preview and the Edit form (`scriptSwitchHTML`/`setScript`, `.seg`/`.segbtn` in the chip look, `data-scriptseg`/`data-scriptset`, `.scriptline` with the switch at the right, no "Simplified …" reference line beside it — the switch starts on the side the reader detected and shows the state). It appears **only when the reader detected traditional characters on the photo, or the card carries the traditional form** (`sg.tradDetected`), and once shown it stays for that preview or form so Simplified can be undone; a plain reading has no switch. Traditional sets the mark through the character table, Simplified drops it; the strip, the line input and the label follow at once, the AI is not asked again, `sg.tradUser` keeps the v142 typed-line rule from overriding a hand-set mark, and the Edit form saves the choice (`upd.trad` set or deleted). Rejected on the way: a link "Not traditional" / "Traditional on the photo" (v146, H: "works, but could be misunderstood"), the switch on every text with the Traditional side greyed where both scripts are the same (v149, after the vanishing switch on 工作 had looked like a bug — H: "show the toggle option only if traditional chinese was detected in the photo"); the checkbox of v101–v103 and the separate field went earlier (v104, v110). Otherwise the preview shows the **text itself in traditional characters** (H, v104: no checkbox — "show text in traditional and add simplified for reference"): the strip buttons and the line input show the traditional form (`tradLine`, from `sg.tradText`, which follows the text unless edited: the AI's `zht` when it matches in length — the payload carries `script:"traditional"` and the prompt asks for `zht` then —, else the character table, which cannot tell 分鐘 from 分鍾), the label reads "Characters (traditional, as on the photo)" (no reference line under it since v148; the front and the Cards list keep theirs); typing into the line converts to simplified underneath (`t2s`, `sg.tradTouched`) and the picker keeps the traditional line in step (`s2t` of the chosen character); the taps and candidates act on the simplified line, the card's key. The Edit form shows a traditional card the same way (label, strip with its line input in traditional, reference line); the separate traditional-characters field went in v110 (H: redundant) — the strip's line carries the form, and there is no switch back to simplified. Saved as **`card.trad`** (line breaks like `c`, the key `c` stays simplified for dictionary, pinyin and dedupe). The **front** then shows the traditional form with **one pill "Traditional" under the box** and the back carries "Simplified 养乐多" above the pinyin (`simpRefHTML`, `.script.back`; v227, H's "Go" on the design review — until v226 the front had the two lines "Traditional characters, as on the photo" and "Simplified 养乐多", H v102/v106; the pill is the Cards list's), the Cards list shows the traditional form with a "Traditional" pill and "Simplified 养乐多" on the line below, and searches both, the Edit form edits it through the strip's line. One line of ≤ 4 characters becomes a word card (`t:"Custom"`, `seg`, crop image), anything longer a sign card (`kind:"sign"`, lines joined with `\n`, `segs`, `gloss`, `img` + `shot`). Cards checked here are stored `mt:{src:"llm",verified:true}`.
   - **Second look** (H: a Maotai label in a loose frame with stripes, ribbon and neck label was read as one false character from the ribbon): **on every reading** (v96 — until v95 only when the first pass found text in under 60 % of the frame or was unsure; H: "time is no issue", and the copies read 脊 where the first pass had 疹) that text area is cut out — its vertical band from the boxes (heights are reliable) plus half a height, its **horizontal ends from the image** (`textRowExtent`: within the band, walk the columns outward from the reader's span until a gap wider than 1.3 text heights or the edge; the boxes' ends drift and the first pass may have lost a character altogether — 骑 was cut in half once, then lost altogether) plus one text height, then 1.5 text heights of plain margin in the crop's **median colour** (the reader wants margins, real ones bring the clutter back, and a corner-sampled red ribbon once framed a white label in red) — straightened on its own and read at four character heights (45/60/75/90 px, `scales`) plus native while cheap. When a tightened reading wins, the **card image** is the same area with a real photo margin of one text height, cut from the crop as framed (`cutUnrotated`: the area's corners turned back by the straightening angle, their bounding box cut — v145; until v144 it was rotated like the reading crop and carried the filled corners), not the padded reading crop. Runs silently — the reading crop is not shown (H: "run it in the background, the user doesn't have to see it"; it served to diagnose the cut-off crops of v73–v74). Not yet timed on the Xiaomi.
   - **Black-on-white and chromaticity copies** (H: 业主直租, white on red behind glass, read as 本; v95: 活菌型乳酸菌乳饮品, red print on a curved cream Yakult bottle, read as nothing — and the logo 養樂多 as 夸交多 / 一汰 / 洛相本): the reader's own thresholding gives nothing on such crops. The tight crop is always read as a **black-and-white copy** (`toBW`: Otsu on the grey image, polarity so that the majority is white — reads the glass case) and as a **chromaticity copy** (`chromaCanvas`/`toChroma`: each pixel's r/(r+g+b), g/(r+g+b), distance from the median chromaticity, Otsu on that, minority = ink — shading falls out, so the bottle's shadow no longer matters; measured on the Yakult line: grey nothing at any size, chromaticity 菌型乳酸菌乳饮品 at 96 %; on black-on-white text it is noise and simply loses) at the same sizes (45/60/75/90/110 px plus native up to 200 px). **Still weak (< 180)? The traditional-character reader** (v96, `chi_tra` from tessdata_fast, 1.7 MB, a second worker made on first need by `traWorker` from the same recipe, `makeWorker`) reads all three copies at the same sizes; its lines are converted to simplified characters (`t2s`, `vendor/t2s.txt` = OpenCC's character table, 3 222 pairs; `s2t`/`vendor/s2t.txt` the other way, 3 881 pairs, both loaded by `loadScriptTables`) and compete like any other reading. H's Yakult logo 養樂多 is traditional: the simplified model can only answer with the nearest simplified shapes (养兴多, 和准浴多), chi_tra reads 義樂多 → 义乐多; on a simplified sign it reads the traditional forms of the same characters (專業脊柱矯正 → 专业脊柱矫正), so it never hurts. The "best" (float) models were measured too and bring nothing on these crops — not shipped. When the best reading is still weak (`WEAK_READ` 180 since v96 — the logo's garbage scored 58, nothing found is 0), the **whole frame** is read as both copies by both readers at sizes from the ink itself (`inkHeight`: the longest run of ink rows in a small chromaticity copy — the old guess from the frame height read a line filling 40 % of a loose frame at 19 px). Cost: 15 passes on the tight crop always, 15 more with the traditional reader and 12 on the frame when the reading is poor (H: "time is no issue"); the Yakult crops take 3–9 s in headless Chromium, not yet timed on the Xiaomi.
   - **Readings compete and merge:** the lines of all tight readings are **merged by vertical band** (a line's band = the median centre ± half the median height of its boxes, not their extremes — inflated boxes once spanned both lines of a two-line sign and folded them into one cluster, so the first line vanished; each reading tends to get one line right and lose another; the most confident line per band is kept, short low-confidence strays dropped) and the merged result competes with the single readings; `readingScore` = mean confidence **cubed** (cf × (cf/100)², v96: real text reads at 90–97 %, the reader's garbage at 79–86 %, and a linear weight let one more character outweigh that) × n^0.35 × fragment penalty for lines under three characters (v96: /3 — /4 punished a real three-character logo against four garbage characters) × (1 − 0.5 × share of 1–2-character lines; v95: a soup of seven short lines, n = 15, once outscored the real three characters) — **both waived for a single line whose characters are as tall as the frame's ink** (median box height ≥ 0.75 × `inkHeight`; v116, H's door plate 推: ten passes read the one character at 98–99 %, it scored 18 as a "fragment" and lost to a merged soup of eight garbage characters at 34 — the chromaticity copies of a white character on a black plate are noise; a merged soup has several lines, so it is not exempt) × (0.75 + 0.5 × dictionary word coverage — a stronger coverage weight was tried in v96 and let a garbage soup with accidental two-character words win), because the model's output swings with scale even on a clean crop (measured: the same image perfect at 0.7×, garbage at 1×); at the competition a pass with **more lines than the frame can hold** is penalised quadratically (`lineFit`: `maxLines` = ⌊frame height / (0.9 × ink height)⌋, at least 1; `over` in the diagnostics — v137, H's bicycle sticker 减震单车: a whole-frame pass read the sticker's wave pattern as a second line 一一八位, the count doubled and that soup beat four passes agreeing on 减震; cutting the extra lines instead was tried first and removed a soup's fragment penalty, so a three-character wave line won — the text stays as read, only the score drops; the regression images, whose frames are tall enough for their lines, are unaffected), the score gets an **agreement bonus**, × (1 + 0.1 per other pass with the identical text, ≤ 1.5) (v96: 业主直租 from three passes lost a tie to 业主直祖 from one), and a **size check** against the frame's ink height (`inkHeight`, computed once per reading, `r.ink`/`r.frameH`; `sizeFitOf`: a pass whose median box height is under 0.5 × it is penalised quadratically — v98, from H's phone diagnostics: the tight crop, placed by a garbage first pass, read the Yakult logo as a four-line soup of 17 stroke-sized "characters" at 87 %, and the count outweighed ten passes agreeing on X乐多 / 养X多 at 90–92 %; real readings measure 0.4–2.5 × the ink height, `h` in `region.passes` — the boxes' heights swing and the ink measure is noisy, hence the low bar). **The gates use the effective score too** (`effScore`, v100 — H's granite sign 等候区: a soup of seventy fragments scored 183 raw, so neither the copies nor the traditional reader nor the whole-frame passes ever ran, five passes instead of forty). The ink height counts only **stroke-rich rows** (some ink, not solid, ≥ 6 edges — v100: the stripes of the decorated test sign made it the frame height, and the real text looked like fragments); **a dense row inside a text band still counts** (v144, H's bicycle sticker 减震单车 framed with the wave pattern above: the bold blue characters with their white outline fill 70–83 % of the middle rows of a tight frame, the band broke into slivers of 21 px on 130 px characters, so every fallback size clamped to k = 1.5, the second look never came, the frame was allowed twelve lines and the phone showed a two-line soup marked traditional — ink up to 92 % with ≥ 8 edges extends a run a text row began, trailing dense rows do not count; measured: 21 → 129 on the sticker cut, which then reads 减展单车 with 减震单车 among the alternatives, the ten regression images unchanged); the whole-frame fallback runs each **distinct scale once** (v144: with the tiny ink height all three sizes were the same pass, counted three times in the agreement bonus and in the traditional vote); it also sets the second look's sizes when the first pass's boxes disagree with it by more than 3× (garbage boxes of 15 px on a 150 px sign once left only the native scale). **Dictionary consensus** (v99, `dictConsensus`, from H's phone diagnostics: after the size check the logo's passes gave 养浴多, 次乐多, 开乐多, 养举多, 养座多 — each one character away from the dictionary's 养乐多 "Yakult", and the AI still refused): when the best reading is a single line and no dictionary word itself, and two or more distinct readings are within one character of the same dictionary entry of the same length (2–6 characters, CJK only), that entry becomes the reading and the old best joins the alternatives (`region.consensus`); a scan of the dictionary keys, ~80 ms. Only entries **of the best reading's length** count, and the best must be a supporter itself unless three readings agree (v100: 业主直租 once became 下人, a two-character word that garbage fragments circled). Measured: the phone's list → 养乐多 with five supporters, both screenshot crops → 养乐多; 业主直租 / 专业冰矫正 / the Yakult line stay untouched (no entry within reach). Inside a pass, lines of tiny characters beside a tall line are dropped (`readPass`, 0.45 × the tallest line's median box height; v96: a soup of five such lines, 18 characters, outscored the logo's three). A merged band is kept only when two passes saw it or it reads at ≥ 85 %, and when its height is at least 0.45 × the tallest band (v95: the oval of the Yakult logo became 和一一 in the colour pass and rode along). **Known limit:** two characters read as one at a small scale (专业脊柱矫正 → 专业冰矫正 at 98 %) beats the right six-character reading; the LSTM's symbol boxes are evenly spaced guesses, so a fused glyph leaves no geometric trace (a box-width penalty was tried and did nothing) — the AI fixes it from the alternatives. Measured on a striped, ribboned composite: garbage at 86 % → 本区域禁止违规 at 99 %; the 12° sign still reads both lines; H's granite sign 等候区 (yellow paint in carved characters on speckled stone) reads at 93 % once the fallbacks run (v100, 44 passes, ~30 s in headless Chromium); the Yakult screenshots read 5菌型乳酸菌乳饮品 (the faded 活 at the bottle's edge becomes a stray 5) and, since v99, 养乐多 by dictionary consensus (the passes themselves give 养兴多 / 义乐多 / 和准兴多 — 楽 is a Japanese glyph neither model knows, and the 和 is the oval's left arc). Diagnostics: `region.passes` (the competition) and `region.tightBlob` (the tight crop).
   - **Several readers at once, and a clear reading skips the copies** (v209, H: "wherever possible try to improve speed" — measured first in headless Chromium: a reading of 19–49 passes took 1.7–4.5 s, 60–75 % of it inside the passes, which ran one after another on one worker): the second look's and the whole-frame fallback's simplified passes go to a **pool of readers** (`ocrPool`/`runPasses`: the first worker plus up to two more `chi_sim` workers made on first need — one fewer than the phone's cores, at most three, only one on a phone with 3 GB or less by `navigator.deviceMemory`, since each holds the model; `poolSize`), every free worker takes the next pass and the results come back in the jobs' order, so the competition sees the same passes as before; in the whole-frame fallback the traditional reader's chain runs on its own worker at the same time. **A clear reading skips the black-and-white and chromaticity copies** (`r.clear`, the step "the reading is clear …"): when two colour passes of the second look agree on the same text of dictionary words at 95 % or more (`meanCf`, `dictCover`, at least two characters) — those copies exist for light-on-colour and shaded text, where the colour passes are not clear, and on a clean print sign they only lose. Measured: the ten regression images and the consensus case read the same (the two-pass and consensus suites pass); the clean test sign 坚果供应 drops from 19 passes to 3 (1.6 s → 0.2 s), the decorated composite 4.4 → 3.8 s and the Yakult logo 4.5 → 3.6 s in the four-core sandbox, where the pool of three shares the cores with the page — the phone's eight cores should gain more; not yet timed there. What stays: the traditional reader is one worker, the AI check is the network's time (v208 took Qwen's thinking out of it). **The copies overlap the readers and the traditional chain starts early** (v236, H: "Optimize everything for speed" — profiled first in headless Chromium with the pipeline's functions wrapped in timers: a 3 s reading spent 0.6–1.5 s on the main thread making the black-and-white and chromaticity copies, one by one at the moment a worker asked for it, so the workers waited for each copy and the main thread sat idle while they read; the traditional reader made the same copies again; and its 18-pass chain on one worker waited for every simplified pass — H's granite sign took 13.9 s): `staged(makers)` makes the first copy at once and every later one after a turn of the event loop, so a finished worker gets its result handled and its next job meanwhile; the second look caches each copy per mode and scale (`srcCache`, the traditional reader reuses them) and starts the black-and-white and chromaticity copies while the colour passes run (a clear reading throws them away, the fast path loses at most one copy); when the colour passes are already weak the traditional chain runs beside the simplified copies and its results are appended after them in the old order, so the competition sees the same passes; the whole-frame fallback stages its copies the same way; `poolSize` allows four simplified readers on a phone with 8 GB or more (three before, each ~100 MB). Measured, same passes and lines on every image: the decorated composite 3.1 → 2.4 s, the 12° sign 2.8 → 2.0 s, the Yakult logo 3.0 → 2.2 s, the granite sign 13.9 → 11.6 s (the sandbox's four cores are saturated by three readers plus the traditional worker; the phone's eight should gain more, and the fourth reader is not measurable here); the two-pass, consensus, picture and english suites pass. Also measured and left alone: boot 162 ms, the Cards list 53 ms, a 4000×3000 photo's import 115 ms, a save 10 ms, the deskew estimate 11 ms, the dictionary consensus 9 ms — nothing to gain there. What a further step could bring: a second traditional worker on an eight-core phone (its sequential chain is the long pole of a hard reading, 24 passes), not built.
   - **What the reader sees must be a JPEG** (`READ_JPEG` 0.95): this Tesseract build misreads large canvas PNGs and WebPs (same pixels: JPEG → the full line at 99 %, PNG → one character); intermediate crops stay PNG so only the last step is lossy — two JPEG generations in a row lost the line too. `deskewBlob` always hands back a JPEG; `jpegOf` re-encodes card images to JPEG 0.85 at save. The pad's small black-on-white images are indifferent (measured).
   - **The coloured ink is the snap's third candidate (v347, H's Nongfu Spring vending machine, 2026-09-08 — the card's picture cut 泉 at the right: "Here it crops the right character a bit why?"; H's "Go ahead with the color fix for the snap"):** the diagnostics said the AI's box 17–84 % across was snapped to 21–77 %, and the photo rebuilt from H's screenshot showed why — the red text stands on bright glass, so under the box's one grey Otsu cut the four characters were one blob fused with the machine's dark panel behind, while the glass showing between and inside the strokes became eight small blobs whose area beat the characters': `snapBox` took the light for the text (the v297 area rule) and its union ended inside 泉. Now the box is binarised a second way — each pixel's chromaticity against the median inside the box, Otsu on the distance, the reader's own copy for text on colour (v95) — and that mask runs through the same machinery as a third candidate (`comps[2]`, `fused[2]`, `masks[2]`, the plate rule and the fine-print filter over all three; `tc` is the mask's index, `tcg` the grey side the coloured ink mostly falls on, for the shadow pass and the band search, which cut their strips by grey). **It wins only where the grey cut found no characters:** at least two blobs of a character's size and shape (0.5–1.4 text heights tall, 0.3–1.5 wide), at least two more than the grey pick has, **and** it must reach across at least as much of the AI's box as the grey pick does — the last guard came from H's 流浪地球 poster, where the brush title is one fused blob under the grey cut (so its characters count for nothing) while the title's colour alone found pieces and dropped the credits beside it, cutting the snap from 22–88 % to 31–91 %. A grey photo has no third candidate (the mask is dropped when its ink covers under 2 % or over 60 % of the region). What it costs: one more binarisation and one more labelling pass per snap, a few tens of milliseconds. Measured on the rebuilt vending machine: the snap 19–91 % across where it was 21–77 %, so 泉 is whole and the frame 15–98 % across. Suite `verify-aiframe` (`glass`: the photo from H's screenshot with Qwen's real box, the snap's right edge past the box's own and the frame's past 85 % of the photo; the wander, plate, icons, near, far, tilt, street, carton, qr and arri cases unchanged); the autocard, picture, badpic and english suites pass. **Field-checked 2026-09-08, and the guard loosened in v349** (H at v347: "Vending machine works not yet" — the log showed the same snap as v345, 21–77 % across, so the switch had not fired on the phone's own pixels): the case rebuilt with Qwen's box from that log (19–99 % across, 7–49 % down) reproduces the phone to the percent, and the reach guard is what blocks it — the coloured ink covers 77 % of the AI's box, the grey cut 89 %, while the coloured pick holds four character-shaped blobs against the grey pick's two. The guard is a **share** since v349 (`SNAP_REACH` 0.85 of the grey pick's reach): the vending machine passes at 0.87, and H's 流浪地球 poster — the case the guard exists for — does not, at 0.61 (56 against 92 %). Measured: the snap 19–85 % across where the phone had 21–77 %, so 泉 is whole, and the frame 17–91 % where the card showed 20–82 %. The `glass` case carries the field box since (its own numbers, not the older 17–84 % answer); the aiframe suite's 32 cases and the autocard, picture and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the vending machine taken again is the check: 农夫山泉 whole in the card's picture.
- **Fixing one character** (H: "sometimes one character is wrong while the others are right"): every character of a line is a button (`charStripHTML`, low-confidence ones ringed in the tint). Tap one → picker (`openCharPick`) under the line, **two levels: suggestions, then drawing** (H: "you are mixing typing and drawing" — no type field in the picker; typing goes into the line input under the strip, in the preview and the Edit form alike). Any change drops the AI check and re-checks the line automatically.
     - **The character as it looks in the photo** is shown in the drawing sheet only (H, v89: no thumbnail in the picker's "Replace with" row — it crowded the candidates). The sheet's view starts at `charBox(sg,k,i)` — **not** at Tesseract's symbol box: those drift along a Chinese line (measured on the test sign: from the third character on, a box covers the right half of one character and the left half of the next; H saw a blank patch). The line's overall extent and the box heights are right, so the span from the first x0 to the last x1 is split evenly among the characters (signs are monospaced; a line mixing Latin or digits will be less precise) and the size comes from the median box height.
     - **Level 1, candidates:** while the AI is live it is asked at once for that position (`aiCharAlternatives`, a small separate text request; green-ringed buttons first); what the other readings saw at that position comes next (`altCharsAt`, v96), then the dictionary adds characters that form a word with the neighbours (`charCandidates`: 2–4-character words fitting around the position, longer context first, then by how common the character is, `CHARFREQ`); or **Remove** it — a red button "Remove 人" in the picker's action row (v119; until v118 a plain text link at the right that H did not find: "even when I draw one character by hand, I cannot delete the other"); **the last character of a line has no Remove** (v123, H: an empty strip leaves nothing to tap, so no way to draw). **Several characters at once** (v204, H: "after scanning, I want to remove multiple characters at once — select them first, then remove them all together", described first and built on "Go"): a soft **Select** button under the strips (`selRowHTML`, `.selrow` in `.fieldacts`, after the script switch) puts the reading into selection (`sg.sel`, the set of "k,i" positions): the hint reads "Tap the characters to remove, then Remove.", a tap on a character marks or unmarks it (filled in the tint, `.ck.on`) across all lines instead of opening the picker (an open picker closes), **and a finger drawn along the strip marks every character it passes** (v205, H: "swipe over multiple characters in select mode and select them all", described first and built on "Go": `pointerdown` on a character captures the pointer, the first character decides — starting on a marked one, the stroke unmarks —, `elementFromPoint` under the moving finger marks across the line break too, a tap is a stroke of one character; the strips carry `.selecting` with `touch-action:none` while selecting, so the stroke does not scroll, and the click handler stands back), and the row shows a red **Remove N** and **Done**; Remove is disabled while nothing or everything is marked, so the text can never be emptied. Remove (`removeSelected`) drops the positions and an emptied line with them (the per-line `orig`, `conf` and `boxes` stay aligned; a hand-edited traditional line loses the same positions, else it is re-derived), then re-checks like the picker's apply; Done leaves selection with nothing changed; typing into a line input drops the selection, since it shifts the positions. The row sits in the Read preview and in the Edit form, whose strips are the same component (`wireSlines`/`wireSel`, refreshed in place by `selRowRefresh`, no full re-render until Remove). The picker's single Remove stays. **Where the row sits (v272–v273, H's screenshots of the Edit form: "Wofür ist der Select button? … it looks like it belongs to traditional/simplified", then on a two-line card "With 2 lines, that position doesn't make sense neither"):** at the right end of the **Characters label's line** (`.labelrow` around the label and the `.selrow`, in the Read preview and the Edit form alike) — above every strip, since it acts on all lines at once; Select beside "Characters", in selection Remove N and Done on the same line (the long traditional label wraps beside them, `flex:1 1 60px`). Until v271 the row followed the script switch and read as part of it; v272 put it on the first strip's hint line, which on a two-line card read as line 1's. In the Edit form the row lives outside the `#e-lines` box, so the wiring looks in the whole field (`selScope`) and `drawLines` refreshes the row after a Remove; a card without text gets the row on the label's line with its first typed line. The trad and multiremove suites read the row from the label's line since; the savenow, edit, english, charpick and recrop suites pass; the Edit form checked by screenshot on the two-line bakery card idle and in selection, light and dark, and on the traditional card in selection. **Adding a character** (v119, back from v91 — taken out in v92 as "not convinced", asked for again on the 拉 door plate read as 人人): "+ before 人 / + after 人" links under the picker's actions (`ck-ins0`/`ck-ins1`) open the picker in insert mode (the + tile at the end of the strip of v119–v120 went on H's request in v121 — the strip shows the characters only) (`openCharPick(...,"ins")`, head "Add a character at the end / between 坚 and 果"): candidates are dictionary words that continue the neighbours (`charCandidates(line,i,true)`, a placeholder at the gap), the AI is asked for the gap (`aiCharAlternatives(line,i,true)`), or draw it (the sheet shows the whole crop, `charBox` has no box for a new character); no Remove and no insert links in insert mode. The picker closes with an **× in its top-right corner** (`.ckhead`/`.ckx`, v125 — the "close" text link at the right of the action row "looked stupid") or by tapping the highlighted character again (from insert mode that tap goes back to replacing, `box.dataset.mode`). The Edit form has the same links; its line input takes typing too.
     - **Level 2, Not here? Draw it** (when the right one is missing): a **full-screen drawing sheet** (`openDrawSheet`, `.drawsheet` fixed over everything, `body.noscroll`) with **two equal squares** (H: "symmetrical, same size as the drawing field"): the photo character on top (`attachRefView`: a 600 px square view of `sg.img` that starts at `charBox` × 1.5 and is **moved with one finger and zoomed with two** — or the mouse wheel —, photo edges padded in `--fill`; H: "why can't I just drag the image into position and enlarge it?" — the automatic crop is a guess, the hand corrects it) and the pad below (faint 田字格, pointer events); `fitRef` gives both one side = min(sheet width, half the height left after heading, status, result row and buttons), ≥ 160 px (~295 px on a 844 px viewport), refit on resize; Undo/Clear and a red **Done** at the bottom.
     - **Reading the strokes:** **the lines are guided first** (`guideStrokes`, v139, H: "there are ways to guide the lines"): every stroke is smoothed, reduced to its corners (Douglas–Peucker, tolerance 3.5 % of the character), and segments within 12° of horizontal or vertical are snapped to the axis, so the print model sees straight strokes and clean corners — measured on 16 characters drawn as jittered skeletons, three wobbles each: top-1 23 of 48 raw, 46 of 48 guided (top-5 29 → 47); diagonals and curves keep their shape. H felt no improvement on the phone (v140), and his real strokes from the diagnostics showed why: the print model recognised the box of 团 but guessed the inside (回 园 加 / 包 句 问 / 团 园). **Since v141 the strokes are matched first** (`strokeMatch`, described and built on "Go!"): the stroke medians of 9,534 characters (Make Me a Hanzi, from the Arphic fonts, `vendor/strokes.txt.gz`, loaded on first use like the reader files and listed in `OCR_FILES`; `loadStrokes`, `prepStrokes`) — every stroke scaled into the unit square with the whole character and resampled to eight points, a candidate with a stroke count within one (two from eight strokes on) scored by the **best assignment** of drawn strokes to its strokes (Hungarian method, `assignCost`; a stroke drawn backwards costs a little extra, a missing or extra stroke a fixed skip), so **H's own stroke order does not matter** (he closes the box of 团 third, the standard order closes it last); matches under cost 0.4 come first, the print model's readings after them, then the neighbour ranking as before; the diagnostics show both lists. Measured: H's three real drawings of 团 → 团 first each time (cost 0.08–0.13, ~30 ms per match after a 1.2 s load); the synthetic set 36 of 48 first (47 of 48 among five) — the skeletons there are rough. **Nothing is read until Done is tapped** — no timer (H: an inline pad below the fold went unseen, and reading after every stroke "takes it without me confirming"). On Done the strokes are rendered black on white at ~80 px and read by the same Tesseract model (`recognizeStrokes`: the model gives no per-symbol alternatives, so three readings are combined — single-word PSM 8 reads hand strokes far better than single-character PSM 10 — ranked by confidence, then what fits the neighbours first); the results appear as big buttons under the pad; tapping one replaces the character and closes the sheet; Clear to draw again. Nothing downloaded, offline, nothing leaves the device; a print-trained model on handwriting, so clean strokes matter — field accuracy unknown.
4. **Meaning priority** (H's rule): online AI → phrasebook → offline model → word gloss. `aiLive()` = key set + auto on + `navigator.onLine`. While the AI is live the offline model is not run and Save waits for the running AI check (`sg.aiPromise`); if the call fails, `sg.aiErr` is set and the offline model takes over; with neither, the card gets the gloss and `mt.pending:true` and is completed by the post-save auto run when online.
5. **After saving:** the note under the photo says "Card saved — 养乐多." plus "Translation pending." / "Flagged for review." when so (no sources, no AI — H, v105) (the **Next photo** button of v39–v93 is gone — H, v94; the camera tab's own button is enough). Photos picked from the album that are still waiting keep their CROP button below. `QSCARD[id]` remembers the card; if the AI answers later, the green suggestion box with **Accept / Dismiss** appears right there.
6. **Save now — a card before the reading is done** (v237, H: "take a photo and make a crop in a rush, hit Save and move on. App will finish everything in the background and when resources are available", described first in three lines and built on "Go"): while the bar reads "Reading the text …", a soft **Save now** button sits at the right of that line (`readingHTML` with `data-savenow` in `.readrow`, delegated in `renderShots`; it appears when the reading starts, 1.2 s after the finger lifts, and only while a frame stands). A tap makes the card at once (`saveNow`): id `reading#<timestamp>`, the crop as framed as `img` (`cropBlob(id,rect)` takes the frame as an argument since), `shot`, empty `c`/`p`/`m`, `mt` gloss-pending and **`reading:{rect,at}`** — the frame it was saved with; the frame goes, the box shows the bar with one green line "Card saved — the text follows when the reading is done." in place of the reading text (a stuck step appends "Still at: …"; `PENDING[shotId]` = the card's id; `renderShots` shows the bar, never the preview, while the shot is pending, and the photo's row offers Delete alone — no Crop, since a second frame would feed the waiting card), and H takes the next photo or leaves the tab. The reading, the second look, the picture path and the AI check run on as before (`cropSign` keeps `cardImg` and `weak` on `SIGN[id]`, and never touches `S.pendingImg` while a card is pending); at the end `finishPending` awaits the AI check, builds the card through `readingCard(id,sg)` — the former body of `saveSign` up to the fields, DOM-free, `saveSign` calls it too — copies every field but `id`, `at`, `img`, `imgFull` (the inbox's Delete may have copied the photo onto it meanwhile), `shot` and `tags` onto the placeholder, replaces `img` with the tightened cut when there is one **and drops the list's cached thumbnail** (`dropThumb`, v242 — H after a re-crop saved during the reading: "the card with a photo before the re-crop currently remains" — the Cards list had made its thumbnail from the crop saved first and kept it after the fill), drops `reading`, and **flags the card for review** (v245, H: "flag cards that were saved before the final stage, with an appropriate comment" — nobody saw the preview: the note reads "saved before the reading was done — check text, pinyin and meaning", or "saved before the reading was done, and the reading is weak — …" when `mt.suspect`, `sg.weak` = the best pass under `WEAK_READ`, or a `bad` AI answer; until v244 only the weak ones were flagged, with "check the reading") and the note under the photo becomes "Card saved — 坚果供应. Flagged for review."; a reading that finds nothing or fails (`failPending`) leaves the card empty with `reading.failed` and the flag "the reading failed — edit the card or frame the photo again". **Meanwhile** the Cards list shows the crop with "Reading …" in the text's place in the row's own size ("Nothing read" once failed; `.crow .c .lbl`), and **the detail shows the waiting card without a back** (v238, H's screenshot of a Save-now card in the detail with a bare grey "Reading …" in the reticle, a speaker for nothing and a Test button: "Please polish design"): the text box carries the reading bar with "Reading the text …" (`waitingHTML`, `.reticle .glyph .wait` — the bar of the inbox, centred at 58 % of the box, no crosshair for an empty text) and the line "The text, pinyin and meaning follow when the reading is done." under it, the head badge says "Reading …" instead of "Unverified, translation pending", no pinyin, speaker, meaning or parts row, no **Test this card** (Learn skips the card anyway), Edit, Flag and Delete stay ("Delete this card?" in the sheet, since there is no text to name); a failed reading shows "Nothing could be read." in the box, the badge "Nothing read yet" and the flag box with its note, no extra line; the "N photos" pill and the linked-cards row never count empty texts as the same text (`byText`, `sameText`). `buildQueue` skips cards without text (Learn never shows an empty card), and `aiQueue`/`aiAuto` skip them too (the failed card is flagged and pending, but has nothing to check). **Edit** on such a card shows no empty strip and no Select — the hint says "Type the text below." and the strip and the Select row appear with the first typed line (`slineHTML`'s `empty`, `selRowHTML`, the `change` handler in `wireSlines`; v238) — and takes the typed text: `applyCardUpdate` drops `reading` (from the card and from the form's copy) and abandons the background reading (`abandonReading`, `PENDING` cleared) **when the saved text differs from the card's** (v243: an edit of pinyin or meaning on a card whose re-crop is still being read lets that reading finish and fill the card; typing another text is H's decision and stops it). **The work needs the page open** (Android cuts a background page's work as it cuts its requests, v201): a card still waiting at the next start is read again 1.5 s after boot (`resumePending`, sequentially) — from the inbox photo with the saved frame, or from the crop itself when the photo is gone (`cropSign(id,{blob})`); a reading that is not on screen is allowed while a card waits for it (`cropSign`'s "no box" exit). Delete of the placeholder while it reads is harmless (`finishPending` finds no card). The usual flow — wait for the preview, then Save card — is unchanged; `saveSign` takes the picture from `sg.cardImg` (the reading's own) and falls back to `S.pendingImg`. Not yet field-checked.

### Online AI review (T3, opt-in, text only)
- **Setup (the owner's, since v190 — H: "move the AI review details into the locked area and only leave the switch for the users"):** the AI review row shows everyone the state line ("On, through the app owner's relay: DeepSeek (deepseek-chat) checks the text, Qwen (qwen3.7-plus) reads the framed area when the reading is weak." / "On: DeepSeek (deepseek-chat) checks the text. Photos never leave the phone." / "Off. DeepSeek (deepseek-chat) and Qwen (qwen3.7-plus) are set up through the app owner's relay — tick the box to check new cards." while the checkbox is off (v196, H: "it cannot say On when I've unticked the checkbox") / "Off. The app's owner sets it up under Advanced settings." — v195; v193 had tried a short line without model names, H: "I liked the previous text more, revert and polish the first paragraph"), the sentence "What is sent: the Chinese text, pinyin, meaning and your note of flagged, doubtful or pending cards. The framed area of a photo only when the reading is weak, to a provider that takes pictures. Without a key of its own this phone sends through the app owner's relay, which forwards to the provider and keeps only a count." and one checkbox **"Check every new card with the AI automatically (when online)"** (`#ai-auto`, saved at once, setting `aiAuto`; **since v193 it covers the picture too** — H: the picture went out while the check was off, "counterintuitive"; `aiAutoOn()` gates the picture path beside `pictureProvider()`); the **Set up / Settings** button and the form below render only while `S.admin` is set, like Reset and the downloads. A friend's phone has no key of its own — since v191 it uses **the owner's relay** (below). The form: More → Online AI review → a **chip row of providers** (v172, H: "several AI providers to switch between" — "I don't want to delete DeepSeek": until v171 the app held one key, and choosing another provider overwrote it; `AI_PROVIDERS`: DeepSeek default — **field-verified from the phone, no VPN** —, Qwen/Alibaba Bailian, GLM/Zhipu, Claude, Other = custom OpenAI-style base URL; the Chinese ones take WeChat Pay/Alipay, H could not buy Anthropic credit from China). **One account per provider**: setting `aiAccounts` = `{deepseek:{key,model,base}, qwen:{…}}`, `aiProvider` names the active one; `aiKey(pv)`/`aiModel(pv)`/`aiBase(pv)`/`aiAcct`, `setAiAccount`. The form shows one provider at a time (`form.dataset.pv`, the lit chip, `data-aipv`), its key, model and base from its account, and a line "DeepSeek: key saved, in use." / "Qwen (Alibaba Bailian): no key yet." (`#ai-acct`); a tap on a chip **with a key switches at once** (the row reads "On: Qwen (Alibaba Bailian), qwen3.7-plus."), a tap on one without a key only shows its empty form and the active provider keeps working; Save stores the shown provider's account and makes it active when it has a key; Remove key takes the shown provider's key only — when that was the active one, the next provider with a key takes over, without any the AI is off. **Migration** (`migrateAi` at boot): the old `aiKey`/`aiModel`/`aiBase` become the active provider's account and are deleted; a bare `aiKey` set later still counts as the active provider's key (the suites set it that way). Model defaults `deepseek-chat`, **`qwen3.7-plus`** (since v172 — Bailian labels it multimodal, the model H chose for the picture path), `glm-4-flash`, `claude-sonnet-5`; checkbox "Check every new card with the AI automatically" (default on, `aiAuto`). Keys stay in the settings store, device only, never exported, never in the usage row.
- **Qwen's thinking is switched off on every request (v208, H with Qwen as the active provider: "Check pinyin and meaning takes way too long"):** the hybrid `qwen3…` models think by default and the thinking takes many seconds before the short JSON comes; until v207 only the picture path sent `enable_thinking:false`, the text check (`aiAsk`) and the picker's candidates (`aiCharAlternatives`) did not — `noThinking(pv,model,body)` now sets it on all three OpenAI-style bodies (the relay forwards the body unchanged, so it holds there too). The cause is the code's, the timing on the phone is not measured: the AI log now carries each answered call's duration (`ms` in `logAi`, shown in Diagnostics as "→ 200 in 3.2 s"), so the next diagnostics text tells. Not yet field-checked.
- **The model's pinyin is taken only when it fits the characters (v187, H's calligraphy 志在千里 read from the picture: Qwen answered "zhì zài qiān l" twice, the ǐ lost, and the card showed it — "he forgot the i of li"):** `saneP(p,zh)` counts one vowel group per character (syllables may be joined into words, as some models write them; digits aside) and rejects any token without a vowel; on a mismatch the app's own pinyin (`pySpaced`, pinyin-pro loaded on demand) replaces the answer's, line by line. Applied to the text check (`aiAsk`, not for `bad` answers) and the picture answer (`aiReadPicture`) alike; the meaning is English and cannot be checked that way ("a thousand li" in the first answer was the model's wording for the unit 里, not a lost letter). Measured in the harness: "nài sī cān b" → the app's "nài sī cān ba"; the older mocked answers with joined syllables ("gōngyìngshāng", "zì hōngbèi") still pass, a one-letter "x" no longer does.
- **A brand name gets what it is in brackets (v280, H's 蜜雪冰城 card with the meaning "Mixue Bingcheng": "Warum ist hier die meaning nicht richtig übersetzt?" → described, "Go"):** the prompt's brand rule of v97 made "zh" the brand's name and left "m" as the bare romanised name, which tells a learner nothing. Both prompts (`aiSystem()` for the text check, the picker's candidates and Translate all; `picSystem()` for the picture path) now say: when the text is a brand, shop or product name, "m" is that name as it is known (romanised or international), followed in brackets by what it is, in the app's language — "Mixue Bingcheng (ice-cream and bubble-tea chain)", on a German phone "Mixue Bingcheng (Eis- und Bubble-Tea-Kette)" —, never the bare name alone. `saneM` accepts such a meaning in every language (Latin letters, or kana or hangul beside the name). Existing cards keep their meaning until the next AI check or Translate all; nothing else changes. The aibad, ai, addai, aikept, picture, translate and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the next brand card from H's phone is the check.
- **One meaning per line when the lines say different things (v300, H's 流浪地球 poster, 2026-09-07: the card carried four lines — the title, the credits, the names — under the one meaning "The Wandering Earth (film)": "it's not clear for the user which characters actually mean Wandering Earth" — described in three lines, "Go"):** both prompts (`aiSystem()`, `picSystem()`) say that when the text has several lines that say different things (a film poster: the title, then credits), "m" gives one short meaning per line, in the same order, joined with " / " as the pinyin is — "The Wandering Earth (film) / a film by / producer, original novel / Guo Fan, Liu Cixin" —, and lines that form one phrase keep one meaning (the two-line sticker 骑车勿盯 / 还车勿忘 stays one sentence; the model decides). Only the prompt changed: `saneM` already ignores "/", the fields, the front and the shared card show the meaning as it comes, existing cards change at their next AI check or Translate all. The aibad, ai, picture, aiframe and english suites pass (english once failed its "Processing photo" placeholder timing under five parallel suites and passed alone). **Field-confirmed 2026-09-07** (H on the 流浪地球 card: "Wondering earth works now").
- **A number keeps its unit (v323, H's Nongfu Spring label read by Qwen as 农夫山泉 / 饮用天然水 净含量, the 380ml left out with the note "not Chinese characters": "Wenn du net content erwähnst, musst du die 380ml auch noch mitnehmen!"):** the picture prompt (`picSystem()`) said "without Latin letters", and the model took the unit with them. Now it says: without lines of Latin letters (a brand's English name) and without numbers of the decoration, but a number that belongs to a Chinese line stays in that line with its unit as written (净含量380ml, 30分钟, 3月1日). Three helpers follow: `saneP` counts a number with its unit (`NUM_PART` since v343; its own `NUM` until then) as a number token, so an answer with "380ml" is no longer thrown away for a token without a vowel (the v318 card had shown "380 m l" — the app's own pinyin after that rejection); `pySpaced` glues the unit letters the pinyin library hands out one by one back onto the number (380ml, not 380 m l; a syllable is never glued, only single letters after digits); `lineMeaning` takes the Latin unit into the number's part (380ml, 20% — one part that reads as itself; a CJK unit still joins through `mergeUnits`). Suite `verify-units` (净含量380ml → the parts 净含量 and 380ml with the pinyin "jìng hán liàng 380ml", 满100减20% keeps 20%, `pySpaced` on 净含量380ml and 3月1日, `saneP` keeps an answer with 380ml and replaces one with a broken syllable beside it through the app's pinyin, which now gives 380ml too); the picture, ai and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the bottle's label taken again is the check: 净含量380ml on the card, "380ml" in the pinyin.
- **Answers are checked (v97):** every `zh` is normalised to simplified characters (`t2s`, v103 — told that the sign is traditional, the model answered with traditional `zh`, and 養樂多 has no dictionary entry, so the meaning lost "Yakult"; a traditional `zh` becomes `zht` when the model gave none; the prompt says "always simplified"); a meaning that is the Chinese text echoed, or all Han characters outside Japanese (the model once echoed the Chinese text as `m`), is dropped and logged (`saneM`, by language since v256), the fields keep the reading's own gloss; the prompt says "in English only" — in the app's language since v256 (`meaningLangName()`) and that the text is usually a sign, menu item, product name or brand — "when the readings circle around a well-known brand, zh is that name"; before calling a text bad it must try the `alt` readings and answer with the plausible one (v98 — on the phone it called the soup garbage, correctly, and left 养浴多 / 次乐多 / 开乐多 unused).
- **A clearly read character is not the AI's to change (v143):** H's bicycle sticker 减震单车 — every pass read 减 at 98 %, DeepSeek answered 共享单车 "shared bicycle", a common phrase that is not the sign, and the preview swapped the right 减 for 共 ("Zunächst wird der erste character richtig angezeigt und dann jedoch zu einem falschen geändert"). `aiSettled(sg,lines,zh)`: when the answer keeps the line count and each line's length, every changed position must be open — the reading's confidence there under `AI_SETTLED` (90), or another reading of the same line length shows the AI's character at that position (养兴多 → 养乐多 with 义乐多 among the alts, 和 → 活 with the 活菌型… alt). A settled position blocks the whole answer: the reading stays, `sg.ai={zh:c,proposed,kept}`, the fields keep the reading's own pinyin and gloss, the remark reads "The AI suggested 共享单车, but 减 was read clearly, so the reading stays. Meaning composed word by word, unverified.", Save stores the card unverified with its gloss (the post-save auto run may then offer the AI's text as a suggestion with Accept — a suggestion, not a silent replacement). A line edited by hand before the check has no confidences left and is never guarded; an answer of another length (专业冰矫正 → 专业脊柱矫正) is not guarded; the guard lives in the Read preview only (saved cards carry no confidences). Field-confirmed with v144 on the sticker (H: "OK. Works.").
- **A garbage verdict sends the picture (v302, H's 绿皮书 poster framed by hand and saved with Save now, 2026-09-07: the reader's soup 绿区十 / 月时，全网上妾 scored 196 — confident garbage with two accidental dictionary words, above `WEAK_READ` —, so no picture went to Qwen, DeepSeek called it garbage three times, once from the Edit form's button, the card kept its gloss and the flag and the Edit form said nothing — "Looks like AI check for pinyin and description doesn't work anymore?"):** `picOnBad(sg,guesses)`: when the text check answers `bad` and the picture path is open (`pictureProvider()`, the automatic check on, online) and this reading has not sent its picture yet (`sg.picAsked`; `cropSign` keeps `sg.picBlob` — the placed frame's cut when the frame stands on read text, else the straightened frame, the same picture the weak path sends), the picture goes now (Diagnostics "the text check called the reading garbage — the AI gets the picture", `region.pic` as on the weak path), and a good answer replaces the reading as the weak path's does — open characters, no boxes, the reader's texts as the alternatives, the mark on the label, no flag; a bad answer or a failed call leaves the garbage verdict as before (the red head, the flag). Once per reading; a Save-now card gets the picture's text through `finishPending`, which awaits the check. **The Edit form does the same with the card's own crop** (`recropImg||d.img` through `picOnBad`; the answer fills text, pinyin and meaning as a good check does) **and says so when even that fails:** "The AI says this text looks misread. Fix the characters, or crop the photo again." under the meaning, in six languages (until v301 a garbage verdict left the form without a word). Suite `verify-badpic` (a mocked DeepSeek that always says garbage and a mocked Qwen: the preview's reading replaced by 绿皮书 with the mark, the meaning field, the alternatives, the log line and one call each; Qwen bad → the red head, the flag and no second picture on a second check; a reading without a picture sends nothing; the Edit form filled from the crop, then Qwen bad → the line; the five translations); the aibad, ai, picture, edit, english and savenow suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the same card's Ask AI in the Edit form is the check: 绿皮书 from the crop.
- **Garbage is garbage (v95):** the prompt tells the model that the OCR text may be unreadable and asks for `bad:true` then — keep `zh`, empty `m`, say so in the note, **never replace an unreadable text with a guess** (H's Yakult logo: the AI turned 习/汶… into 了 "completed action marker" and waved 夸交多 / 一汰 / 洛相本 through as checked). A bad answer changes nothing in the Read preview: red head "This reading looks wrong — frame the text tightly and read again, or fix the characters.", the fields keep the reading's own pinyin and gloss, the review flag is pre-ticked with the note "the reading looks wrong", Save stores `mt.suspect` ("the text looks misread") and unverified; after saving, a bad suggestion shows as "AI: this text looks misread" with **⚑ Flag for review** (`aiFlag`) instead of Accept, and Accept-all flags such cards.
- **The framed area goes to the AI when the reading is weak (v173, H after 奈斯餐吧: "Send the crop to the AI when the reading is weak" — the one exception to "photos never leave the phone", under H's own switch; described first, built on "Go"):** a provider that takes pictures (`vision` in `AI_PROVIDERS`: Qwen with its multimodal model, GLM with `vmodel` `glm-4v-flash`, Claude) — `pictureProvider()` = the active provider when it takes pictures, else the first one with a key that does; `pictureModel`; setting `aiPicture` (absent = on), the checkbox "Send the framed area to the AI when the reading is weak" under the Model field, shown on the chips of picture-capable providers only (`#ai-picfield`). In `cropSign`, after the competition: when the best pass scores under `WEAK_READ` by `effScore` or nothing was found, and a picture provider is set, the automatic check is on (v193) and the phone is online, the bar says "Asking the AI about the picture …" and `aiReadPicture` sends the framed area — the placed frame's cut when the frame stands on text the reader could read, else the **whole straightened frame** the reading started from (see the frame section; never the second look's band: v173 sent `best.img`, and on H's two-line sticker 骑车勿盯 / 还车勿忘 the tightened band held the lower line only, so the AI read 还车勿忘 alone and the card image was that band; since v175 the frame goes and the card image is the crop as framed) as a JPEG of at most 800 px (`pictureJpeg`, `PIC_MAX`; never the whole photo) with up to six distinct reader texts as guesses (`picSystem()`: one JSON object `{zh,p,m,note,bad}`, simplified, the picture's lines, no Latin letters; `bad` only when no Chinese text is readable). the answer carries `box` since v293 and `boxes` since v312 — where the text and each of its lines stand in the picture — and the main text only, without fine print, see the frame section; OpenAI-style `image_url` with a data URL, `enable_thinking:false` for Qwen models starting with `qwen3` (the hybrid models think by default), Claude's `image` block with base64; the log entry carries `[picture 800×442 JPEG, 24 KB]` and never the base64; tokens and the picture itself count in the usage counters (`pics`, v178). A good answer **replaces the reading**: the AI's lines with no confidences (every character open in the picker) and no boxes (the drawing sheet shows the whole frame), the reader's texts become the alternatives, `sg.ai={…,ok:true,pic:true}` fills pinyin and meaning, nothing under the meaning, and a **small mark at the right end of the Characters label** — the green AI capsule of the Cards list (`--ok` on `--ok-soft`, radius 999, 11 px) with a filled sparkle and "AI", floated right, title "Read from the picture by the AI" (`picMark()`, `.picmark`; v180 — H: "polish the layout, place the AI icon more sexy" — until v179 a grey outline sparkle in `--label3`; v176 — H: "a small unobtrusive AI icon so that I know the picture analysis was used" — put it under the meaning, v177 moved it to the characters — H: "the icon belongs under the Chinese characters, not the translation"; v173 had the sentence "Read from the picture by the AI." under the meaning, gone in v174 — H: "not relevant to the user"), no text check follows, Save stores `mt:{src:"llm",verified:true}`. A `bad` answer keeps the reading, marks it wrong (red head, the flag with "the reading looks wrong") and skips the text check; a failed call (`r.picErr`, logged) falls through to the reading and the text check as before. A good reading never sends the picture; without a picture provider or with the switch off nothing changes. The AI row reads "Sends card text, and the framed area of a photo when the reading is weak (Qwen, qwen3.7-plus).", the "What is sent" line and About (`aboutText`, `#about-s`, live with the settings) say it, `privacy.html` has the bullet. Qwen recommended over GLM for the sign fonts; the key path is above. **Field-confirmed the same day:** the first call from H's phone failed with "no connection … refuses calls from a browser" while the VPN was on; with the VPN off, 奈斯餐吧 came back read from the picture (H: "Worked without vpn", "Yes") — so Bailian's compatible-mode endpoint does accept a browser page and `qwen3.7-plus` takes `enable_thinking:false`, and **Qwen must be called without the VPN**; a Supabase edge function as a relay was described and is not needed. GLM (`glm-4v-flash`, free) remains unverified. Measured in the harness with a mocked Qwen: 奈斯餐吧 scores 164 and goes to the picture, 业主直租 scores 174–181 and goes too (a correct reading under the gate costs one call and comes back unchanged), the two-line sign at 266 does not.
