- **The owner's relay (v191, H: "Why would a friend enter a key of their own?" → "Go with the relay"):** a phone without a key for a provider sends its OpenAI-style requests to H's Supabase edge function `ai-relay` (`relayUrl()` = `SHARE_URL + /functions/v1/ai-relay`, `RELAY_PROVIDERS` = deepseek and qwen, `viaRelay(pv)`, `relayFetch`, setting `aiRelay` absent = on): body `{provider, body}`, headers `apikey` and `Authorization: Bearer` with the publishable key and `x-install` with the installation id; the function adds H's provider key from its secrets, counts the call in the table `relay_usage` through the SQL function `relay_bump` (per phone and per day) and refuses with 429 above `CAP_PER_PHONE` 200 or `CAP_ALL` 2000 calls a day ("the daily limit of the owner's relay is reached — try again tomorrow"; 404/503 = "the owner's relay is not set up"), then returns the provider's JSON unchanged with CORS headers. **The phone's own key always wins**; `aiOn()` and `pictureProvider()` count a relay provider as available, so a fresh install has DeepSeek text checks and Qwen pictures from the first card. The three transports (`aiAsk`, `aiCharAlternatives`, `aiReadPicture`) each carry the relay branch; the status line says "…, through the app owner's relay", the form's provider line "no key on this phone — the owner's relay is used.", the What-is-sent line and `privacy.html` describe the relay (it forwards and keeps only a count; H's key never reaches the phone). The code lives in the repo: `supabase/functions/ai-relay/index.ts` (Deno, deployed by H from the dashboard's editor with "Verify JWT" off and the secrets `DEEPSEEK_KEY`, `QWEN_KEY`) and `supabase/relay.sql` (the table and the counter function, run once in the SQL Editor); neither is in the shell. **Field-confirmed the same evening:** H deployed the function from the iPad (the dashboard's editor, the name and Deploy button in the footer below the code, the JWT switch under the Settings tab as "Verify JWT with legacy secret"), the first call answered 400 "unknown provider" until the secrets were in (the function says that for a missing key too), then 焦香蜜汁肠 came back through the relay from H's phone without any key ("Looks like it worked"). Remove key on the last key had switched the automatic check off (the v172 rule for a phone without AI) while the relay still read pictures — since v192 the check goes off only when no AI is left. The sandbox cannot reach supabase.co, the relay is mocked in the harness.
- **Every AI request is tried twice (v201, H switched apps during "Checking pinyin and meaning …" and came back to "no connection (offline, or this provider refuses calls from a browser — try another provider)": Android cuts a page's requests when it goes to the background, and the text named a provider problem the user could do nothing about):** the three transports and the relay fetch through `aiFetch` — a request that fails is sent once more, after the page is back in the foreground when it went to the background meanwhile (`HIDDEN_AT` from `visibilitychange`, `whenVisible`), else after `AI_RETRY_MS` 1.5 s; the bar keeps moving meanwhile. A second failure is `AI_NET_ERR` "The AI could not be reached" everywhere — the Read preview adds ". Tap Ask AI to try again." and keeps the Ask AI button (`aiErrText`, which also turns a provider's lowercase answer into "The AI check failed: …"), the Edit form ". Tap the button to try again.", the picker and the Add form say it as before; the detail ("Failed to fetch (tried again after the app came back to the foreground)" / "(tried twice) — offline, or this provider refuses calls from a browser" / "— the API may be blocked without a VPN" for Claude) lives in the AI log only. Not yet field-checked.
- **A try that does not answer is dropped after 25 seconds (v331, H after the auto card's long reading: "he was reading quite long. why?" — described, "Go"):** `aiFetch` sends every try through `timedFetch`, an `AbortController` that aborts after `AI_TIMEOUT_MS` 25 s (a `let`, so the harness shortens it); the aborted try counts as a failure like a dead one — the v201 retry follows (after the return to the foreground when the app went to the background meanwhile, else after 1.5 s), and a second hang gives `AI_NET_ERR` with the Ask AI button as before; the AI log's detail reads "no connection: no answer within 25 s (tried twice) — the connection hangs, or the provider is slow" (`aiAsk`; the picker's and the picture's transports carry the same message without the suffix). Until v330 a hanging connection held the reading for as long as the browser waits, a minute or more, before the retry even started. What it costs: an answer that would have come after 25 s is thrown away and asked for again — Qwen's picture answers take 3–10 s on H's phone ("→ 200 in 10.1 s" in Diagnostics), so 25 leaves room; a relay that answers at 30 s costs a second call and then fails. Suite `verify-bgretry` (the timeout at 1.2 s: two hanging tries end in the sentence and the Ask AI button after about 4 s with the log's hang line; a first try that hangs and a second that answers fills the fields with two calls); the english, ai and picture suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the next reading whose AI call hangs is the check: the reader's result after half a minute instead of the bar standing.
- **Request:** OpenAI-style `POST {base}/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer` (system + user message, temperature 0), or `api.anthropic.com/v1/messages` for Claude (`anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access`). Payload per card: hanzi, pinyin, meaning, note, why (flagged / uncertain reading / pending), gloss, alt (the other readings, v96) — **never photos**. Reply = JSON array `[{c,zh,zht,p,m,note,ok,bad}]` (`zht` only for `script:"traditional"`). Cost ≈ 400 input + 100 output tokens per card, well under ¥0.01 at DeepSeek; a hundred cards a day ≈ ¥0.10–0.20.
- **Where it acts:** in the Read preview before saving (result shown in green, Save stores it verified); after saving as a suggestion stored `ai:{zh,p,m,note,ok,at,model}` with **Accept** (applies pinyin/meaning, fixes the text in place via `applyCardUpdate`, clears flag and `suspect`, `mt.src:"llm",verified:true`) or **Dismiss** — on the study back, in the card detail, under the photo, and via the **"N AI suggestions waiting — Accept all"** bar on the Cards tab; in the **Edit form** ("Ask AI to check text, pinyin and meaning" fills the fields in place, stored on Save). **Ask AI about N cards** in More sends the whole queue (`aiQueue()` = flagged + uncertain + pending) in one request; the auto run (`aiAuto`, `aiAutoSoon` 1.5 s after a save, and on `online`) does the same for pending/uncertain cards.

### Offline translation (T2, opt-in)
Firefox Translations `zh-Hans→en` v2.0a2 (Mozilla, MPL-2.0; model 44 MB + shortlist 4.7 MB + vocab 0.7 MB gzipped = 50 MB) in the Bergamot WASM engine (`vendor/nmt/translator-worker.js` + `bergamot-translator-worker.{js,wasm}`, npm `@browsermt/bergamot-translator` 0.4.9, no SharedArrayBuffer). Fetched by the **GitHub Action "Fetch zh→en translation model"** (workflow_dispatch) from **Mozilla Remote Settings** (the source Firefox uses; the Git LFS objects on GitHub are gone, 410; the build sandbox cannot reach Mozilla) — it writes `nmt-model.json` (sizes, sha256), bumps both version markers and pushes branch `nmt-model`; Actions may not open PRs here, so the PR is opened by hand. Enable under More → Translation (size prompt; files cached in `zt-ocr-v1`), setting `nmt`; used **only without a connection** once the AI is set up. The preview's translation is kept on the reading (`sg.nmt`, the lines it was made for) and Save reuses it for the same lines (v194, H: the Save button read "Translating …" — until v193 the model ran a second time at Save; the label stays for the rare case that the lines changed after the preview; a re-render of the preview no longer translates the same lines again either). Suite `verify-nmtsave` (a mocked model: one run in the preview, none at Save, the card carries the preview's meaning). `signMeaning(lines)` → `{m, src: phrasebook|nmt|gloss, pending}`; "Translate pending" in More completes gloss-only cards. Verified on the build machine (~40 ms per sentence after load), **not yet timed on the Xiaomi** — the More row shows "loaded in N s, last translation N s" (`NMT.loadMs`, `NMT.lastMs`) for that field test.

### Learning and the library
- **Learn:** SM-2 light; progress rows are `{c, interval, ease, due, reps, fails, last}` (`last` = day of the last review); every review day is appended to setting `days` (max 400) for the **streak**; `learnStats()` → cards learned / reviewed this week / streak, shown on the All-clear screen and in More → Progress — `fails` counts consecutive "again" (hard keeps it, good/easy reset it) and at `LEECH_FAILS` = 4 the card is **flagged automatically** ("failed N times in a row — check text, meaning and photo"): a leech is usually a bad card, not a bad memory. Front = photo (crop) above the text box, **both boxes the same shape on every card** (v223–v231, from H's two screenshots of a square 養樂多 beside a wide 嘉悦芭蕾: "Warum sind manche Hanzi felder quadratisch und andere rechteckig? Quadratisch nimmt zuviel vertikalen raum in anspruch. Bitte consistency!", then the design review that followed). **The card's picture is a 16:9 window around the text (v329, H: "Bitte analysiere alle bisherigen Fotos und sag mir, ob das 16:9 Format Sinn macht oder du einen besseren Vorschlag hast" — measured on the 21 real photos of the harness and this session: one-line signs and labels run 2.3–6.8:1, posters, plates and two-line signs 0.9–1.6:1, about half and half, almost nothing near 16:9 itself, so the tight crop filled the box's height or width only half and the rest was the blurred fill; a different ratio would move a tenth from one half to the other; described, "Go"):** `windowRect(rect)` takes the text frame (the layer rect, its angle) and widens it to `FRAME_RATIO` in the direction it lacks — a one-line sign gets the sign's surroundings above and below, a poster title the poster left and right —, never smaller than the frame, the same centre, shifted to stay inside the photo and clamped to the photo's size (an upright window; a turned one keeps its centre and `cropBlob` fills what lies outside the photo, v185); `windowCut(id,rect)` cuts it from the photo record, null when the photo is gone. Every save takes it: `saveSign` (the preview's Save card, from `CROP.rect`), `finishPending` (the placed frame or the saved rect, for the card made by itself and Save now), `saveNow`'s placeholder, the Edit form's Save changes (the hand-off's rect and the re-cropped frame, through the v247 window's coordinates as `photoFrame` does) and Image only from the inbox (`cropOk`); the reading's own cut (`sg.cardImg`) stays the fallback when the photo is gone (a card resumed from its crop alone). **The card's `frame` stays the text's frame** — Crop again starts from it, the AI's placement and the snap are untouched; only `img` is the window, so the text keeps its size and place in the box (it spans the same edge as before) and the surroundings fill the rest instead of the blur. Cards from before v329 keep their tight picture with the blurred fill until they are edited. What it costs: neighbouring text shows in the picture without being on the card (the pinyin under a street sign, the small line above a title). Measured in the harness: the synthetic poster's card 664×373 px for a frame of 664×125 (as wide as the frame, 16:9 tall), 邪不压正's turned frame 664×420 → the window 747×420 at −8°. The aiframe, autoframe and autocard suites compare the saved picture with `windowRect`'s width since (until v328 with the frame's); the savenow, recrop, english and picture suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the next card is the check: the box filled with the photo, the text as large as before. **A dark card picture gets its light back (v372, H's rice-cooker labels: "Some Cards really look bad because their part of the image was in the shade, etc. Can you enhance the visual quality of those cropped images?" — described in three lines, "Go for the brightening", then "Move the brightening button into the admin area. And make brightening a default process for all new Cards."):** **every card picture** goes through `cardJpeg` — the one boundary where a picture becomes a card's `img` (the finished card, Save now, Crop again's hand-off, the split panel's labels, Image only, the Add form's chosen picture; nine call sites that all encoded through `jpegOf` before) —, and there `brightenBlob` measures it and stretches it when it needs it. **What the reader and the AI see is untouched**: they cut their own copies through `cropBlob`, which never brightens, so the whole reading pipeline of v287–v349 keeps its measured behaviour. **The measure is the highlight end, not the median** (`brightLut`): the 1st and 99th percentile of the luminance (`BR_CLIP` 0.01, sampled at up to `BR_SAMPLE` 200 000 pixels), and a picture is left alone when its brightest pixels reach `BR_HI` 200 **and** its span is at least `BR_SPAN` 120. A straight stretch of that span to black and white follows — **no gamma**: a poster on a dark wall is not underexposed, and lifting its midtones would wash it out (the first cut used the median and a gamma, and 绿皮书, 流浪地球, the scooter badge and the Nongfu bottle all came out grey). The gain is capped (`BR_GAIN` 40, so at most 6×) and a span under `BR_MIN` 24 is noise and is left alone. **The stretch is per channel, which is a white balance (v375, H: "kannst du beim brightening noch einen Weissabgleich machen, damit alle bilder aus einem batch gleich aussehen?"):** the labels cut from one panel each carried their own share of the light's colour, so the seven rice-cooker cards came out in seven different tints — one cream, one grey, one yellow — though they are one photo. Each channel's own 1st and 99th percentile go to black and white, so whatever the light did to the picture is taken out and the sibling cards match by themselves; no batch, no shared measurement, each picture balanced on its own. It runs **only where the three channels are of a kind** — the widest span at most `BR_WB` 2.5 times the narrowest: a crop that is one colour through and through (red text on a red button, the green 绿皮书 cut with its spans 182/88/62) has no white in it to balance against, and stretching its empty channels would drain the colour, so it keeps the one luminance curve of v372. The gain cap is the luminance span's and the same factor goes on all three, or the cap would tilt the balance. Measured on the harness's real photos: all seven rice-cooker labels come out neutral and matching where they were cream, grey and yellow before; the green cut is byte for byte what v373 made of it; 流浪地球, the scooter badge, the Nongfu bottle, the parking sign, the street sign, the ARRI poster and the washing-machine panel are still left alone; a synthetic grey panel under a warm light ends at rgb ends 255/252/249 and red on red at 255/32/27 — the red kept. Not yet field-checked. It is **idempotent** — a stretched picture measures fine the next time. Measured on the harness's real photos: all seven rice-cooker labels come out readable (低卡饭 from a dark grey plate with darker characters to black on light grey, 保温/取消 the same), the green 绿皮书 cut and the tilted poster get a mild lift that only sharpens them (gain 2.1 and 2.4), and 流浪地球, the scooter badge 九号, the Nongfu bottle, the parking sign, the street sign, the ARRI poster, the whole washing-machine panel and all twenty of its label cuts are left exactly as they are — a black panel with white text has its highlights. **The pictures already on the phone are done by the app itself, once (v373, H: "Run the brightening over my deck now and remove the manual option completely. I thought i have to run it from the app and wanted to remove it afterwards."):** the deck lives in the phone's IndexedDB, so nobody can run it from outside — `brightenPass` starts 2.5 s after boot beside the other resumed work, walks the deck, measures every card picture and writes back the ones that were dark or flat, in chunks of `BR_BATCH` 10 (`idbPutMany`, each row rebuilt from the card **as it stands at the write**, so an edit meanwhile is not overwritten), dropping each list thumbnail; the Cards or Learn screen is drawn again at the end when anything changed. **No row, no button, no question, nothing to see.** It waits while a photo is being framed or read (`reloadBusy`, `READING`) and breathes `BR_PAUSE` 60 ms between cards, so a deck of 167 takes about half a minute of wall time and none of it is felt; the idle reload of v327 waits for it. The setting `brightPass` is written **only when the pass has been through the whole deck**, and the curve is idempotent, so an interrupted or failed pass simply runs again at the next start and re-measures what it already did. It runs on every phone, once. The owner's row of v372 — “Card pictures” with its button and its question — is gone with it; do not bring a manual button back unasked. What it costs: a decode per card picture at the first start after the update (a few milliseconds each, spread out), and one JPEG generation on each picture that is brightened. There is no undo — the pass is the app's own decision, as the line-break repair of v338 is. What it costs: a decode and a measurement per card picture at save (a few milliseconds; a picture that needs nothing is not re-encoded at all), and one more JPEG generation on a picture that is brightened. **And the picture is sharpened at the cut (v378, H's "Go" on the offer after "Any other image improvements you suggest?"):** a photo is stored at 1 600 px, so one rice-cooker label is 184 px wide in it while the card shows it at about 322 CSS pixels — two to three times its own size on the phone —, and that softness is in the pixels, where the brightening cannot reach. An unsharp mask puts the edge back (`sharpen`): the picture blurred with `SH_PASS` two 1-2-1 passes (a radius of about one pixel), the difference added back at `SH_AMOUNT` 0.8, and a difference under `SH_MIN` 3 left alone — that is the JPEG's own grain, and a panel's flat surface must not become crunchy. It runs **only on a picture under `SH_W` 800 px wide**, one the card enlarges; a big crop already has more pixels than the box and is left as it is. The stretch runs first and the sharpening after it, so the threshold reads the picture as the card will show it. Measured on the rice-cooker labels enlarged three times: 0.8 is the last amount without a light halo around the strokes (1.4 haloes and lifts the grain), and two blur passes read far better than one, since the softness spans three or four pixels; the 773 px 绿皮书 cut at its own size comes out a touch crisper with no halo, and the whole photos over 800 px are untouched. What it costs: 9–17 ms on a label cut, 42 ms on a poster cut. **It is not idempotent** — sharpening twice is visible —, so it happens only where a picture is cut fresh from the photo (`cardJpeg`, `brightenBlob(blob,true)`), never in the deck pass of v373 (`brightenBlob(d.img)`, no flag): the cards already on the phone keep their look until they are cut again. Not yet field-checked. Suite `verify-bright` (`brightLut` on three synthetic pictures — the shade stretched, a lively one and a poster on a dark wall left alone; the white balance on a warm-lit grey panel and on red on red; the unsharp mask raising a soft picture's edge energy by a fifth, a 900 px one left alone, and the cut's path sharper than the deck pass's on the same blob; no row and no button anywhere, unlocked and on More; the pass running by itself at the next start with no sheet and nothing on screen, one of three cards brightened and the other two's bytes unchanged; the stretched picture across a reload and no second pass; a card saved through the Add form carrying a brightened picture); the english, admin, savenow, autocard, cards, reload and order suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's next photo in the shade is the check, and one run over the deck for the cards that are already there.
- **The photo box** (`.picbox`): a fixed 16:9 box at the card's inner width on `--card2` with the card radius and the text box's hairline edge (`box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--sep)`), the crop fitted inside (`object-fit:contain`); behind it a **blurred copy of the crop** (`.picbg`, `object-fit:cover`, blur 24 px, saturation 55 %, brightness 85 %, opacity 55 % over the card's grey, enlarged by 20 % so the blur's edges never show, hidden from the accessibility tree) fills the room beside a tall crop in a **subdued hint of the photo's colours** — a 16:9 crop covers it entirely (H's steps: a flat grey fill v224, desaturated blur at 45 % v229 "washed a red crop to pink", the colours at full strength v230 "match the soft blurred fill with the color of the photo", darkened to 65 % "how it's usually done" v231, then "Mach die Hintergründe bitte dezenter" v234 — the current values; a fill in the card's grey was built on a misread and dropped unmerged). The whole-photo tap keeps the photo's own shape, the linked card's peek uses the box too, the sign card's picture sits in the same box. **Rejected: the photo edge to edge across the card** (v228, the review's item 5 — the box past the card's padding and up to its top edge on Learn; reverted in v229, H: "Nein, Bild in der Box gefiel mir besser" — do not bring it back unasked). **The text box** (`frontBox`): the wide box at the card's width, `FRONT_H` 150 px tall for one photo line, the font capped at `FRONT_FS` 72 px so a short word fills it without growing it (1–3 characters 72 px, 4–8 the usual 58, longer fitted to the width), a second photo line adds its height; until v222 up to three characters sat in a 260 px square (see **Line breaks** and Design). No tag row, no Reveal button — **tapping the character toggles the back on and off; tapping the photo swaps the crop for the whole photo and back** (`S.fullPic`, `frontPic`, reset on the next card). **The tap hints show only while the app is new** (v226, H's "Go" on the design review: "Tap the character to reveal, or the photo for the whole picture." under every card forever is noise): `showHints()` = fewer than `HINT_REVIEWS` 20 reviews all time in the usage counter; the Learn front's hint and the detail's two hints ("… to show / hide the answer …") go together, the card gets shorter, nothing else changes. Back = pinyin with a **speaker button** (the phone's own zh-CN voice via `speechSynthesis`, nothing downloaded; shown whenever the phone can speak at all — v164, H: "the speaker icon doesn't appear on my Android phone": until v163 it showed only with a Chinese voice found, and Android hands the voice list over late and sometimes in two parts, so a "no voice" answer stuck for the session; now `ttsVoice()` looks again on every tap until a voice is found, a tap without one still speaks with lang zh-CN, and the hint "No Chinese voice on this phone — add one under Settings, Text-to-speech output." (`#say-hint`) comes **only when speaking fails** — the utterance's error, or neither start nor error within three seconds — never from the voice list: **H's Xiaomi reports no voices at all and speaks Chinese anyway** (v165, diagnostics "voices (0): none reported", H: "Sound works now, but app shows [the hint]. This is wrong"); Diagnostics lists the voices the phone reports, `voiceList`), meaning, a **parts row** — the card's dictionary words as buttons (from `gloss`/`segs`/`seg`; a single-word card shows its characters instead), tap one for pinyin and meaning, a multi-character word then offers its characters as a sub-row (`cardParts`/`charsHTML`/`charInfo`; **the dictionary and the pinyin library load in the background the moment a back is shown**, in Learn and in the detail — `warmParts` on the revealed render, v235, H's "Go" after "Why loading the dictionary?": until v234 the first word tap of a session waited a second or two while the 2.5 MB `cedict.tsv.gz` parsed, and the line "Loading the dictionary …" read like a network wait — it never was: the file sits in the reader's cache after the first reading or the download under More → Text recognition; the tap still loads on demand when the warm-up did not run, and the loading line shows only when the files are not ready yet; CC-CEDICT clutter like `[Tian1 jin1 shi4]` and `CL:` stripped by `cleanSense`). **A number and its unit are one part (v309, H's 绿皮书 card with the line 3月1日 全国上映, 2026-09-07: the row showed 月 and 日 alone, the tap on 日 said "abbr. for 日本, Japan" — "Bitte context beachten!! 日 muss zusammen mit der 1 also Datum/Tag erkannt werden!"):** `lineMeaning` kept a number as its own token and let the dictionary cut the unit after it as a word of its own. Now `mergeUnits` joins a number with a lone unit character after it — `UNIT_WORDS` 年 月 日 号 点 时 元 块 层 楼 岁 米 人 位 折 — into one part with a meaning read from the pair (`unitMeaning`: 3月 March, 1日 "the 1st (day of the month)" after a month and "3 days, or the 3rd" without one, 5号 the same after a month and "No. 5" otherwise, 2026年 "the year 2026" and 3年 "3 years", 3点 "3 o'clock", 10元 "10 yuan", 3楼 "3rd floor", 2人 "2 people", 8折 "20 % off"; the pinyin "3 yuè"); a dictionary word that starts with the unit (月份, 人人 in 1人人们) is a word, not a unit, and stays apart; a Latin unit after the number (380ml, 20%) is one part with it since v323. The gloss, the segs, the composed meaning ("3月 March · 1日 the 1st (day of the month) · …") and the Add form's autofill carry the merged part; the parts row and its tap read the gloss through `cardGloss`, which merges at display time, so cards saved before v309 show 3月 and 1日 too; a merged part offers its unit character as the sub-row (日 alone → "sun"). And `bestSense` skips an "abbr. for …" sense as it skips surnames and variants — CC-CEDICT's 日 opens with the abbreviation for Japan before "sun; day". Suite `verify-units` (the date line's gloss, meanings, pinyin and segs, ten other pairs, the row of a fresh card and of a card with the old stored gloss, the tap on 1日 and on 日 alone; screenshot `units-parts`); the cards, english, addai, sign-card and twopass suites pass. Not yet field-checked — the 绿皮书 card's back after the update is the check: 3月 and 1日 in the row. **A number with its unit is a part of the row (v336, H's 24H存包 card, 2026-09-08 — the row showed 进口 食品 折扣 店 存 包 and no 24H: "das 24H sollte auch in der Zeile bei den chinesischen Schriftzeichen dabei sein, da es zur Bedeutung dazugehört"):** `cardParts` kept only parts with a Chinese character, so the number part `lineMeaning` had cut (24H, 380ml, 20% — v323) fell out of the row at display time, though the gloss carried it. Now a part matching `NUM_PART` (a number, a point, up to four Latin letters or % — three until v343) stays in its place — 24H before 存 —, and its tap shows the number once with "A number, read as it is." (six languages) instead of the number as its own pinyin and meaning, no sub-row. Cards saved before v336 show it too, since the filter was the row's. **And the tap says what the unit means (v337, H: "24H is not only a number, it means 24 hours"):** `latinUnitMeaning(w)` — `LATIN_UNITS` maps h/hr/hrs, min, s/sec, ml, l, g, mg, kg, km, m, cm, mm, %, kcal, w, kw, v, mah (case-insensitive) to their words, singular for 1, through `t()` in six languages — gives "24 hours", "380 millilitres", "20 percent", "24 Stunden" on a German phone, "380 ミリリットル" on a Japanese one; a bare number or an unknown unit keeps "A number, read as it is." The composed word-by-word meaning still shows the number as written. Suite `verify-units` (the tap's line "24H24 hours", the helper on nine inputs in three languages); the english suite passes. **Field-confirmed 2026-09-08** (H, on the 24H存包 card at v337: "Looks all good"). Suite `verify-units` (the 24H card: the row with 24H before 存, the tap's line with the number once and the sentence, no sub-row; screenshot `units-24h`); the cards, detail and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's 24H存包 card after the update is the check. No word or gloss tables on the back (H: redundant) —, (context word), **the other cards with the same text** (v122, H: "if one character connects to various photos, then link them" — `linkedHTML`/`sameText`: a row "Also on another photo / N other photos" with their crops, newest first, `wireLinks`; on the study back and in the card detail alike — in the card detail a tap opens that card's detail; **the row is one line in the Cards list's box look since v345** (H, after the v344 polish: "Ideas how to improve 'on x other photos' as well?" — four ideas offered, "Go with 1"): the thumbs wrapped, so four photos broke 3 + 1 under the centred label; now the row is one line that scrolls sideways as the tag chips do (`flex-wrap:nowrap`, `overflow-x:auto`, no scrollbar, `justify-content:safe center` while it fits), and each tile carries the list's box look — the crop fitted with a subdued blurred copy behind it (`img.thumbbg` beside `img.thumb`, the v232/v234 values) instead of the crop sitting small on grey. The tile is 74×44 (78×50 until then), so four fit the card at 390 px and the touch target keeps its 44 px; a fifth scrolls. The v328 override `.shot .result .thumbs img{width:auto}` went with it — the linked row sizes its own two pictures now. **The three ideas not taken** (do not build them unasked): a status or date under each thumbnail, Learn showing one card of a set of duplicates and skipping the rest, and one card holding several photos — the last is a deck format change, and v118 decided the opposite, that the same text from another photo becomes its own card. Suite `verify-link` (the row's nowrap and auto overflow, one `img.thumb` and one `img.thumbbg` per other card); the detail, autocard and english suites pass; checked by screenshot on a seeded 农夫山泉 with four other photos in light, dark and on the Learn back with the peek ring. Not yet field-checked; **in Learn a tap shows that photo on the card in place** and a second tap returns (`S.peek`, the thumb ringed in the tint, reset on the next card; v155, H: "when tapping an 'also on another photo' card, I'm getting out of the learn mode. That should not happen" — until v154 the tap left the session for the Cards tab)), then **Again / Hard / Good / Easy** with interval preview (after grading the next card appears at the top of the screen), and underneath **⚑ Flag for review** and **✎ Edit** (`S.editFrom="study"`, Back/Save return to the revealed card). No example sentences (H's decision). Empty deck → "No cards yet" + Take a photo.
- **Line breaks — the card text follows the photo, automatically (H's rule):** the front never wraps on its own; every word card gets the wide box and the font is fitted to the longest line (`frontLines`, `frontBox`; the 260 px square for up to three characters went in v223). **A number stays in the word card's lines (v338, H's 24小时营业, 2026-09-08 — the front showed 小时营业 while the pinyin and the parts row had the 24: "Hier fehlt das 24 in der 2. Zeile"):** the front draws a word card's lines from `seg`, and two places built `seg` from Chinese characters alone — `readingCard` filtered the reading's parts by `CJK`, and `segWithBreaks` (the Edit form's rebuild) walked the line's Chinese characters only. Now `readingCard` keeps a part matching `NUM_PART` too, and `segWithBreaks` cuts a number with its Latin unit (`NUM_TOKEN`) as a word of its own between the dictionary words — `["24","小时","营业"]`, `["净含量","380ml"]`, `["3","月","1","日","全国","上映"]` (the CJK unit joins at display through `mergeUnits`). **Cards from before v338 are repaired once at boot** (`fixNumberSegs`): a word card whose text has more digits than its `seg` gets its lines rebuilt — the old lines' Chinese characters keep their line, the numbers between them follow the text's order — after the dictionary is loaded (without it the next start repairs), written in one transaction. Suite `verify-units` (a seeded 24小时营业 with `seg` ["小时","营业"] repaired to ["24","小时","营业"] in memory and in IndexedDB with the front reading 24小时营业, a two-line 2人3日 keeping its lines, `segWithBreaks` on three lines); the english, edit, cards and wrap suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's 24小时营业 card after the update is the check: 24 in the box. **The line's width counts digits and spaces (v339, H's 绿皮书 sign card, 2026-09-08 — the second line 3月1日 全国上映 ran past the text box: "warum geht hier das 映 über die graue Box hinaus?"):** `fitLines` sized the font by `glyphs`, the count of Chinese characters, so that line measured six wide while the digits and the space make it about eight. Now `lineUnits(s)` measures a line in character widths — a Chinese character 1, a digit or a Latin letter 0.6, a space 0.35, other punctuation 0.5 — and `fitLines` fits the font and picks the longest line by it; the sign card's font cap and `headFont` keep the character count. Suite `verify-units` (the 绿皮书 sign card at 390 px: both lines inside the box, the helper's count 7.55; screenshot `units-fit`); the wrap, box, english and sign-card suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's 绿皮书 card after the update is the check: 映 inside the box. **One exception (v152, H's 16-character escalator sign ran past the card: "statt überlaufende Zeile bitte zweizeilig"):** a photo line too long for the box is shown on two (or more) lines, cut nearest its middle between the card's words (`fitLines`, words from `seg` via `frontWords` or `segs`), while the fitted font would fall under 30 px in the reticle or 22 px in a sign card's text — display only, the stored lines stay; and the box width is measured from the card (`frontWidth`: main's inner width capped at 440, minus the card's padding — until v151 it was guessed from the window and overflowed the card on the phone). Where the lines come from: the reader keeps Tesseract's lines (sign cards: `c` with `\n`; word cards: a `"\n"` token in `seg`); the AI's answer is re-cut to those lines when the model drops the `\n` (`recutLines`: old break offsets, so a corrected character keeps them and an added one only lengthens the last line); a rename keeps them the same way (`segWithBreaks`); the Edit form shows word cards with one strip and one line input per photo line (the hint sentence about it was confusing and is gone). Cards without any line data (before v39) get their lines **estimated once at boot** from the crop's shape (`autoBreaks`: L ≈ √(n·h/w), cut at word boundaries, stored with `lb:"auto"`); cards with real line data are `lb:"photo"` and never touched.
- **Wording and labels** (the consistency passes of v126 and v134, H: "check for consistency throughout the app", "check the app for consistency and professionalism"): every button label in sentence case (Crop / Delete / Cancel in the inbox, Cancel in the preview, Crop / Whole photo in the Add form, "Delete card" in the detail as in the Edit form), the flag checkbox reads "⚑ Flag for review …" everywhere and its note field shows only while the flag is on (the Edit form too since v136), every More row is a full sentence in sentence case with no `·` and no "OCR" ("Progress and cards as one file, via the share sheet.", "Not persistent yet. Install the app so the system keeps the data.", "On: DeepSeek, deepseek-chat. Sends card text only, never photos.", the Review queue says "N cards waiting: …" and its button just "Ask AI", the translation button just "Download", a section "Share" with Share the app and Feedback opens More (v211–v212), the sections are ordered for the learner since v257 (see More in the learner's order), About reads "PWA vN. Works offline. Cards and photos stay on this phone; anonymous usage counts go to the app's owner." since v170), the done screen's button is "Pull the next cards forward", pinyin fields use the UI font (no `mono` — the design rule), the list's "Traditional" and "N photos" pills sit on the pinyin line so the text keeps its room, the "27 of 27" count never wraps, the linked-photos row is centred on the card, and `cleanSense` also strips "(Taiwan pr. …)" and is applied to the composed word-by-word meaning too. Since v134 also: the study hints read "Tap the character to reveal, or the photo for the whole picture." (no `·`), the back's flag action is "⚑ Clear flag" / "⚑ Flag for review" like the detail, the status pills and badges are in sentence case ("New", "Review", "Unverified, translation pending", "Not studied yet.", the SRS line as a sentence), the search placeholder is "Search", the More section is "Start over", the Edit badge "Edit", the preview's fields sit on the photo card's secondary surface like the form's and its Tags field follows the flag as in the Add and Edit forms, the list's pills (Traditional, N photos, tags) have their own small wrapping line under the pinyin, and an AI failure in the picker says "The AI could not be reached." instead of the browser's error text.
- **Questions are the app's own sheet, not the browser's dialog** (v222, H on the card's Delete: the browser's box said "henglicam.github.io says" — "Gehts auch etwas professioneller?", then "Und polish das box layout"; described first and built on "Go"): `askSheet({title,text,ok,danger})` shows a dimmed backdrop and a card that slides up from the bottom (`.ask`, `.sheet`, `askup` 180 ms) with a bold title (19 px), one line of consequence in `--label2` (15 px), a neutral Cancel on `--fill` (`.btn.plain`) and the action button named for what it does — red on soft for a deletion, the filled tint for the import (`danger:false`); Cancel, the backdrop or Escape answer no, the promise resolves true or false. The questions: the card's Delete in the detail and the Edit form ("Delete “学”?" / "The card and its learning progress will be removed." / Delete — **until v267; since v268 the card is deleted at once with Undo**, see above), Photos → Delete N ("Delete 3 old photos?" / "The cards keep their own picture." / Delete), the import ("Import 70 cards and 53 progress entries?" / "Existing entries of the same cards will be overwritten." / Import — `nOf` takes an irregular plural since), Reset ("Start over?" / "All progress, cards and inbox photos on this phone will be deleted." / Delete everything). The plain notices (the clipboard fallbacks, the import's sentence, the errors) were browser alerts until v265 and are the same sheet with OK alone since v266 (`noteSheet`). Suites that go through a delete, an import or the cleanup answer the sheet by overriding `askSheet` with `async () => true` (it is a top-level function, so assignable from the page).
- **Tags** (v133, H: "make the cards sortable, for Chinese class, HSK …" — grouping by own labels, described first and built on "go"): a card carries `tags` (an array of free labels, several allowed; absent when none). A **Tags** field on the Read preview (`stags-<id>`, kept in `sg.tags`), the Add form (`f-tags`, in the draft) and the Edit form (`e-tags`): an input for typing (comma-separated, `parseTags`) plus the labels already in use as chips that toggle (`tagsFieldHTML`/`wireTags`/`allTags`). The Cards tab shows a chip per tag next to Flagged / AI / Unverified (`S.filterTag`, one at a time), the rows show their tags as tinted pills on the pinyin line, and the search finds tags. The Learn tab shows a chip row above the card and the done screen — "All cards" plus one per tag (`learnChipsHTML`/`wireLearnChips`, setting `learnTag`, persisted): `buildQueue` takes only that tag's cards. **Untagged** (v156, H: "provide a default tag for not-tagged cards", described first and built on "Go"): a group, not a label — while tags exist and some cards carry none, the Cards tab shows a chip "Untagged (N)" after the tag chips and the Learn tab a chip "Untagged" after the tags (`UNTAGGED` sentinel in `S.filterTag` / setting `learnTag`, `hasTag`, `untaggedCount`); a card that gets a tag leaves the group by itself, exports are untouched. **The filter is one pill and a sheet (v365, H on the eight kind tags of v364: "Now with so many tags, we'll probably need a separate tags page or something like that. please make good suggestions." — four ways offered (order the chips by use; the pill with a sheet; a Tags page under More with rename, delete and a star; the kinds out of the chip rows), H: "I like B most. Any sexy and common suggestion?" → described the way Mail and Photos do it, "Go"):** the chip row that had to be swiped sideways is **one pill** — the sliders glyph (`filterIcon`, `stroke:currentColor`) and the filter's own name, `filterPillHTML(scope)`: "All cards" plain while nothing is set, the filter's label with its count lit in the tint while one is ("Appliance (23)"), `t("Filters ({0})")` while several are — and the whole list lives in **the app's own sheet** (`openFilterSheet`, the `.ask` backdrop and the `askup` slide of v222 with a `.sheet.filter`): the head "Filter" with **Clear** at the right while a filter is on, then the groups `filterGroups(scope)` gives — **Status** (⚑ Flagged, AI while suggestions wait, Unverified) and **Tags** (every tag with its card count, Untagged last) on the Cards tab, **Tags** alone with "All cards" first on Learn —, each row its label at the left, its count in grey at the right and a tint check when it is on (`.frow`, hairlines between, the group heads in `--label2`, the list scrolling inside the sheet at `max-height:78vh`), and Cancel below. A tap on a row runs `setFilter` and closes the sheet: the three status filters stay independent toggles, the tag is one at a time, Clear drops all four; the backdrop and Escape close it and change nothing. Learn's pill sits where its chip row sat (`.learnchips`, the top edge of v158) and shows only while tags exist. What it costs: changing a filter is two taps instead of one, and a tag used constantly is no longer one tap away (the fallback offered with it — the two most-used tags kept as chips beside the pill, as Photos does on a wide screen — was not asked for). The keys "⚑ Flagged ({0})", "AI ({0})", "Unverified ({0})" and "Untagged ({0})" became "⚑ Flagged", "Unverified" and "Untagged" in six columns (the count is the row's own now), and "Filter", "Status" and "Filters ({0})" came in six. Suite `verify-filter` (the pill's three states with its glyph, the row on one line that does not scroll; the sheet's title, groups, counts and order; a tag row filtering the list, naming the pill and closing the sheet; the tick; Clear; a status row and a second filter beside it with "Filters (2)"; the backdrop and Escape changing nothing; Learn's pill, its sheet with All cards first, a tag studied and All cards back; the German pill, heads and rows); the sheet checked by screenshot in light and dark, the pill and the Learn screen in light; the tags, untagged, aifilter, flag, ai, sign-card, kind, pick, cards and english suites read the sheet's rows since and pass. Not yet field-checked — H's next tap on the pill is the check. **Several rows at once (v366, H: "Bitte beim filtern multiple choice zulassen"):** a tap ticks or unticks its row and **the sheet stays open** — the list behind is filtered at once (`after` is `render()`, which only rewrites `#main`) and the sheet's rows are refreshed in place (`sync`: the `on` classes, the counts, and Clear appearing or going), so nothing slides or scrolls away; the button below is **Done**, and the backdrop and Escape close it as before. **The rule: within a group any ticked row counts, between the groups all must** — the three status rows are an OR of each other now (Flagged or Unverified; they were an AND until v365, and nothing relied on it), the tags are an OR (`S.filterTags`, an array in place of the single `S.filterTag`; a card in any of them shows), and a status pick and a tag pick narrow each other. **Learn takes several tags too** (`learnTags()`, setting `learnTag` an array since — a phone that stored the single string keeps working, and "All cards" clears the list); `buildQueue` and `learnStats` take a card in any of the picked tags. The pill reads the one filter's label while one is on, `t("Filters ({0})")` while several are; Flagged cards → Show clears the tags with the other filters. Suite `verify-filter` (a second tag ticked with both rows lit and the list at two, the same tag tapped again unticking it, Done closing the sheet, Clear leaving it open with nothing ticked, two status rows giving their union and a tag narrowing that to one, "Filters (3)", the backdrop and Escape, Learn with two tags and its queue from both); the tags, untagged, aifilter, kind, pick, flag, cards, english, order, progress, sign-card, ai and detail suites pass; the multi-pick sheet checked by screenshot in light. Tags travel with the export and import like any field. **A card from a photo tags itself with what the photo is (v364, H: "Could you automatically label the Cards? Would that make sense?" — three ways offered, H: "1 now, 3 straight after it", then "Go"):** `KINDS` — Menu, Street sign, Shop, Product, Appliance, Transport, Office, Notice, and App since v367 — is one word the AI names inside the answer it already gives (`"kind"` in `picSystem()` and in `aiSystem()`, each with its examples; no extra call, one word of tokens), and `kindTag(k)` turns it into an ordinary tag **in the app's language** (`t("kind:Menu")`, the eight words in six columns of `lang.js`; a German phone tags "Gerät", and a language switched later leaves the old tags as they are — a tag is a free label, not a translated string). `readingCard` appends it to the card's `tags` after H's own, so it shows as a pill on the card, gives the Cards list a chip and Learn a chip, is found by the search, travels with the export and can be renamed or deleted in any tags field. Every label of a split panel gets the photo's kind (`splitCards` passes `sg.ai` on). Nothing is tagged when the model answers "" or a word outside the eight, or when it called the reading garbage (`sg.ai.bad`) — no tag rather than a wrong one. **The kind is asked for even when the answer lists labels (v377, H: "warum ist der Reiskocher nicht als Appliance getaggt?"):** the model left `"kind"` out of every panel answer — H's washing-machine reply carried `zh`, `p`, `m`, `note`, `box`, `apart` and `labels` and no `kind`, while the 三立方咖啡 sign, whose answer has no labels, came back with `"kind":"Shop"` —, so **no card of a split panel has ever been tagged**. `picSystem()` says it now: always give "kind", also when "apart" is true and you list "labels" — the kind is the picture's, not one label's. The cards already on the phone are the work of **Tag all cards** (v368), which asks from the text alone. Suite `verify-kind` (the sentence in the prompt, once); the panel, picture and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's next panel is the check. A card typed by hand gets none (the Add form's autofill ignores the field), and a waiting AI suggestion's kind is not applied on Accept. Suite `verify-kind` (a panel's five cards each tagged Appliance with the Cards and Learn chips, the chip narrowing the list to five, the tag in the Edit form's tags field; one card from a picture answer tagged Menu; an empty kind, a word outside the eight, a missing field and a bad answer each leaving the card untagged; the German "Gerät"; `aiAsk` handing the kind back from the text check; `kindTag` on five inputs; the eight words distinct in all six languages); the panel, autocard, cards, tags and guide suites pass. The guide's Cards section names it. Not yet field-checked — H's next photo is the check. **Tag all cards (v368, the third of the three ways offered at v364 — H: "1 now, 3 straight after it", then "Tag all cards"):** the kind of v364 rides on a new card's own AI answer, so the deck H already had stayed untagged. More → Learning → a row **Tags** under Card order — "N cards carry no tag yet." with the button **Tag all cards** (`tagRowHTML`, `#tag-all`, shown only while such cards exist and an AI is set up) — asks the AI for the kind of every card that carries **no tag at all**; a card with a tag of H's own is never touched, and neither is its text, pinyin or meaning (`aiCardPayload`'s `tagOnly`: "name \"kind\" for this card and nothing else; keep zh, p and m exactly as given"). It works like Translate all in every way: the run's state lives in `TAGALL`, not in the row (v257's rule), batches of `TAG_BATCH` **10** (the answer is one word a card, where the translation takes five), the moving bar with the count of the card the AI is on, every answer **staged** in setting `tagStage` `{m:{id:kind}}` and **every card written together** in one transaction at the end (`applyTagStage`, `idbPutMany`) — so an interruption changes nothing —, the run remembered in setting `tagRun` until no card is left (`rememberTagRun`) and picked up again 2.5 s after boot, on `online` and on foreground (`resumeTagAll`), asking only for the cards not staged yet. A failed call shows "The AI could not be reached. 0 tagged, 21 left. It goes on by itself when the AI can be reached again." and keeps the button; offline it says so; the finished line reads "Done — 21 cards tagged." and the button goes. A `bad` answer or a word outside the eight (nine since v367) stages `skip` — no tag rather than a wrong one. A run holds the idle reload of v327 as a translation does. Five keys in six languages ("Tag all cards", "Tagging {0} of {1} …", "{0} tagged, {1} left.", "Done — {0} tagged.", "{0} carry no tag yet."), and the guide's Cards paragraph names the row (and says Learn shows the tags you pick, the v366 rule). What it costs: one AI run over the untagged cards — about three calls for 21 cards, seventeen for 167 —, which count against the relay's daily limit on a phone without a key. Suite `verify-tagall` (a deck of 21 untagged cards and two with H's own tags: the row's line and its place under Learning, the running line with the count and the disabled button, three calls of 10/10/1 with the tagOnly payload, all 21 tagged and H's two untouched, the texts unchanged, the stage and the run gone, the tags across a reload and the row gone with them; a cut connection leaving no card tagged with the stage empty and the run kept, the line naming the resume; the reconnect finishing it in three calls for 21 cards; the German row); the translate, english, order, kind, guide and reload suites pass (the translate suite's own checks pass — its six console lines are the aborted calls of its dead-call case, there on v367 too); the row checked by screenshot. Not yet field-checked — H's deck after one run is the check. **Undo last run (v369, H after v368: "And maybe Tag all cards should be a general AI re-run on all Cards? Because ai models get better over time? And then there should be an 'undo last run' option as well… Any suggestions?" → three ways offered — the re-run as suggestions, the re-run that writes with an undo, the undo on its own — and my recommendation "C first, then A" for one reason: on a saved card the v143 guard is gone (no per-character confidences), so a blanket re-run that writes would silently rewrite correct cards; H: "Your recommendation?", then "Go" on the three-line description of C):** a run that writes many cards at once keeps what it replaced in setting `lastRun` `{kind, keys, n, at, m:{id:{field…}}}` (`saveLastRun` at the end of `applyStage` and `applyTagStage`, the fields that run touched and nothing else — `m`/`ml`/`ms` for Translate all, `tags` for Tag all; a field the card did not have is stored as absent and the undo deletes it again), and the row it belongs to carries **Undo last run** under it — "Tagged 21 cards at 14:32." / "Translated 43 cards at 14:32." (`undoRunHTML(kind)`, under the Tags row in Learning or the Meanings row in Language, `runWhen` giving the time in the language's locale, the day and time when the run was not today) — until the next run replaces the store. **Undo** (`undoLastRun`) puts every card back in one transaction (`idbPutMany`), drops the store and the finished lines (`TRANSLATE`, `TAGALL` cleared), and the row goes with the line "Undone — 21 cards put back."; the store survives a reload, so the undo is there at the next start. **One run deep on purpose** — the run before it is gone. A finished run now re-renders More (the mode tested beside study and cards at the end of `translateAll` and `tagAll`), so the Undo row appears without leaving the page. Four keys in six languages ("Undo last run", "Tagged {0} at {1}.", "Translated {0} at {1}.", "Undone — {0} put back."). Suite `verify-tagall` (the row after a run with its line, kind, keys and count, the same line after a reload, Undo putting all 21 back with H's own two untouched, the store and the row gone and Tag all cards offered again, the tags still gone after a reload, the German row and title) and suite `verify-translate` (the row after the first run with keys m,ml,ms, Undo giving every card its English meaning back with no `ml`, and Translate all running again after it); the english, order, guide, kind, reload, progress and admin suites pass. **Check all cards again (v370, option A of v369 — H: "Go"):** More → Learning → a row **Check all cards again** under Tags — “Checks every card again. You see each change before you accept it.” with the button of the same name (`recheckRowHTML`, `#recheck-all`, shown while the deck holds a card with text and an AI is set up) — sends every card (`toRecheck`: text, no waiting reading) to the text check in batches of `RECHECK_BATCH` **5**, and **an answer that differs becomes the card's waiting AI suggestion** (`ai:{…}` as the auto run's does), never a write: the green box with **Accept** / **Dismiss** on the study back, in the detail and under the photo, and the bar “N AI suggestions waiting — Accept all” on the Cards tab. That is the difference from Translate all and Tag all, which write: on a saved card the v143 guard cannot run (the per-character confidences live only in the Read preview), so a run that wrote would silently rewrite characters every pass had read clearly — the suggestion box is where H sees the change first. An answer equal to the card (`zh`, `p` and `m` unchanged) and a `bad` answer store nothing, so a deck the model agrees with ends at “Done — nothing to change.”; otherwise “Done — 12 AI suggestions.” The run works like the other two (`RECHECK` outside the row, the moving bar with the count, the button disabled meanwhile, the idle reload of v327 held), with one difference: nothing is staged, since a suggestion changes no card — the answered ids are kept in setting `recheckRun` `{at,done}` instead, so an interrupted run resumed 2.5 s after boot, on `online` and on foreground (`resumeRecheck`) asks only for the cards it has not asked about yet. A failed call shows “5 checked, 20 left.” and keeps the button. **And Accept all can be taken back** (`aiAcceptAll` records the v369 store before it applies: `keys` `“*”` — the whole card minus its pictures — and the row “Accepted 12 AI suggestions at 14:32.” with **Undo last run** under the Check row), so a run whose suggestions H accepted in one tap is one tap from being undone; the store is one run deep as before, and a single Accept in the detail is not recorded. Nine keys in six languages (“Check all cards again”, its sentence, “Checking {0} of {1} …”, “Done — {0}.”, “Done — nothing to change.”, “{0} checked, {1} left.”, “Accepted {0} at {1}.”, “AI suggestion”/“AI suggestions”). What it costs: one AI call per five cards over the whole deck — about 34 calls for 167 cards —, which count against the relay's daily limit on a phone without a key, and a deck full of waiting suggestions until H works through them or taps Accept all. **The row says why, and where the result is (v371, H: "You should mention why check all Cards again. Be customer friendly/sexy and professional. And don't forget the final professional polish."):** the row's title is **Check-up** (the button keeps “Check all cards again”, so the two no longer read the same twice), and its sentence is the reason: “The AI keeps getting better. Let it look at your whole deck again — you see every change before you accept it.” — a learner's answer to “why would I run this?”, in six languages (German “Die KI wird ständig besser …”, French “L'IA s'améliore sans cesse …”). The finished lines say what to do next: “Done — 12 cards could be better. See them on the Cards tab.” (the suggestions sit there behind the Accept all bar) and “Done — nothing to change. Your cards are in good shape.”; the Undo row reads “Accepted the AI's changes on 12 cards at 14:32.” **The polish found three keys that stood twice in every language column** (a duplicate key in a JS object literal is not an error — the later one silently wins): “Undo” was the drawing pad's *and* the Undo line's, so the pad's French **“Annuler le trait”** had been overwritten by the delete line's “Annuler” (and the Korean 되돌리기 by 실행 취소) — the pad's button has its own key `pad:Undo` since, with its English in the `en` column; “Untagged” and “Delete {0}” stood twice with the same meaning and lost their earlier entry. The v370 count words “AI suggestion”/“AI suggestions” collided the same way with the AI box's own label and are gone with the lines that used them — the two lines count cards now. And a dead helper of v370 (`waitingSugg`) went. A duplicate-key check per column is worth running after every language change (`re.findall` over each column, `collections.Counter`) — the static key check cannot see this, since both entries exist. Suite `verify-recheck` (the row's title, its sentence, the running line with the count and the disabled button, three calls of 5/5/2 with only the four real changes stored and no card content changed, the Cards bar “4 AI suggestions waiting” with Accept all, Accept all applying them and writing `lastRun.kind` “accept”, the Undo row's line, Undo putting meanings, tags and `mt.verified` back and surviving a reload, an interrupted run resuming and asking only the rest, the German row and title); the english, order, guide, translate, tagall, draw, untagged, tags, pick, ask and photostore suites pass, the audit script and the static key check report nothing new; the row checked by screenshot in light, dark and German. Not yet field-checked — H's deck after one run is the check: are the suggestions worth the calls? (HSK tagging as an automatic level lookup was built in v45 and removed in v46; these are H's own labels.)
- **Add form (Cards → + New)** (a card from scratch starts without a picture — v188, H: after a photo card, + New opened with the old picture still in it; `saveSign` now clears `S.pendingImg`/`S.pendingFull`/`S.pendingShot` after use and the + New handler clears them too, the photo path still brings its crop in through `cropOk`) (v159, H: "when creating a new card from scratch, please provide a 'draw character' option" and "also add an automatic AI translation function", described first and built on "Do"): under the Characters field a soft button **"Draw a character"** (`#f-draw`, `.btn.mini` right-aligned in `.fieldacts` like the picker's "Not here? Draw it" — v160, H: "it should probably be a button"; v159 had a text link) opens the drawing sheet with the pad alone — no photo square, head "Draw the character below." (`openDrawSheet` with `sg.img` null, `noRef`; an Edit form without an image gets the same) — and the chosen character is appended to the field; **pinyin and meaning fill in by themselves** (`autoFill`, a second after the last change to the characters): while the AI is live it is asked (`aiAsk`, kind word, text only), the moving bar "Checking pinyin and meaning …" under the meaning meanwhile (v200), the fields filled from its answer and `S.draft.ai` remembered so Add card stores `mt:{src:"llm",verified:true}` when the fields were left as filled; without the AI, or when it fails ("The AI could not be reached."), the dictionary and the phrasebook fill them (`lineMeaning`; one dictionary word gives its meaning alone), the red hint "Pinyin and meaning were filled in automatically and are unverified — check the tones and the meaning." shows and the card is stored `mt:{src:"dict",verified:false,pending:true}` for the post-save auto run; fields typed by hand are never overwritten (`pinTouched`/`meanTouched`), a stale answer is dropped when the word changed meanwhile. The empty fields show 你好 / nǐ hǎo / hello as the example (v161, H: "don't always just use shutter or 快门 … it should be more generic and easy to understand for anybody" — 快门 / kuàimén / shutter until v160).
- **Cards:** every card newest first with a **124×70 thumbnail** (104×58 until v350, H: "Shouldn't the pictures in the card list be a bit bigger?" — the row's height was set by its three lines of text, not by the picture, so the bigger box costs 7 px of row and about half a row per screen, and the text column keeps 186 px of 322; 140×79 was measured beside it and squeezed the meaning for a whole row; loaded lazily, v213) of the **crop** (`img`; the whole photo only for cards without one — H, v86, reversing v54; `object-fit: contain`) **in the front's box look** (v232, H: "in der Cards Liste auch" — `.thumbbox` with the hairline and a darkened blurred copy of the crop behind the fitted picture, `.thumbbg`, blur 10 px, the same subdued values as the front since v234; the `img.thumb` keeps its class for the suites) or a glyph tile, pinyin, meaning, ⚑ review / green AI pill and the real SRS status (`due`, `in N d`); a **"N photos" pill** on every card whose text exists on other cards (v122); search over hanzi, pinyin, meanings, context words and flag notes; chips **⚑ Flagged (N)** (flag only), **AI (N)** (waiting suggestions, shown only when there are any) and **Unverified (N)**; **+ New** opens the Add form. **A long press starts the marking (v354, H's screenshot of the chip row with "Unverified (0…" cut off under the count: "Der card selection mode sieht kaese aus. Ist zu eng. Wie waer's mit longpress auf karte enters selection mode?" — three ways offered, "Go" on the long press):** the **Select** button of v351 is gone from the Cards chip row and from the inbox head — it had stolen the width a row with "157 of 157" and three chips did not have. **Press and hold** a card row or an inbox photo for `LP_MS` half a second and the marking starts with that one marked, with a short buzz (`longPress`, `navigator.vibrate(12)`); a press that moves more than `LP_MOVE` 10 px, or that the browser turns into a scroll (`pointercancel`), cancels, so scrolling is untouched. The click that ends the press would toggle the row straight off again, so the press arms a one-shot capture listener on the document that eats the next click within `LP_EAT` 250 ms. **While marking the chip row is the selection line** — "12 selected" at the left (`#pick-n`, refreshed by `pickBar` on every tap), **All / None** at the right — so nothing is cramped; the red **Delete N** and **Done** stay in the bar above the tab bar, and out of the marking the row is the chips and the count again, as before v351. The inbox head reads "12 selected" the same way. What it costs: the long press is invisible until you know it — the guide's Cards section carries the sentence in six languages —, and the filter chips are hidden while marking, so a search or a chip has to be set **before** the press (until v353 it could be changed during the marking). No long press while a frame stands or a photo is being read (`busy` decides the row's `data-lp`), and none on a list of one. **The mark sits at the right end of the row (v355, H on the v351 tick: "Irgendwie gefaellt mir das design mit den kleinen kreisen in der ecke auch nicht.", then "Your suggestion?" — three ways were rendered as screenshots and B recommended):** the small circle over the top left corner of the picture is gone. A markable card row carries an empty 22 px ring in its own column at the trailing edge (`.crow.pick{grid-template-columns:124px 1fr auto 22px}`, the tick as the row's last child after the status column), filled with the tint and a white check when marked, and the marked row itself sits on `--tint-soft`; an inbox photo carries the same ring at the right of its timestamp line (`.meta`) instead of on the picture, its card tinted the same way. So the picture is never covered, the marks line up in one column, and an empty ring says which rows can be marked — the way Mail and Files do it. Nothing else changes; the suite's counts of `.crow .tick` and `.shot .tick` are unchanged. **The count shows only while the list is narrowed (v356, H's screenshot of the chip row with "157 of 157" beside a chip cut in half: "157 of 157 steht immer noch da. Muss das sein?"):** no — with no search and no chip the count repeats the header's Deck capsule and takes the width the chips need, so `#cnt` carries `hidden` whenever the shown count equals the deck (`renderCards` and its `refresh` set it), and the chip row is the chips alone. A search or a filter brings it back as "16 of 25". The untagged suite checks the badge is hidden and takes no width with no filter (it read "visible, wider than 10 px" until v355); checked by screenshot in dark, idle and searching; the cards, aifilter, tags, pick and english suites pass. Suite `verify-pick` (the idle row with the chips and no Select; a press marking that card with the ticks, the line, All and Delete 1; the next tap making it 2 with no detail opened; All and None; Done, then a tag chip, then a press and All marking only the tag's two rows; Delete with both gone from IndexedDB and the Undo line, Undo bringing them back; a tab tap ending it; the same for two photos with "Deleted 2 photos"; no `data-lp` while a frame stands; the German "2 ausgewählt / Alle / 2 löschen / Fertig"); checked by screenshot in light, dark and German and on the inbox; the cards, english, guide, aifilter, ask, tags, detail and autocard suites pass. Not yet field-checked. **The list keeps its place (v352, H: "When selecting a card from the cards list and then going back to the cards list by pressing the arrow in the upper left corner, then please be at the place where the card was and not at the top of the list"):** a row tap notes `window.scrollY` in `LIST_SCROLL` and opens the card at the top of the screen (until v351 the detail inherited the list's scroll, which the shorter page clamped), and the detail's **← Cards** goes through `backToList()` — the list is drawn again and the page scrolled back to `LIST_SCROLL` in the next frame, so the card H came from is under the thumb. The value is the last row tap's, so a hop from one card to another through the linked-photos row comes back to the first card's place; Delete card, the Flagged filter's Show and the study screen's ← Cards still land at the top. The cards suite scrolls the list to 900, opens the sixteenth row (the detail at 0) and checks the list comes back to 900; the detail, link, english, pick, ask, share, trad and flag suites pass. Not yet field-checked. **Many cards or photos marked and deleted in one go (v351, H: "implement a function to batch mark and delete cards and photos" — described first, "Go"):** a plain tint **Select** at the right end of the Cards chip row, beside the "27 of 27" count (shown from two cards on), and the same at the right of the Camera tab's **Inbox (N)** head (from two photos on, and not while a frame stands or a photo is being read — `busy`). A tap starts the marking (`PICK` `{kind:"cards"|"shots", set}`, `marking(kind)`): every row carries a tick (`.tick`, an empty ring, filled tint with a white check when marked; **at the right end of the row since v355**, see below), a tap on a row marks or unmarks it instead of opening the card, and **Select becomes All / None** (`pick-all`, `pickAllBtn`) — All marks what the list shows now, so a search or a chip decides the set (the marks survive a filter change). Above the tab bar sits the Undo line's own bar (`.pickbar`, the same surface and place) with a red **Delete N**, disabled while nothing is marked, and **Done**. Delete acts at once through the paths that already exist — `delCustom` per card, `delShot(id,true)` per photo, so a photo's picture is copied onto its cards first — and the v268 Undo line aggregates them into one "Deleted 12 cards" with **Undo**, which puts every card back at its old place with its progress row, or every photo back into the inbox. Done, a tab tap, + New and an emptied list all end the marking (`endPick`). The photo picker shows the photos alone — no frame, no reading box, no result card, no Crop or Delete per row. Nothing else changes: the single Delete in the detail, the Edit form and the inbox row stays, and Photos → Delete N keeps its own sheet. Suite `verify-pick` (Select and the bar's states, two rows marked with no detail opened, All and None, a tag chip narrowing what All marks and the marks surviving the chip's release, Delete with both cards gone from memory and IndexedDB and the line reading "Deleted 2 cards", Undo bringing both back, a tab tap ending it, the same for two photos in the inbox with "Deleted 2 photos" and Undo, no Select while a frame stands, the German Alle / 2 löschen / Fertig); checked by screenshot in light, dark and German and on the inbox; the cards, english, detail, link, aifilter, ask, tags, photostore and autocard suites pass. Not yet field-checked. **A filter whose chip is gone is dropped (v308, H, 2026-09-07: "I accepted two ai suggestions, and now no cards are showing up in the list anymore" — the list said "No cards match." at "0 of 131" with no chip lit):** the AI chip shows only while suggestions wait, so once the last one was accepted the filter had no chip left to switch it off, and every render filtered the list down to nothing; `renderCards` now clears `S.filterAi` when no suggestion waits and `S.filterTag` when its tag has no card left or the Untagged group is empty (the same trap: the last card of a tag re-tagged, the last untagged card tagged). A reload had cleared it too, since the filters live in memory. Suite `verify-aifilter` (two seeded suggestions, the AI chip tapped, Accept all — the chip goes and every card shows with the count, `S.filterAi` off; the tag chip's last two cards re-tagged and the Untagged group emptied the same way; on the v307 code the first case ends at "0 of 27" with the filter on); the cards and english suites pass. Not yet field-checked — H's list after the update is the check.
- **Card detail:** no card-type label (the "sign card / word card" badge and the `t` tag in the tags row went in v105 — H: "not relevant"; the badge keeps "unverified / translation pending / reading uncertain", the back says "meaning unverified" without naming a source); front + back preview with the study screen's taps (H: "enable all test functions in the Cards preview" — tap the character to hide and show the answer, `S.detailHide`; tap the photo for the whole picture, `S.fullPic`; both reset on opening a card and on ← Cards; speaker and parts row as on the back), SRS stats, **Test this card** (studies that card and then continues down the list, newest first; ← Cards stops and restores the session queue), **Edit**, **⚑ Flag / Clear flag**, **Share** (one image through the share sheet, v269) and **Delete** (at once, with Undo, v268).
- **Edit form:** **one card** (`.form`, v124 — H: "the edit screen doesn't look like one thing"; the fields, the strip and Save sit on a white/dark card with radius 16, inputs and strip tiles on `--card2`, the picker on `--fill`; Delete card stands below the card; the Add form is the same card), **laid out like the Camera tab's Read preview** (v245, H's to-do "consistency between the Crop and Edit screens", built on the side-by-side: the photo first — the image field at the top of the form, with Crop again's whole photo, its Cancel directly under the photo at the right as the inbox's row, and the preview or result under that —, then Characters, Pinyin, Meaning, Flag, Tags, the AI button where it shows, and **"Save changes" with "Cancel" in one row** (`.cropacts`, `#e-cancel` = Back without saving) like "Save card" and "Cancel"; after a good AI answer nothing — the fields fill, the green "AI: looks right" box of v135 went, H's v105 rule; after a Crop again reading the source line "Meaning composed word by word, unverified" sits under the meaning until the AI answers, as in the preview): the text is edited **like the Read preview** (H): a character strip per line with the picker and the drawing sheet (`SIGN["editN"]` carries the lines and the card's crop as the photo reference; `sg.onChange` lets the form own the re-render and trigger its AI button), the line input below the strip, pinyin following the text unless edited by hand, meaning — both full-width fields that **grow with their text** instead of cutting it off (`textarea.grow`, `autoGrow`/`wireGrow`, also in the Add form; read back as one line — H) —, the image field showing the **crop itself** (not the whole-photo thumbnail — H) with removal, flag; **Crop again** (v239, H: "allow to re-crop a photo in edit mode", described first and built on "Go"): a link beside Remove image, shown only while the whole photo is still on the phone (`fullPhoto(d)` — the inbox photo or the copy); a tap opens the whole photo in place of the crop with the Camera tab's frame layer, **with the frame the card was cut with already drawn** (v244, H: "use the previous cropping area as starting point" — every card cut from a photo since v244 carries `frame` `{x,y,w,h,a}` as fractions of the photo (`frameOf`, set by `saveSign`, `saveNow`, `finishPending` from the saved `reading.rect` and the Edit form's saves; Remove image drops it); `placeFrame(rid,d.frame,{noRead:true})` lays it on the layer once the photo is laid out and shows the preview with "The frame the card was cut with — adjust it to read again." and **no automatic reading** — a corner drag, Read now or Image only start one; **a card without a stored frame — every card saved before v244 — gets its old crop looked up in the photo** (v246, H: "Crop again does not work as specified" — the phone's cards all predate v244, so they had been getting a fresh proposal instead of the previous cropping area: `findFrame(full,d.img)` searches a grey copy of the photo at ≤ 240 px for the crop's position, coarse grid then the pixels around the best, mean difference under 22 counts as found — the crop is a cut of the photo at its own pixels, JPEG twice; a turned frame's cut or a crop from another photo finds nothing; ~100 ms, "Finding the frame …" meanwhile, `CROP.locating`, a Diagnostics line "the old crop found in the photo (difference 4.2)"; the frame found is stored on the card, so the search runs once); **a small frame opens enlarged** (v247, H's photo of a vocabulary sheet whose found frame was a sixth of the photo: "Now that crop area is so small and hard to see and handle. Any suggestions?" — three options offered, "OK, 1"): when the frame is under `WIN_MAX` 45 % of the photo's width, the photo record shows a **window** cut around it — a plain sub-rectangle of the photo at its own pixels (PNG, so the reader's cut stays one JPEG generation), the same fraction of width and height so the layer keeps the photo's shape and the frame's angle, `windowFor`: twice the frame's width or 1.3 × its height, at least 30 % of the photo, centred on the frame and shifted to stay inside — and the frame is laid into the window's coordinates (`toWin`); corners, moving, turning, the preview, the reading with its second look and the picture path run on the window as on a photo. The badge adds "Tap outside the frame for the whole photo."; **a tap outside the frame switches to the whole photo and back** (`onZoom` in `RECROP[rid]`, taken by `wireCrop`'s tap in place of the enlarged view of v169; the frame follows, mapped through the window, `placeFrame` with `silent`; a result under the photo stays with the frame, a running reading lands on it; the window is made anew around the frame as it stands, so a frame moved on the whole photo gets its own window, and a frame grown past 45 % stays whole), the badge then says "Tap outside the frame to enlarge it again." Everything that leaves the form is mapped back to the whole photo — the card's `frame` (`photoFrame`) and the hand-off's `reading.rect` on the photo's pixels (`photoRect`; the record goes back to the whole photo before the background reading, `leaveWindow`) —, so Save, resume and a later Crop again see photo fractions and photo pixels as before. Measured on the 640 × 300 test photo with a frame of 26 % × 33 % around 合同: the window is 52 % of the photo (333 × 156 px, the frame 1.9 × larger on the layer), the toggle maps the frame to within a pixel both ways, Read now in the window reads 合同, the saved frame is the seeded one to four decimals, the hand-off's rect sits on the photo's pixels and the fill lands with the same frame; on H's 1600 px photo a frame of a sixth would be shown about three times larger. Not yet field-checked. Rejected on the way: the frame's handles enlarged (crowded at that size), and a full-screen crop sheet with pinch zoom (option 3, a new screen — not built), and only when nothing is found **the app's proposed frame** (v241, H: "show the crop frame instantly" — `CROP.auto` and `proposeFrame(rid)` as for a fresh photo: "Finding the text …", then the 16:9 or text box, the preview and the reading by itself); adjustable either way, Cancel keeps the old crop) — the photo becomes a photo record outside the inbox (`SHOTS_EXTRA[rid]`, `shotRec(id)` = the inbox's record or that one, used by `cropBlob`, `cropOk`, `cropSign` and `proposeFrame`), `CROP={id:rid}` and the form registers hooks in `RECROP[rid]` (`redraw`: `renderShots` calls it instead of drawing the inbox while such a frame stands, so the zoom tap and the reading's re-renders draw the form's view, `drawRecrop`; `onRead`; `onImage`; `end`) — so drawing, corners, moving, turning, the enlarged view, the preview with Read now and Image only, the 1.2 s wait, the reading with its second look and the picture path all run unchanged, with Save now beside the bar since v341 (see below; `readingHTML` hid it for a `RECROP` id until v340) and without `S.pendingImg` (the reading keeps its picture on `SIGN[rid].cardImg`). The reading's result goes into the form (`onRead`): the strip, `orig`, confidences, boxes, the straightened crop for the drawing sheet, the alternatives and the traditional verdict, pinyin follows unless typed (`pinTouched`), the meaning takes the word-by-word gloss unless typed (`meanTouched`, new) and the form's own AI button is pressed when the AI is live (`cropSign` skips `signAskAI` for a `RECROP` id, so one call); **the photo and its frame stay open** (v244, H: "don't exit crop mode so fast, do as in the initial crop screen" — until v243 the view closed on the result and the page scrolled to the top): the result sits under the photo as the Camera tab's preview does — the new crop with "Read as “合同条件”. Adjust the frame to read again, or save."
