(`res` with the frame's `rectKey`, drawn by `drawRecrop` while the frame is unchanged; `RECROP[rid].stage` = idle / waiting / reading, set by `showCropPreview`, `cropSign` and `setResult`), a corner drag re-reads as in the inbox, and the view goes only on Save changes, Cancel, Back, Delete or a tab tap; **Cancel restores** the characters, pinyin, meaning and label from before Crop again (`before`, `restoreBefore`) and keeps the old crop; **the Edit form's AI bar and its error line sit under the Meaning field** since v240 (`#e-aistatus` in the Meaning field, as the Add form's `#f-aistatus` and the preview's bar — until v239 it sat under the AI button at the bottom of the form, far from the field it changes; the busy and background-retry suites read it by id and pass); **the fields grow with a value the app fills in** (v281, H's screenshot of the Edit form after the v280 check: "Mixue Bingcheng (ice-cream and bubble-tea chain)" cut off after the first line — "the meaning needs more than one line": `autoGrow` ran only on typing and at the form's render, and a value set by script fires no input event; now the AI answer's pinyin and meaning, the pinyin that follows the strip, the gloss after a Crop again reading and Cancel's restore all call `autoGrow`; the ai suite mocks a long answer for 识 and checks the field shows it whole, screenshot taken); Image only (`cropOk` → `onImage`) takes the crop alone, the text stays, the view too ("Picture taken, the text stays. Adjust the frame to read again, or save."); a reading that finds nothing shows its line in the box with the frame still there, as in the inbox; Save changes stores the new crop as `img` (`recropImg`, JPEG through `jpegOf`, the thumbnail dropped) with its frame (`recropRect` → `frame`), `shot` stays; a stored frame left untouched saves nothing new; **Save changes while a reading is due or running for the standing frame hands it over** (v241, H: "allow instant saving"; since v244 only while `RECROP[rid].stage` is not idle — a frame already read, or the stored one untouched, saves without a hand-off): the card takes the crop of the standing frame now (`handoff` in the save handler: `cropBlob(rid,rect)` → `img`) and `reading:{rect,at}` beside its old text, the form's hooks go while the photo record stays (`endRecrop` stands back once `PENDING[rid]` is set), `PENDING[rid]` = the card's id and the reading runs on — or starts, when the 1.2 s wait had not passed — and `finishPending` fills the card like a Save-now card (the AI check through `signAskAI`, since the form's hooks are gone; flagged only when the reading is doubtful since v342); meanwhile the detail's badge says "Reading …" with the line "The new frame is being read — the text follows when it is done." above the back, the Cards row carries a "Reading …" pill beside the old text, and the list or the detail re-renders when the fill lands (`finishPending`/`failPending` render on the Cards tab whenever no form is open); a reading that finds nothing keeps the old text, drops the frame (`reading` gone, v243) and flags "the new frame could not be read — the old text stays" (`failPending`); at the next start `resumePending` takes every card with an unfinished `reading`, text or not — from the inbox photo with the frame, from the photo copied onto the card (`imgFull` as a photo record outside the inbox, `dropExtraShot` when done) or from the crop alone; Back, Save, Delete and a tab tap end the view (`endRecrop`: `abandonReading` — the timer, the run number, the reading's `SIGN` and status, shared with the inbox's Cancel since v243 —, `CROP`, the record and its object URL). **The card can be saved before the analysis is done, and the analysis then counts (v341, H: "Please allow cropping an image in edit mode and saving it before the AI finishes. Same as when taking a photo." — "In this case, what finally counts is the newly analysed hanzi, pinyin and meaning."):** until v340 the hand-off covered only a reading still due or running (v241), and a Save changes while the form's own AI check ran — the bar under the meaning after a Crop again reading — stored the reader's text with its word-by-word gloss as a card a human had verified, and the AI's answer landed in a form that was gone. Now **(1) Save now sits beside the reading bar in Crop again** as in the Camera tab (`readingHTML` no longer hides it for a `RECROP` id; `drawRecrop` delegates the tap to Save changes, so the same hand-off runs: the card takes the frame now, the detail shows "Reading …", the reading and the AI check fill it; **since v342 the card is flagged only when the reading is doubtful** — `reading.edit` on the hand-off, `finishPending` applies the automatic card's rule of v325 instead of v245's "saved before the reading was done" flag, H's "Go" on the recommendation, since the analysis counts); **(2) Save changes while the form's AI request runs hands the answer over** (`aiRun`, the request as a promise the `#e-ai` handler keeps; `aiLate` in the save handler): the card is saved at once with the fields as they stand — the reader's text and gloss, `mt:{src:"dict",verified:false,pending:false}` —, the form closes, and when the answer comes it replaces text, pinyin and meaning on the card through `applyCardUpdate` (the text re-cut to the reading's lines as the form would have, `mt` llm-verified, `ml` from the answer), the list or the detail re-rendering; the fields as saved are placeholders and the answer wins whatever they held (H's rule); a call that fails after the save marks the card pending, so the auto run offers a suggestion; the form's handler writes nothing into a form that is gone (`ab.isConnected`); **(3) an early save needs no pinyin, meaning or text** — with a hand-off or a running check the required-field errors stand back and an empty text keeps the card's old one until the analysis fills it. What it costs: nothing new for the usual flow; a card saved while the AI runs reads as unverified in the list for the seconds until the answer. Suite `verify-recropai` (the button beside the bar and the hand-off through it — the card with `reading`, the detail's badge, the fill with the AI's meaning, one call, the flag; Save changes during a 2.5 s AI check whose answer changes the first character — the card saved unverified with the gloss at once, then 紧果供应 with the answer's pinyin and meaning on the card, in IndexedDB and in the detail, one call; a connection that dies after the save — the card kept as read and pending, the auto run's call after); the recrop suite expects the button in the form's box since; the edit and english suites pass (a first cut chained `run.finally` and left an unhandled rejection the edit suite caught). Not yet field-checked — the next Crop again saved early on the Xiaomi is the check: the card filled with the AI's text, pinyin and meaning a few seconds after the save. Not yet field-checked; the context-word fields show only on cards that already have one; the AI button ("Ask AI to check text, pinyin and meaning") fills text, pinyin and meaning in place — shown only on cards the AI did not verify, or on a verified card once its text was changed here (`#e-aifield`, `showAi`; v135, H: "why ask the AI when everything was made with the AI anyway?"); **Delete card** at the bottom (H) — from the study back the session goes on with the next card, otherwise back to the list. **The Chinese text itself** may change via `applyCardUpdate`: the id stays, so progress, images and queue entries stay where they are (v118; a corrected text that another card already carries is allowed); pinyin/segmentation/gloss are recomputed unless pinyin was edited by hand; editing marks `verified`, clears `pending` and `suspect`.
- **Usage report and the owner's rows** (v162, H shares the app with friends: "track who is using the app, how many times, how many words, how many tokens" and "protect all those administrative functions with a password" — described first, H chose the report through the share sheet over a server, and one master password): the phone counts in setting `usage` — app opens, reviews, cards created by photo and by hand, deleted cards, AI calls with input and output tokens, pictures read by the AI (`pics`, v178 — H: "record that in the user stats too"; bumped on every answered picture call, shown in the Progress row, the report and the daily row as `pics`/`picsMonth`), and **which analysis did the work** (`models{}`, v179 — H: "record all the models used: the reader, DeepSeek, Qwen": `bumpModel` counts "reader" once per reading and every answered AI call by model name — the card check, the picker's candidates, the picture —, all time and this month; the Progress row says "Analyses: on-device reader 12, deepseek-chat 30, qwen3.7-plus 4.", the report has the line "Analyses by model: …", the daily row carries `models` and `modelsMonth` as objects, `workLines`) (read from each answer's `usage` field, OpenAI-style `prompt_tokens`/`completion_tokens`, Claude `input_tokens`/`output_tokens`, `countTokens` in `aiAsk` and `aiCharAlternatives`) — all time and for the current month (`bump`, `usage()`), nothing is sent anywhere. More → Progress shows the learner's dashboard (v274, see above) and a **Share report** button (since v277 an image of the dashboard, `progressImage`/`shareProgress`; until v276 `usageText`/`shareUsage`, a `.txt` via the share sheet with days used, streak, opens, reviews, cards learned and created, AI calls and tokens, provider, phone model — H asked his friends to send it; the daily row made that unnecessary). **The owner's rows open with a password**: Reset, Diagnostics, All users (v197), Mirror and its address, the Text recognition and Offline translation downloads, and since v190 the AI setup form render only while `S.admin` is set (the usage report too until v169) — a section "Advanced settings" at the end of More holds a password field and Unlock (`ADMIN_HASH`, the SHA-256 of the password via `crypto.subtle`, `sha256`), "Wrong password." on a miss, a Lock button when open; unlocked for the session only. It stops taps, not a reader of the source (the hash is public, the app has no server); changing the password is a new version. The password is not written here — H has it. **Usage sharing (v170, H: "Ich möchte die App sharen und user stats bekommen" — automatic reports from every phone; built on "Go" after H had set the project up from the phone):** once a day, while online and the switch is on, the app inserts one row into the table `reports` (`id`, `created_at`, `data jsonb`; row level security with an insert-only policy for the anon role, H reads the rows in the dashboard's table view) of H's free Supabase project — `SHARE_URL` `https://xttuotninuwxnpcbyejn.supabase.co` and the publishable key `SHARE_KEY` sit in the code, the key can only insert. `reportData()`: a random installation id (`installId`, 16 hex digits, setting `installId`, made on first use), app version, phone model from the user agent, first-use day, days used, streak, opens, reviews, cards learned and in the deck, cards by photo and by hand, deleted, AI calls and tokens (all time and this month), provider and model, inbox count, tag count, language, and since v221 `installed` (`isInstalled`: display-mode standalone or Safari's `navigator.standalone`; the usage report text says "Installed on the home screen: yes/no", `privacy.html` names it) — never card text, never photos, never the API key. `sendReport()` runs at boot, on foreground and on `online`, at most once per day (`lastReport` = the day of the last successful send; a failed send leaves the day open, so the next start or reconnect retries; offline = no attempt; `_reporting` stops overlaps); **a second row the same day when cards changed** (v219, built on "Go" after the first all-users report): `bump` of byPhoto, byHand or deleted sets `REPORT_DIRTY`, and when the app goes to the background (`visibilitychange` hidden) `sendReport(true)` posts another row with `keepalive:true`, so the All users list shows the day's cards the same day — until v218 the counts were as of the day's first start, and a friend's first day always read "0 cards" beside live relay calls. **The first all-users report (2026-09-05, 18 rows) and what it taught:** every row is one browser storage, not one person — a link tapped in WeChat opens in WeChat's built-in browser (Android "wv", Windows a plain desktop UA from WeChat for Windows, iPhone plain), which cannot install the app and may hand out a fresh storage on the next tap, so 12 of the 18 rows were "days 0, cards 0" link taps and the real users were H's Xiaomi (95 cards, 31 of 54 readings weak enough for the picture), H's iPad at v171 and three or four friends with relay calls and no card yet; since v219 `reportData` appends "; WeChat" to the device when `inWeChat()` (`MicroMessenger` in the user agent) and Learn shows a line at the top edge, above the chips — "You are inside WeChat. Open this page in your browser to install the app and keep your cards." (`WX_NOTE`, `wxNoteHTML`, `.wxnote` in the tint on `--tint-soft`, on the card, the done screen and the empty deck, nowhere else). The second report the same evening showed both at work — H's Xiaomi with the day's cards, the WeChat tap marked — and H's "18 phones is incorrect" gave the v220 head line above. More → **Usage sharing**, a row for everyone above Photos: the sentence "Sends anonymous usage counts to the app's owner once a day: days used, reviews, cards, AI calls. No card text, no photos.", the state ("Last sent today." / "Last sent 2026-09-03." / "Not sent yet." / "Off. Nothing is sent.", `shareNote`, `#share-status`) and a checkbox **Send once a day** (setting `shareUsage`, absent = on; off stops it at once); the row also shows **"Your id: 6b3aa0b038fe197f."** (`#share-id`, v171 — H: "Show the id under More → Usage sharing", so a friend can tell H which row is theirs; the per-user query in the dashboard groups by `data->>'install'`; **what is used how much, across all phones** (v179, H: "I want to know what's used how much"): the latest row per install in a CTE, then `jsonb_each_text(data->'models')` summed and grouped by key — one line per analysis with the total and the number of phones, `modelsMonth` for this month; rows from before v179 carry no `models` and drop out of it). The **Progress** row now shows the counts and **Share report** to everyone (until v169 behind the password — a friend without it could not send the report); the owner's rows are Reset, Diagnostics, Mirror and the downloads. About reads "PWA vN. Works offline. Cards and photos stay on this phone; anonymous usage counts go to the app's owner." plus, with a picture provider, "Only when the reading is weak, the framed area of a photo goes to Qwen." (v216–v217, field-confirmed: "goes to your AI provider" was wrong on a friend's phone, which has no provider of its own, so `aboutText` names the provider's short name; the relay stays out of About — H: "Do you have to mention the relay?" — the AI row's What-is-sent line and `privacy.html` describe it) and `privacy.html` (updated 5 September 2026) describes the counts, the switch and the random id. Reachability: `https://xttuotninuwxnpcbyejn.supabase.co/rest/v1/` answered H's phone without a VPN ("No API key found in request", screenshot 2026-09-04); the build sandbox cannot reach supabase.co at all, so the first real row is H's to confirm in the dashboard. The Cloudflare Worker fallback was not needed. **The report inside the app (v197–v199, H: "show an all-users report, password protected"; field-confirmed 2026-09-05, "Works!"):** the owner's row **All users** (behind the password, beside Diagnostics) with **Show** and **Share**: the app posts the password it kept in memory since the unlock (`S.adminPw`, dropped on Lock) to H's second edge function `usage-report` (`reportUrl()`, `supabase/functions/usage-report/index.ts`, "Verify JWT" off, since v212 also `{password, what:"feedback"}` for the feedback rows, **no secret**: the function hashes the password and compares it with `PASSWORD_HASH`, the same SHA-256 as the app's `ADMIN_HASH` — a new password is a new hash in both files), which answers with the SQL function `usage_latest()` (`supabase/report.sql`: the latest row per install with today's relay calls from `relay_usage`, security definer, no rights for anon) — the table itself stays unreadable for the publishable key. `allUsersText` — **every label in plain words since v252** (H: "Make all labels easy to understand", after "What does 'the reader' mean?"): "Phones N" with the note "(one line per browser — a phone that opened the link in WeChat and Chrome is counted twice)", then made cards / read a photo, saved no card / only opened the app / opened it inside WeChat / installed on the home screen / used in the last 7 days; "All phones together" with cards, from photos, typed by hand, cards reviewed, AI checks, with the photo, via the owner's key today, and "work done by" with "on-device reader, readings 112", "deepseek-chat, checks 94" — one figure per line, the numbers in one column, every line but the device's within 40 characters (the box's width at 390 px; 46 wrapped); the sections "Phones with cards (N)", "Read a photo, saved no card (N)", "Only opened the app (N)"; a phone's block: "Phone <id>", "app version 196", "installed on the home screen" / "used in the browser", "device …", "cards 94 (80 from photos, 14 typed)", "cards reviewed 310", "days used 12, app opened 40 times", "AI checks 120, 30 with the photo", "photos read 100, 30 of them poorly", "checks via the owner's key today 12", "first used 2026-08-20", "last report 2026-09-06". The same words in the Feedback report ("app version 212", "from phone <id>"), in More → Progress until v273 ("App opened 40 times, 310 cards reviewed, 120 AI checks, 30 photos checked by the AI. Work done by on-device reader 100 readings, deepseek-chat 90 checks." — since v274 the row is the dashboard and these counts live in the shared report only), in the Usage sharing sentence ("days used, cards made and reviewed, AI checks") and in the shared usage report (`usageText`, since v252 in blocks — the head with app version, first used, device and installed; Learning: days used with the streak, app opened, cards learned, cards reviewed; Cards: from photos, typed by hand, deleted, photos waiting in the inbox, tags; AI: set up, checks, photos checked by the AI, tokens sent and received, work done by, this month), `workLines` giving each model its unit; the old words were reviews, AI calls, pictures, relay today, analyses, readings, weak, opens, installs. **Laid out to be read at a glance since v250** (H: "Kannst du bitte die all user stats ein bisschen übersichtlicher machen? Das dauert immer, bis ich da durchsteige"): the head as two short tables with the counts aligned in a column — "Installs 18" with its note line "(one per browser storage, not per person)", then with cards / only tried the reader / only opened the page / in WeChat's browser / installed on a home screen / reported in the last 7 days, and "Totals" with cards (by photo, by hand), reviews, AI calls (pictures), relay today and the analyses one model per line —, then the sections "Users with cards (N)", "Read a photo, no card yet (N)" (until v250 "Tried the reader" — H: "What does 'the reader' mean?"; the app's word for the on-device text recognition, and since v251 the group counts an install whose reader ran at all, `models.reader`, beside the AI and relay calls) and "Only opened the page (N)" as headings with the count, and **one block of short lines per install** instead of one long line: the id, then "v196, installed, Linux; Android 10; K", "cards 94 (80 by photo, 14 by hand)", "reviews 310, days 12, opens 40", "AI 120 calls, 30 pictures", "readings 100, weak 30", "relay today 12", "first 2026-08-20, last 2026-09-06"; every line but the device's stays under 46 characters, so nothing wraps in the 12 px monospace box at 390 px; checked by screenshot in light and dark. Until v249 the head was one sentence — "18 installs (one per browser storage, not per person): 3 with cards, 3 that only tried the reader, 12 that only opened the page; 2 inside WeChat's browser. 2 installed on a home screen, 18 reported in the last 7 days." (v220, H on the second report: "you show 18 phones and that's incorrect" — a row is a browser storage; with cards = cards > 0, tried the reader = AI calls or relay calls today without a card, the rest only opened the page, WeChat from the device string; the row's status line says "N installs, fetched …" too) —, cards by photo and hand, reviews, AI calls and pictures, today's relay calls and the analyses by model summed over the phones, then (v221, H: "All 3 please" on the report proposals, then "Do only 1 for now" — parts 2 and 3, the day-by-day query and the feature and AI-failure counters, were built and reverted unmerged, to come later on their own) **the real users first, by cards**, one line each (id, version, installed or browser, first day, last report, days, opens, cards, reviews, AI calls with pictures, the reader's readings with the weak share "reader 112, weak 63 of 112", relay today, device), the installs that only tried the reader under their own head, and **the rows that only opened the page folded into one line** — "12 installs only opened the page: 8 Windows, 3 iPhone, 1 Android (2 in WeChat)."; Show toggles a `.diag` block, Share sends it as a `.txt`. **A failed answer names its sender:** Supabase's own JWT gate ("Verify JWT with legacy secret" on) and the function's "wrong password" both answer 401, so `fetchAllUsers` reads the body — `Invalid JWT` → "the report function still checks the JWT — switch off \"Verify JWT\" under its Settings in the dashboard", `wrong password` → "the report function does not accept the app password — deploy the current usage-report code from the repo, it carries the password's hash", 404/503 → "the report function is not set up", anything else with the body's first 120 characters — and every failed answer goes to the error log (`logErr("report",…)`) so Diagnostics shows it. Why the hash and not a secret: the function first read a secret `REPORT_PASSWORD` (v197–v198); H set it in the dashboard, re-deployed, and the function still answered "wrong password" on every call while the app's hash proved the phone sent the right password — why the secret never reached the function was not found from the phone, so the secret went and the hash sits in the code (v199), and the report rendered at once. **Reading the rows on the phone:** the dashboard's Table Editor hides the table list on a narrow screen (H saw only "Create a table"), so H uses the SQL Editor (`…/project/xttuotninuwxnpcbyejn/sql/new`): `select created_at, data from reports order by created_at desc;` for the raw list, and a saved per-user query — `select distinct on (data->>'install') …` with the counts cast from `data`, ordered by `data->>'install', created_at desc` — for one line per phone; the id under Usage sharing (v171) is what a friend reads out so H finds their line. (Supabase's project creation was down in Singapore on 2026-09-04; H created the project later the same day.)
- **Share the app** (v210, H: "add a share app function", described first and built on "Go"; v211, H: "place the share function more easy to find, for example on top — and does it really only work with Chrome? I don't think so" — right: the app installs from Safari, Chrome, Edge and Samsung Internet alike, only the menu entry differs): **the first row of the "Share" section** (the first section of More until v274, the second since v275, under Learning) — "Send the link to a friend. The app installs from any browser, no store." with a **Share** button (`shareApp`, `#app-share`): the share sheet with `APP_SHARE_TEXT` ("识字 Zeichentrainer — learn the Chinese characters you see around you. Take a photo of a sign, get the card. Open the link in Safari or Chrome, not inside WeChat, and add it to the home screen:" — the browser sentence since v219, see the first report below) as the text and `APP_URL` as the url — **the text carries no link** (v215, H's WeChat screenshot: the link stood twice in the message, because Android's share sheet appends the url field to the text; v210–v214 had the link in both); without a share sheet the text plus the link is copied and the row says "Link copied.", without a clipboard it shows the link. Nothing is sent by the app itself. A friend behind the wall needs github.io for the first install — the mirror only serves an installed app; the text does not say so. **Field-confirmed 2026-09-05** (H: "Share link works now, only one link in WeChat").
- **Feedback** (v212, H: "add a provide feedback function — of course I must receive this feedback", described first and built on "Go"): the second row of More's "Share" section for everyone — "Tell the app's owner what works and what does not.", a text box (`#fb-text` — since v218 in the forms' field look on the row's `--card2` surface, full width, placeholder in `--label3`, the tint ring on focus, growing with its text through `wireGrow`; `.mrow textarea` in styles.css — H: "looks a little bit old school"; v212 had a bare browser textarea) and **Send** (`sendFeedback`): one row into the table `feedback` of H's Supabase project with the publishable key (insert only, at most 2 000 characters — `supabase/feedback.sql`, run once after report.sql) carrying the message, the app version and the installation id — no name, no cards, no photos unless typed; "Thank you, sent." and the box empties, "Write a few words first." on an empty box, "No connection. Try again when online." offline with the text kept, "Could not send: the feedback table is not set up" on a 404 (every failure in the error log as `feedback`). **The owner's row Feedback** (behind the password, under All users, `#fb-show`/`#fb-share`/`#fb-out`): Show lists the messages newest first (`feedbackText`, laid out like the All users report since v251 — H: "Same for feedback": a head with the day and "messages N (newest first, up to 500)", then one block per message with a blank line between — "2026-09-05 10:00, v212", "from <id>" and the message indented; until v250 the blocks ran together on one meta line each), Share sends them as a `.txt` (`shareFeedback`); both go through the report function with `what:"feedback"`, which calls the SQL function `feedback_list()` (the latest 500) — `fetchReport(what)` carries the v198 error mapping for both rows, `FEEDBACK` is cached like `USERS` and dropped on Lock. H re-deploys the report function once and runs `feedback.sql` once (done 2026-09-05); `privacy.html` has the bullet. **Field-confirmed 2026-09-05** (H: "Feedback works, message arrived in the owner's row").
- **Review flag:** `flag:true` / `flagNote` on the card; included in export. More → **Flagged cards → Show** opens the Cards list with the Flagged filter on; **Share** sends a plain-text list via the share sheet (`.txt`) for a teacher.
- **Export/import** (More): progress + cards as JSON, `zeichentrainer-YYYY-MM-DD.json.txt` via the share sheet; import validates by content and upserts by `id`, keeping local images (exports before v118 carry the text as the key and get it as the id). **Photos in the export** (v166, H: "could you also export photos? Now only text", described first, H chose crops and whole photos): a checkbox "Include photos (adds about N MB)" under the Export row (setting `exportPhotos`, `photoBytes` × 1.37 for the base64) puts every card's crop and whole photo into the file as base64 (`imgB64`/`imgFullB64` with their type, `blobToB64`, the file then unindented, `photos:true`) and Import turns them back into blobs (`b64ToBlob`, the thumbnail cache dropped); a file without photos keeps the photos already on the phone, as before. Roughly 3 MB for 70 crops, 15–30 MB with the whole photos; WeChat takes files up to 100 MB. Field-confirmed (H's 70 cards, 19.5 MB through WeChat, the photos on the other phone: "OK works"). **An older app imports such a file without a word and without the photos** — the first try landed on a phone still at v165; the receiving app must be at v166 or later (More → About), then the same file imported again restores them. Since v167 the import ends with one sentence on what it did — "Imported 70 cards and 53 progress entries, 68 with photos.", "(N could not be read)" when a photo failed to decode, "The file carries no photos; the photos on this phone were kept." for a text-only file — so a silent loss cannot happen again. The Export row shows **"Last export: N days ago"** (setting `lastExport`), red after 30 days or when never exported — the cards exist only on this phone. **Photos** row: inbox count, and a **Delete N** button for photos older than 30 days that already became a card (cards carry `shot:<inbox id>`; the cards keep their crop, and the whole photo is copied onto them just before the deletion — v214, `keepPhoto`). **Reset** wipes cards, progress and photos. **Diagnostics** (v93, H: "crop draws, nothing happens afterwards" could not be reproduced in Chromium — the phone has to tell): the last 20 uncaught errors and unhandled rejections (`ERRLOG`, `logErr`, persisted in setting `errlog`), the steps of the last reading with their times (`READLOG`) and its competing passes (`LAST_READ.passes`), the last three **drawings** (`DRAWLOG`, v140 — the strokes as drawn, rounded to pixels of the 720 px pad, and what the model answered; H: "I feel no improvement" after v139, and the synthetic strokes of the harness are not a finger — the phone's own strokes are the only field data), the last three **AI exchanges** — request payload and raw reply, never the key (`AILOG`, `logAi`, v97: the phone is the only place DeepSeek can be watched from; it once returned the Chinese text as the meaning and waved 二和玖 through) —, plus page/script version, online state, AI provider (never the key; since v267 with "(text deepseek)" when the text goes elsewhere than the active provider), service-worker presence and user agent — **the reading log keeps its steps since v285** (H's diagnostics of the SF Express card, 2026-09-07: forty lines of "recognizing … 61%" and nothing else — the reader's progress lines had pushed every step and the proposed-frame line out of the 40-entry log, and `cropSign` had wiped the proposal line at the reading's start anyway): a progress line now overwrites the previous progress line (`readingStatus`), and the frame's own lines — "frame proposed by the app: …" and "the old crop found in the photo …", marked `pre` — stay at the head of the new reading's log; the diag suite expects the proposal first, "cutting out the frame …" second and no two progress lines in a row; the autoframe, twopass, recrop and english suites pass — **the last reading's steps and passes survive a restart since v267** (setting `readlog`, written by `saveReadLog` 0.8 s after each step and after the competition, restored at boot into `READLOG`/`LAST_READ` — H's first diagnostics after the v266 update said "Last reading (0 steps)": the update's reload had emptied the in-memory log; the diag suite reloads and finds the steps) — **The AI exchanges survive a restart too since v384** (H's washing machine, 2026-09-09, after five versions tuned against his answer rebuilt from the reading log: "We are trying the same thing again and again, and it doesn't seem to work." — right, and the reason was in his own diagnostics, which said `AI exchanges (0, newest last):` twelve minutes after the reading. `AILOG` lived in memory alone, so the v327 idle reload, an update, or simply reopening the app wiped the model's raw answer between the photo and the sharing; the reading log has been persisted since v267 and the error log since v93, and only this one was not. Every version since v379 was therefore fitted to the box numbers as the reading log prints them — **rounded to whole percent**, which on a label box about 7.5 % wide is a tenth of a box on each edge, wider than the `LB_TMPL` tolerance the drawing test measures — so the harness passed and the phone failed each time, on data that was never the phone's. Now `logAi` writes setting `ailog` 0.8 s after each entry (`saveAiLog`, the reading log's own debounce) and boot restores it, so Qwen's own JSON with its own box numbers reaches the next session. Device only: the daily usage row carries `reportErrors()` and never this, and the picture entry has carried `[picture 800×442 JPEG, 24 KB]` in place of the base64 since v173. **The rule it stands for: when the harness and the phone disagree, the harness is wrong and that is what gets fixed first — no heuristic is tuned against numbers the phone did not actually send.** Suite `verify-diag` (an exchange logged with a raw reply, a reload, the reply still in `AILOG` and in `diagText()` with its box numbers, and absent from `reportData()`); the english, ai and picture suites pass.) **Show** expands the text in place (`.diag`), **Share** sends it as a `.txt` via the share sheet (`shareDiag`; a third share-sheet dance on purpose, the two others keep their own texts), and **Copy** puts the same text on the clipboard (v292, H: "offer the option to share/copy diagnostics and user data and feedback directly from the app" — so it can be pasted straight into a chat): `copyText(text,st)` through `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, the row's line says "Copied.", or "Copy is not available here — tap Show and select the text." when the clipboard refuses; the same button on **All users** (`#users-copy`, the cached rows, else one fetch) and the owner's **Feedback** (`#fb-copy`); the three rows carry Show, Share and Copy under their text (`.fieldacts`, as the Progress row) since a third button beside the sentence squeezed it. Suites diag (the copied text equals `diagText()`, the line, the three buttons, the refusal's sentence), users and feedback (the copied text equals the shown report, no second fetch) pass; the rows checked by screenshot. Not yet field-checked.

### Field lessons that shaped the UI (keep)
- **Flow and UI:** four free corners on the crop frame with a perspective warp of the cut were built in v127 (H: characters photographed from the side are not recognised) and taken out again in v128 on H's request ("No, I don't want this. Leave it as it was.") — the frame is a rectangle with resizing corners; do not bring the quadrilateral back unasked · No tab switching for the AI (v31/v32) · button always visible, never replaced by the auto result (v32) · one action after framing (v39) · plain words instead of jargon and `·` shorthand (v25/v39) · three top-bar pills overflow at 390 px, so Deck only shows off the Learn tab (v25) · Chrome/Android does not repaint after the camera until touched (v34) · the card text keeps the photo's lines, a horizontal word is one line (v42) · reading starts by itself after the frame is released, no Read tap (v47) · the crop frame stays on the photo until Save (v50) · after grading the next card must start at the top of the screen (v53) · two taps on the front: photo = whole picture, character = answer (v55) · the static HTML must already match the first render (Deck pill hidden in index.html), otherwise the top bar jumps at load (v57) · `html` needs the page background, a `color-scheme` and an inline fallback in index.html, otherwise a line in the wrong colour flashes above the page at load (v58, kept for light and dark in v82).
- **Storage, deploys and network:** Android shares `.txt` but not `.json` (v3) · a cached 404 poisoned the dictionary once (SW caches only OK responses) · status text written into a DOM node captured before a re-render disappears silently — keep status in state and re-query (v47) · the inbox re-render previews only the inbox's readings — the Edit form's text state survives a tab tap, and previewing it before the pinyin library is loaded threw "pinyinPro is not defined" into every reading (v108) · github.io is unreliable from China, jsDelivr is not (v48/v49) · the reader's files must be reachable without the VPN too, hence the mirror fallback for `vendor/` (v56) · the worker's fallbacks help only a page the worker controls — a page with "SW no" fetched every reader file from github.io and hung for good; the page needs the same origin-then-mirror rule of its own, a way to ask an active worker to take it over, and a stall that ends a load (v335) · a new worker must fetch the shell past the browser's HTTP cache, or rapid deploys leave the old app under a new cache name (v71) · the script carries its own version and checks it against the page's label at boot, so a mixed shell repairs itself (v72) · "the frame draws, nothing happens afterwards" on v92 was not reproducible in Chromium and vanished with the v93 update (VPN on) — cause never seen, most likely a mixed shell from the mirror, which caches each file on its own; first ask for More → Diagnostics before touching the reader (v93) · a reading that a corner drag superseded must abandon, or two results and two AI requests land on the same frame (v117) · the error log persists across updates, so an error in Diagnostics may come from an older script — compare the stack's line numbers with the current file before chasing it (v117) · an older app imports a file with fields it does not know without a word — every new export field needs the import to say what it did (v166–v167, the photos lost on a phone at v165) · Chrome's voice list says nothing about whether the phone can speak — the Xiaomi lists no voices and speaks Chinese (v165) · a tap on a layer that lets the page scroll must be read from the click event, not from pointerup — iOS Safari sends pointercancel there (v206, H's iPhone; the frame's own handles carry touch-action none and are unaffected) · an installed app keeps its old manifest for days; a new share entry needs a re-install through "Install app", and a home-screen shortcut never gets one (v167) · an app that holds one API key loses it the moment another provider is chosen — H's DeepSeek key had to survive the Qwen setup, hence one account per provider with the old key migrated (v172) · Android's share sheet appends the url field to the shared text, so the text must not carry the link itself, or WeChat shows it twice (v215) · a Blob put into two IndexedDB stores is stored twice — the inbox photo copied onto the card as `imgFull` doubled every phone's photo bytes until v214; keep one copy and a reference, copy only when the original goes · a long-running action must keep its state outside the row it was started from — a tab change re-renders the screen, the old row's status goes nowhere and a "running" guard swallows the next tap (v257, Translate all: "changed page, pressed again and no reaction"; the same rule as v47's status text in state).
- **The reader:** a tilted frame is straightened before reading — Tesseract fails beyond a few degrees (v59) · Tesseract's per-symbol boxes are not to be trusted for Chinese: the heights are right, the horizontal ends drift, stop early on the last character or lose it altogether — take the line's ends from the image columns (v69/v75) · **what the reader sees must be a JPEG** — this Tesseract build misreads large canvas PNGs and WebPs (same pixels: JPEG 0.95 → the full line at 99 %, PNG → one character); intermediates stay PNG so only the last step is lossy — two JPEG generations in a row lost the line too (`READ_JPEG`, `deskewBlob` always hands back a JPEG, `jpegOf` re-encodes card images to JPEG 0.85 at save) · the reader is chaotic on large text: the same clean crop reads perfectly at one scale and as garbage at another, so read at several scales and let the readings compete (v73) · light text on a strong colour defeats the reader's own thresholding — a binarised copy reads it (v77) · shading (a curved bottle) defeats every brightness threshold, a chromaticity copy does not care (v95) · the score must not reward a soup of short lines, and a band only one pass saw or that is tiny beside the others is decoration (v95) · the AI must be allowed to say "unreadable" — asked only to correct, it invents (v95) · a traditional logo needs the traditional model, the simplified one can only approximate (v96) · confidence must weigh more than character count, and a text several passes agree on beats a lone near-equal score (v96) · the AI sees every competing reading, not just the winner (v96) · the AI may fix what was read weakly or what another reading offers, never a character every pass read clearly — asked for a sign, it answers with a common phrase (减展单车 → 共享单车, v143) · when the readings circle around one dictionary word, the dictionary decides before the AI is asked — the AI refused 养浴多 / 次乐多 / 开乐多 twice (v99) · every gate must judge by the same score as the competition, or a long soup of fragments switches the fallbacks off (v100) · the ink height must ignore solid decoration, or the text looks like fragments (v100) · a single line whose characters are as tall as the frame's ink is the text, however short — the fragment penalties are for soups beside real text, not for a lone 推 that ten passes agree on (v116) · a white character on a black plate has no chromaticity, so the chromaticity copies read noise there — they must lose, not be skipped (v116) · a frame one text height tall cannot hold two lines — a second "line" in it is decoration, and the pass must lose score for it, not be rewritten (v137) · bold outlined characters fill a tight frame's rows almost solid — the ink height must let dense rows ride inside a text band, or a one-line sticker reads as a twelve-line soup (v144) · whatever fills the corners of a rotated crop lands in every copy and in the ink height — it must be the crop's own dominant colour, not an edge pixel, not a median, not the border (v138) · six stroke edges per text row is the rule that keeps stripes out; four was tried for big characters and lost 业主直租 through a changed ink height (v138). · **the stroke medians do not read photos** (tried after v142, H: "can the stroke analysis also improve the word recognition in the photo?"): a shape re-ranker was measured in the harness — at every character position the candidates (the reading, the other passes, the dictionary, the true character) were compared with the binarised, thinned photo glyph at `charBox` by symmetric chamfer distance, truncated chamfer and the overlap of the medians drawn at the photo's stroke width, with a small shift-and-scale search. On 56 positions of 14 images the best measure kept 29 right characters, broke 24 and fixed 1; even on the clean print signs it broke 5 of 22, and the clean 台 of a bold banner lost to 后, 富 and 特. The medians are one 楷 style, the signs are bold, rounded and brush fonts, and the glyph box carries the neighbours, a logo oval or speckled stone. Both of H's actual failures (美团 read as 人美加, 减震单车 as 减震单生;) were wrong in length, where a per-position re-ranker cannot act at all. Not built; the matcher stays in the drawing sheet, where the strokes are real. · **A rounded bold display font can be a model limit — check the clean copy before touching the reader** (v169, H's 奈斯餐吧: gold rounded characters on a shaded blue panel, first framed loosely under the Latin "NESTO", then tightly around the four characters; H: "Why can't he figure this out?", "Still nonsense"): the loose frame reads two-line soups on the phone and in the harness — the Latin letters as a Chinese line (和人叨时) above the text as garbage — and the tight frame gives 广包看中 on the phone, 本了了 in the harness (a blob of the colour copy at 95 %). The deskew is right (the text sits at −9.5° in the frame, the straightened black-and-white copy is a clean, perfectly legible 奈斯餐吧), and the model still reads that clean copy as 夫斯均肥 / 雯斯豆妈 / 索斯餐妈 / 去斯餐凤 at six sizes from 80 to 370 px: 斯 always, 餐 half the time, 奈 and 吧 never; thinning the strokes makes it worse, the traditional reader gives 泰新宴吧 / 素箕窒旋. So no copy, size or angle helps — the model does not know this font's 奈 and 吧. The AI then invented "Guangbao Kanzhong (possibly a shop or brand name)" for 广包看中 instead of calling it bad. What would read it: a vision model that sees the crop — ruled out by H's own rule that photos never leave the phone, so it is H's decision, not a reader change. Not built.
- **Fixing a character and drawing:** one misread character is fixed by tapping it, not by retyping the line; the AI is asked first, the dictionary is the fallback (v61) · candidates first, drawing as the second level when the right character is missing; the photo character stays in view while drawing, because the system keyboard's handwriting pad hid it (v62) · the Tesseract worker has **one** logger: progress goes to `_ocrLog`, the handler of the running job, never to the status closure of whoever created the worker — the pad's reading once wrote "recognizing … 100%" into the photo's box and wiped the editor (v63) · action rows in the picker are flex (`.ckacts`), not the auto-fit grid, which broke the pad's buttons (v63) · the picker is two levels only, suggestions then drawing; no type field in it (v64) · the word-picking overlay (pinyin boxes on the photo, selection bar) went unused once the AI-first preview existed — removed with its Split into words button (v91) · inserting a character through the picker (+ before / + after, a trailing + on the strip) did not convince H in v91 and was removed in v92 — asked for again in v119 together with a Remove that can be found: a red button in the action row, not a text link at the right edge · the drawing pad is a full-screen sheet with a Done button — an inline pad below the fold went unseen, and reading after each stroke felt like being cut off (v65) · an automatic crop is a guess — let the hand correct it (drag/pinch on the photo square) (v70) · small black-on-white pad images are indifferent to PNG/JPEG (measured) (v73).

## Hard constraints (learned in the field — do not violate)
1. **No external dependencies / CDNs.** Must run offline and behind the GFW. System CJK fonts only. Libraries and data go into `vendor/` (licenses in `vendor/LICENSES.txt`).
2. **All paths relative** (`./…`). The app lives under a subpath (`/zeichentrainer/`).
3. **Persistence only via IndexedDB** (do not use localStorage/sessionStorage). `navigator.storage.persist()` is requested — Xiaomi/MIUI aggressively evicts storage of non-installed sites. The SW must **never cache non-OK responses** — a 404 cached during a deploy window poisoned the dictionary permanently once; `loadDict` additionally heals such entries.
4. **Phone-only deploys:** H uses GitHub only in the phone browser. Ship changes as small, clearly described PRs. As few files as possible; generate/commit binaries (icons, vendor data) in the repo, never ask H to upload them.
5. **Privacy rule:** Confidential text (real contracts, suppliers, company internals) must **never** end up in the public deck or repo. General vocabulary only. H's provider keys live in the Supabase function's secrets, never in the code (v191). Photos and card images stay on the device — the one exception (v173): the straightened framed area, at most 800 px, goes to a picture-capable AI provider when the reading is weak and H's switch is on, see the AI section; the daily usage row (v170) carries counts only.
6. **Files leave the device only via the share sheet:** programmatic blob downloads (`<a download>`) are silently blocked by Android/MIUI. Use `navigator.share` with a file — and Chrome/Android only shares whitelisted types (`.txt`/`.csv`/images/PDF yes, **`.json` no**), hence the `.json.txt` extension with `text/plain`.

## Design (iOS-style since v82, on H's request: "orient on a modern Apple app")
The lacquerware look (INK/BONE/VERM/JADE) was replaced in v82 by the treatment of a current iOS app. Light and dark follow the phone (`prefers-color-scheme`; `color-scheme: light dark`, two `theme-color` metas, inline fallback background in index.html so nothing flashes at load). The seal red stays as the tint, the big characters keep the serif.
- Tokens in `styles.css` (light / dark): `--bg` #F2F2F7 / #000 · `--card` #FFF / #1C1C1E · `--card2` #F2F2F7 / #2C2C2E · `--fill` #E9E9EE / #3A3A3C · `--label` #000 / #FFF · `--label2` #6E6E73 / #A1A1A6 · `--label3` #AEAEB2 / #6E6E73 · `--sep` hairline · `--tint` #C8372D / #E0483E (+ `--tint-soft`) · `--ok` #2FA36B / #3DBE7A (+ `--ok-soft`, for AI and verified) · `--bar` translucent bars · `--shadow` (light only). Radii: cards 16 (`--rc`), buttons and inputs 12/10 (`--r`).
- Fonts: UI = Apple system stack (`-apple-system`, SF Pro, PingFang SC, then Roboto/system-ui on Android); Hanzi = `'Songti SC','STSong','Noto Serif CJK SC','Source Han Serif SC','SimSun',serif` for the big characters, strips and rows; `--mono` only for timestamps and Diagnostics (the version label is hidden since v225). Pinyin is set in the UI font, semibold.
- Components: large title `识字` in the sticky, blurred top bar (no version label beside it since v225; the version sits in More → About) with stat capsules (Due/Done on Learn, Deck elsewhere — three overflow at 390 px) · translucent bottom tab bar, active tab in the tint · the study card and the done screen are white/dark cards with radius 16 · the **田字格 reticle** stays the signature element (since v223 one shape for every word card: the wide box at the card's width, 150 px tall per photo line, on `--card2`, hairline lines and corner ticks, dashed tint crosshair only for single characters; font fitted to the longest photo line — `frontLines`/`frontBox`; base font by character count 1→150px, 2→104, 3→74, 4–8→58, 9–12→44, 13+→34, capped at 72 px so the box keeps its height — the 260 px square for up to three characters went on H's "Bitte consistency!") · lists (Cards, More) are inset grouped rows with hairline separators and rounded ends · buttons: `.btn.primary` filled tint with white text, `.btn` tinted soft, `.btn.danger` red on soft, `.del` plain tint text, grades as four `--card2` tiles · inputs are white/dark fields with a hairline inset, tint ring on focus · chips are `--fill` capsules, active = filled tint · the character strip buttons (`.ck`) are white/dark tiles with a hairline; low confidence = tint ring, AI candidate = green ring, chosen = filled tint (`.signed .ck.on` — the surface rule once outranked it and the chosen character showed white on white) · the reading bar (`.reading .bar`) is a 4 px `--fill` track with a tint segment sliding across.
- The script draws nothing in fixed colours: inline SVG uses `style="stroke:var(--…)"`, canvases read the tokens at paint time (`cssVar`) — the drawing pad's strokes are `--label`, its grid `--sep`, the photo view's padding `--fill`.
- The crop frame is independent of the theme: white dashed edge with a dark outline and white handles, nothing dimmed — it must stay visible on any photo colour.
- Readability rules kept from the old design: body 17 px, hints and labels 13–14 px in `--label2`, meanings 18 px, touch targets ≥ 44 px, inputs ≥ 16 px (no zoom), safe-area padding top and bottom, plain words instead of jargon.

## Didactics / SRS
- Loop: photo + character/word → tap to reveal → pinyin (+ pronunciation), meaning, (context word). No example sentences (H's decision).
- SM-2 light, grades `again / hard / good / easy` with interval preview. `again` re-queues the card within the same session.
- Session = due cards + max. 8 new ones. "Pull forward" pulls future cards when nothing is due. **Card order** (v153, H: "provide choices for the order in which the flash cards are shown", described first and built on "Go"): More → Learning → "Card order" with the chips Oldest first (the old order: due cards by due date, new ones by creation), Newest first (both groups by creation, newest first) and Random (a fresh shuffle per session); due cards always come before new ones, the up to eight new cards are picked in that order, a tap rebuilds the session at once (setting `learnOrder`, `LEARN_ORDERS`, `orderCards` in `buildQueue`).

## Deck format
No built-in deck; all cards live in IndexedDB `custom`. Schema:
```
{ id:"坚果", c:"坚果", p:"jiānguǒ", m:"nuts", t:"Custom", at:1756800000000,   // id: the key (text while free, else 坚果#<at>) · t: Custom | Sign · at: creation time
  tags:["Chinese class","HSK 3"],     // optional: H's own labels (v133) — the Cards filter and the Learn filter
  seg:["坚果","\n","供应"],           // optional: word boundaries + photo line breaks for the front
  lb:"photo"|"auto",                    // where the line breaks came from (auto = estimated from the crop's shape)
  w:"…", wp:"…", wm:"…",              // optional: context word + pinyin + meaning
  shot:"shot_1756800000000",           // inbox photo the card came from (photo cleanup)
  mt:{ src:"llm"|"dict"|"phrasebook"|"nmt"|"gloss", verified:true|false, pending:false, suspect:"reason" },
  flag:true, flagNote:"…",            // review flag
  alts:["养兴多","义乐多"],               // optional: the other readings of the photo (v96), for the AI check
  trad:"養樂多",                          // optional: the text as it stands on the photo, in traditional characters (v101); shown on the front, c stays simplified
  ml:"de",                               // the language of the meaning (v256) — absent = English: the dictionary, the phrasebook, the offline model and every card before v256
  ms:{ en:"nuts", de:"Nüsse" },          // every meaning the card got, by language (v265) — the app shows the one for its language when the card has it; a changed text keeps only the current one
  ai:{ zh, p, m, note, ok, bad, at, model }, // a waiting AI suggestion (bad: the AI calls the text OCR garbage)
  frame:{ x:0.02, y:0.52, w:0.96, h:0.46, a:0 }, // the frame the crop was cut with, as fractions of the photo (v244) — Crop again starts from it; cards before v244 have none and get the app's proposal
  reading:{ rect, at, failed }              // a card saved before its reading was done (v237, id reading#<at>): the frame it was saved with; c is empty until the reading fills it, failed = why it never did
}
```
Sign cards add `kind:"sign"`, `segs` (per line) and `gloss` (`[{w,p,m}]`, feeds the parts row); word cards from the Read path carry `gloss` too; any card saved from a photo carries `img` (crop) and `shot` (the inbox photo's id); **the whole photo is stored once** (v214, H's "Go" on the v213 audit's storage finding): `imgFull` exists only when the inbox photo is gone — `fullPhoto(d)` = `imgFull`, else the inbox photo through `shot` (the front toggle, the detail, the list thumbnail of a card without a crop — not repeated on the back, H); `saveSign` stores no copy while the inbox photo exists, `delShot` (the inbox's Delete, Photos → Delete N) first copies the photo into every card that references it (`keepPhoto`), `dedupePhotos` at boot drops the copy from cards of v213 and earlier whose inbox photo is still there (and the import drops it the same way), the export takes the whole photo from `fullPhoto`, so files stay the same, `photoBytes` counts it once, and the Edit form's Remove image drops `shot` too (until v213 the front still showed the inbox photo after the removal); saves about half of a phone's photo bytes (field-confirmed 2026-09-05, H: "Look both good" on v214 and the About line) — blobs, local only; in the export only with the "Include photos" checkbox, as `imgB64`/`imgFullB64` (v166).
Rules: **Pinyin only verified, with correct tones — never guess.** Meanings in English, or in the app's language from the AI (`ml`, v256). Dictionary/phrasebook prefills are `verified:false` until a human or the AI checked them; when unsure, flag instead of inventing.
New words come from photos (menus, signs, packaging).

Works.") · the traditional mark by hand and the Untagged group with its chip rows (v146–v157, seen in H's screenshots: the link, the switch vanishing on 工作, the two-line chip row that then scrolled). · the speaker button and the export with photos (v164–v166: "Sound works now", "OK works" after the receiving phone had updated) · the app re-installed as a proper web app from Chrome's "Install app" (v167, after the share entry had not appeared; ## Play Store launch (started 2026-09-03, H: closed test first, then sell)
The app goes to Google Play as a Trusted Web Activity — a thin Android wrapper (built with PWABuilder/Bubblewrap) that opens the live GitHub Pages URL full screen; the web app itself does not change. Facts established: H registers a **personal** Play Console account as a China resident (supported; Chinese ID for verification, $25 fee needs a Visa/Mastercard, whole flow over VPN); a personal account created now must pass a **closed test with ≥ 12 testers opted in for 14 continuous days** before production; Google Play does not distribute to users in mainland China at all; Chinese app stores (GetApps/AppGallery) would need a 软著 filing — out of scope.
- **Done:** `privacy.html` in the repo root (Play requires a privacy-policy URL; not in the SW shell, so no version bump; light/dark checked by screenshot). The manifest is TWA-ready (maskable icon, standalone, scope).
- **Decided rules:** the signing keystore never goes into the public repo. `assetlinks.json` must live at the **origin root** — `henglicam.github.io/.well-known/assetlinks.json`, i.e. a separate one-time repo `henglicam/henglicam.github.io` (project-path `.well-known` does not work for asset links); it gets the SHA-256 of the **Play App Signing** key, which exists only after the first upload — until then the TWA shows a browser bar, which is fine for testing.
- **Open decision (irreversible, before the Console listing is created):** a Play listing can go paid → free but **never free → paid**. Recommendation: create the app as **paid** from the start and add the 12 testers as license testers so they get it free; the alternative (free + in-app unlock) needs Play Billing inside the TWA (Digital Goods API) — real code. Known tension, accepted for v1: the web app at the public URL stays free (GitHub Pages, public repo — a hard constraint), so the paid Play app sells packaging and store presence, not exclusive access; true gating would be a re-architecture (hosting, licensing check).
- **Still to do:** H creates the Play Console account and confirms paid/free → TWA package via pwabuilder.com from H's phone (keeps the signing key on H's side, out of the repo) → first upload to a closed track → H pastes the Play App Signing SHA-256 → `assetlinks.json` content for the root repo → recruit 12 testers, 14 days → merchant account + price + store listing (screenshots, descriptions — Claude prepares texts) → production. A "Privacy policy" row in More (Play's User Data policy wants the link in-app too) is a UI change: described, waiting for go.



## Testing
No test files in the repo. A full audit (dead code, unused CSS, duplicated helpers, stale comments) was done at v81 and again at v151 (H: "a complete audit and polish" — found: an unused `.narrow` rule and two dead `.scriptref` rules, the reader's "Loading OCR" and "OCR failed" texts, five lowercase status badges; the identifier check against this file passed; layout checked by the twelve design screenshots); the mechanical scan for unreferenced functions, constants and CSS classes, and the check that every identifier named here exists in the code, are worth repeating after larger changes. **The third audit at v202** (H: "complete audit and polish") found: a dead variable in the second look (`frac`), a stale comment block on the selection bar removed in v91, four status texts not in sentence case ("failed: …" twice in More, "translated N cards", the study badge "testing from the list"), a stale CSS comment placing the picture mark under the meaning, the `.croprect` rule split in two, and the error-body snippet of the AI transports written three times (now `apiErrText`); no unused CSS class or id, every identifier named here exists (the names left over are the edge functions' and the SQL's), the twelve design screenshots in light and dark showed nothing to fix. **The fourth audit at v213** (H: "complete audit and polish — anything we can optimize?") found: four stale comments (the AI section's head still said "never photos" and "doubtful OCR", the owner's rows still listed the usage report and not All users, Feedback and the AI setup, a thumbnail comment said the whole photo where the code takes the crop, the `CROP` shape lacked the angle, the proposal and the zoom), the privacy page's date behind its feedback bullet, and nothing unreferenced (the audit script's two hits, `altCharsAt` and `vocabNames`, are used through a spread and a destructuring); the Cards list's thumbnails load lazily since. **The fifth audit at v233** (H: "Optimize and polish", after the design review's stretch v222–v232) found: the text box's CSS default still the 260 px square of v82–v222, overridden inline on every card since v223 (now the wide box's size), the whole-photo image rule without a note that it serves the `.full` view alone since v224, and the design line naming the version label as a `--mono` use; nothing unreferenced (the audit script's one hit is the `$` query helper), every identifier named here exists, the eighteen design screenshots in light and dark showed nothing to fix. **The sixth audit at v243** (H: "a complete audit and polish, also for consistency", after the Save now and Crop again stretch v237–v242) found: the abandon-a-reading steps written three times (the inbox's Cancel, the Edit form's `endRecrop`, the edit over a pending reading — now `abandonReading`), two comments still placing Save now "beside the bar" and the resume path without the copied photo and the Edit form's hand-off, the "Still at:" of a stuck step capitalised mid-sentence since v237 (lowercase again after "Reading the text …", capitalised only after the saved card's own sentence), a failed re-crop leaving its frame on a card with text, and the edit guard of v237 dropping a re-crop's pending reading on any save — even one that only touched pinyin — where only a changed text should; nothing unreferenced (the audit script's one hit is the `$` query helper), every identifier named here exists, the eighteen design screenshots in light and dark showed nothing to fix, the recrop, savenow, english, lock and edit suites pass. **The seventh audit at v260** (H: "Audit und Polish", after the language stretch v253–v259) found: a dead helper (`modelsText`, replaced by `workLines` in v252), a stale CSS rule (`#e-aibox`, the green box that went in v245), two stale names here (`PIC_SYSTEM` and `PIC_MARK` became `picSystem()` and `picMark()` in v254–v256), and **fourteen local variables named `t`** in functions that also call `t()` — arrow parameters and locals in `renderAiRow`, the Edit form's `pinyinFollow`, `cropSign`, `openDrawSheet`'s status, `wireSlines`' pinyin and meaning inputs, `signTranslate`, the Cards list's and the Tags field's tag maps — none of them wrong today (each shadowed `t` only inside its own arrow), all renamed so no later edit trips over them; the tags suite read the Add form's fields right after `S.custom` grew and raced the clearing — it waits for the ok line since. **The eighth audit at v270** (H: "Make a final check and polish", after the improvement list v266–v269) found: the shared card's blurred fill drawn through the canvas filter, which Safari does not have — on the iPhone an unblurred enlarged copy would have shown behind the crop (now the fill is skipped where `filter` is missing, and a WebKit without `roundRect` gets square corners); ## Working with H — the how-to rules (v128, agreed after the four-corner episode)
**What H sends.** One request per message: a sentence, and a screenshot when it is about a screen. When the reader is wrong, More → Diagnostics → Share with it — the phone is the only place the reader can be watched from. "Leave it as it was" means: revert to the previous behaviour, no discussion.

**What Claude does with a request.**
1. **Restate before building.** One sentence: what changes for H on the phone. Two readings that lead to different work → one question, not five. Otherwise no questions.
2. **Size gate.** Wording, layout, a rule in the reader, a bug: build at once. **Anything that changes how H handles the app** (a new gesture, a new field or control, a new screen, a changed flow) is described first in three lines — what H does, what H sees, what it costs — and waits for "go". A "go" is for that description; if the built thing feels different, H says so and it is reverted in the same session.
3. **Only the ask.** No "while I'm at it" changes. Consistency passes, cleanups and refactors only when H asks for them.
4. **Field first.** A reader change is judged by the phone, not by the harness. Every claim about the phone is either seen on the phone (screenshot, diagnostics) or marked "not yet field-checked".
5. **One request, one PR, one version.** Every PR: the three version markers bumped, the suites that touch the change run, CLAUDE.md updated in the same PR, merged by Claude, branch reset onto main. A revert is a PR like any other, with its own version.
6. **CLAUDE.md is the memory.** Decisions, rejected features and field lessons go in with the reason; "Zieh CLAUDE.md nach" means consolidate into the current state, not a version log. What H rejected is not brought back unasked.
7. **Report short, in English.** What changed, what was verified and where, what is open. Honesty over confidence: "I don't know" beats a guess; limits are named.
8. **Wording and design.** Sentence case, plain words, no jargon (no "OCR"), no "·" shorthand; iOS-style tokens; pinyin in the UI font; the rules in Design and Hard constraints are not up for interpretation.
9. **Tone (H, v255): relaxed.** "This is a fun app. No formal, boring translations within the app. Customers are passionate language learners. Same for the design and layout." — every language addresses the learner as a friend (du, tu, tú, 해요체), no formal register, no stiff notices; design and layout stay light the same way.
- Build-first. Physical reality beats spec.
- On every change: confirm the app still runs with no network dependencies and relative paths.
